### 评论 #3 (作者: XC96000, 时间: 6个月前)

正好最近也在做自己的AI探索工作流，我直接进行一个抛砖引玉：

首先声明一下我的工作流程是用代码对话llm实现的，而不是使用ai编程工具配合mcp，skills。

介绍一下我基于数据集的工作流程：

数据集分析——>字段精选——>主题确立——>操作符选择——>表达式构建

数据集分析主要针对一些数据字段很多的数据集，如GLB的fundamental17，存在895个字段，如果一股脑丢给AI可能会分析的不够精准，我的方式是先获取数据集的描述，将其构建到提示词，让AI分析数据集主题，限定AI回复关键词聚类：

```
def build_discovery_strategy_prompt(self, dataset_meta: Dict) -> str:"""根据数据集元数据，让 LLM 制定探测策略/方向"""returnf"""You are a Strategic Quantitative Researcher. I have a new dataset.DATASET CONTEXT:- Name: {dataset_meta.get('name')}- Description: {dataset_meta.get('description')}TASK:1. Briefly analyze the theme of this dataset.2. Define a Discovery Strategy as an intensive KEYWORD CLUSTER.- CRITICAL: This cluster will be converted into a vector for SEMANTIC SEARCH against thousands of field descriptions.- USE: Specific financial keywords, economic concepts, and technical terminology (e.g., "earnings revision", "operating leverage", "inventory turnover").- AVOID: Any framing language like "Focus on..." or "We will look at...". Give me only the raw ingredients.- **ORDER: Place the most distinctive and impactful keywords at the BEGINNING. Use a descending order of priority.**RESPONSE FORMAT:One line for the summary, followed by:STRATEGY: [Your keyword-dense strategy here. LIMIT to 100 words. Start with the highest-priority terms.]"""
```

这里得到AI回复后，使用向量数据库做语义匹配，先粗筛出匹配度最高的120个字段，方法大致如下：

```
defsearch_multi_path(self, query: str, top_k: int=50, core_count: int=5) -> List[Dict]:"""双路径聚合搜索：1. Core Path: 提取前 core_count 个核心词，避免长尾词稀释权重，进行高精准检索。2. Global Path: 使用全量词簇，捕捉复杂的语境关联。"""# 1. 提取核心路径词汇 (取前 5 个单词或短语)# 支持逗号、空格或分号分隔import reterms = re.split(r'[,; ]+', query.strip())core_query = " ".join(terms[:core_count]) if terms else querylogger.info(f"🔎 [Double Path] 核心路径: '{core_query}' | 全局路径: '{query[:30]}...'")# 2. 并行/顺序执行两次检索# 核心路劲结果我们给稍微少一点的名额，但它们往往极其精准core_results = self.search(core_query, top_k=top_k)global_results = self.search(query, top_k=top_k)# 3. 合并去重seen_ids = set()merged = []# 按照“核心词优先”的策略合并，如果 ID 相同，保留相似度更高的那个for f in (core_results + global_results):fid = f['id']if fid notin seen_ids:merged.append(f)seen_ids.add(fid)else:# 如果已经有了，更新为最高相似度for existing in merged:if existing['id'] == fid:existing['_similarity'] = max(existing['_similarity'], f['_similarity'])break# 4. 重新全局排序并取 Top Kmerged.sort(key=lambda x: x.get('_similarity', 0), reverse=True)return merged[:top_k]
```

再将这120字段全量发送给AI做一轮精筛；

提示词构成如下：

```
def build_field_refinement_prompt(self, fields: List[Dict], dataset_meta: Dict =None) -> str:"""从初步探测选中的大量字段中，进行“决赛”筛选，选出最核心的 50-80 个"""field_lines = [f"- {f['id']}: {f.get('description', '')}" for f in fields]fields_str = "\n".join(field_lines)returnf"""You are a Lead Quantitative Researcher. We have performed initial screening on a dataset and identified {len(fields)} potential fields.We need to narrow this down to the most impactful 50-80 fields for immediate strategy development.{f"DATASET CONTEXT: {dataset_meta.get('name')} - {dataset_meta.get('description')}" if dataset_meta else ""}POTENTIAL CANDIDATES:{fields_str}TASK:1. Review these candidates and select the 'Cream of the Crop'—the highest quality fields that provide unique and robust insights.2. Ensure you keep a balanced mix of "Alpha Drivers" and "Normalizers/Denominators".3. Return ONLY a comma-separated list of the chosen Field IDs."""
```

这里得到了一个相对精选的数据字段列表，我们可以构建提示词让AI分析这些数据字段，聚类并限定操作符分类，提示词构成如下：

```
defbuild_clustering_prompt(self, fields: List[Dict], dataset_meta: Dict =None) -> str:"""Phase 1.5: 询问如何聚类并提供需要的算子"""# 全量展示已由 Stage 1.2 精炼过的字段列表field_lines = [f"- {f['id']}: {f.get('description', '')}" for f in fields]fields_str = "\n".join(field_lines)available_cats = ["Arithmetic", "Group", "Time Series", "Cross Sectional", "Logical", "Transformational"]returnf"""You are a Quantitative Researcher analyzing a dataset.{f"DATASET CONTEXT: {dataset_meta.get('name')} - {dataset_meta.get('description')}" if dataset_meta else ""}AVAILABLE FIELDS:{fields_str}TASK:1. Identify 2-4 logical trading themes (e.g. Momentum, Valuation, Sentiment) that can be built from these fields.2. For each theme:- Provide a professional `logic` explanation of the financial theory.- Select ALL relevant Field IDs from the provided list that support this logic (`relevant_fields`).- Identify which operator categories are most important (`categories`).Choose from: {', '.join(available_cats)}RESPONSE FORMAT:Return ONLY the JSON list, no extra explanation text.{JSONFormats.THEMES}Output EXACTLY this JSON format (logic < 100 words):[  {    "theme": "Momentum Clustering",    "logic": "Exploiting the persistence in stock returns using volume-weighted price trends.",    "categories": ["Time Series", "Arithmetic"],    "relevant_fields": ["field_id1", "field_id2"]  }]"""
```

至此得到了一个包含多个主题分类的JSON格式的回复，每个块中包含了主题名称、主题逻辑、需要的操作符类别以及涉及的字段（这里每个主题使用的字段已经大大降低，基本不会超过20个）。

接下来再将这里得到的多个主题分开构建进下一个阶段的提示词（分开可以让AI专心处理每一个主题）；

操作符筛选提示词构成如下：

```
defbuild_operator_selection_prompt(self, all_available_ops: List[Dict], clustering_context: str, fields: List[Dict] =None, has_vector: bool=False, has_group: bool=False) -> str:"""Phase 1.5.5: 让 LLM 从大类中精选具体的算子"""# 按类分下组，方便 LLM 阅读cat_map = {}for op in all_available_ops:cat = op.get('category', 'Other')if cat notin cat_map: cat_map[cat] = []defn = op.get('definition', op['name'] + "(...)")desc = op.get('description', '')cat_map[cat].append(f"- {defn}: {desc}")op_list_str = ""for cat, ops in cat_map.items():op_list_str += f"\nCategory: {cat}\n"op_list_str += "\n".join(ops)op_list_str += "\n"special_instructions = []if has_vector:special_instructions.append("- **CRITICAL**: The dataset contains **VECTOR fields**. You MUST select at least 2-3 Vector operators.")if has_group:special_instructions.append("- **CRITICAL**: The dataset contains **GROUP fields**. You MUST select Group operators.")special_str = "\n".join(special_instructions) if special_instructions else ""# 使用统一的分层字段格式field_info_str = ""if fields:field_info_str = "### AVAILABLE DATA FIELDS FOR THIS THEME:\n"field_info_str += self._format_fields_layered(fields)field_info_str += "\n---"returnf"""You are a Trading Strategy Expert. You just analyzed a dataset and identified this specific theme:---{clustering_context}---{field_info_str}### AVAILABLE OPERATORS POOL:{op_list_str}{special_str}### FINAL TASK:Based on the theme logic and the data fields listed above, please pick the most relevant operators from the list above.Pick about 10-15 operators in total that best match your strategy.RESPONSE FORMAT:Provide a brief justification for your choice, then end with exactly this line:SELECTED_OPERATORS: op1, op2, op3..."""
```

至此，我们获取到了所有需要的信息，包括：细分的主题逻辑、所需的数据字段、所需的操作符。

用这些信息构建最终提示词生成表达式：

```
def build_themed_generation_prompt(self, fields: List[Dict], available_ops: List[Dict], region: str="USA", instrument_type: str="EQUITY", dataset_meta: Dict =None, clustering_strategy: str=None, fixed_neutralization: str=None) -> str:"""Phase 1.6: 分组结构化生成"""rules_sections = []guideline_sections = [] # Initialize guideline_sections heredefformat_op(op):returnf"{op.get('definition', op['name'] +'(...)')}: {op.get('description', '')}"# 按照优先级顺序渲染算子分类与指南priority_cats = ['Arithmetic', 'Time Series', 'Vector', 'Group', 'Cross Sectional', 'Transformational', 'Logical']for cat in priority_cats:ops = [format_op(op) for op in available_ops if op.get('category') == cat]if ops:rules_sections.append(f"- {cat}:")rules_sections.extend([f" - {o}" for o in ops])# 特殊类别指南探测与追加if cat =='Vector':guideline_sections.append(self._get_vector_guidance())elif cat =='Group':has_custom_groups = any(f.get('type', '').upper() == 'GROUP' for f in fields)guideline_sections.append(self._get_group_guidance(has_custom_groups=has_custom_groups))field_info = self._format_fields_layered(fields)rules_info = "\n".join(rules_sections)guideline_info = "\n".join(guideline_sections) if guideline_sections else ""strategy_part = f"### CLUSTERING STRATEGY & THEMES:\n{clustering_strategy}" if clustering_strategy else ""context_part = f"### DATASET CONTEXT: \n{dataset_meta.get('name')} - {dataset_meta.get('description')}" if dataset_meta else ""neut_parts = self._get_neutralization_parts(region, instrument_type, fixed_neutralization)neut_instruction = neut_parts["instruction"]neut_section = neut_parts["section"]prompt = f"""You are a Senior Quantitative Researcher. Analyze the following data fields from WorldQuant Brain.{context_part}{strategy_part}### DATA FIELDS FOR ANALYSIS:{field_info}### AVAILABLE OPERATORS POOL:{rules_info}### OPERATOR USAGE GUIDELINES:{guideline_info}### NEUTRALIZATION:{neut_section}### DECAY:- Integer n >= 0. Used to reduce turnover by combining current values with previous days.{SharedRules.STRICT_SYNTAX}- Complexity limit: Max 2 signal fields and 4 operators per template prior to neutralization.### FINAL TASK:1. Based on the clustering theme(s) described above, create **as many high-quality Alpha insights as possible (target 8-12 variants)** to fully explore the logic.2. For EACH insight, generate its production-quality Alpha template using the available fields and operators.- **NO SINGLE-FIELD EXPRESSIONS**: Every template MUST include operators or combine multiple fields. Bare fields are FORBIDDEN.- USE YOUR STRATEGY KNOWLEDGE to make the Alpha robust and logically sound.3. For each template, {neut_instruction}Output as a JSON list (Keep "description" extremely concise, < 15 words). [  {    "template": "expression...",     "category": "ThemeName",     "fields": ["field_id1", "field_id2"],     "description": "Reasoning insight",    "decay": 5,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  }]"""
```

这里得到的表达式准确率还不算高，后续还有纠错的部分，以及模拟得到表现不错的因子后的优化部分，比较长，这里不多赘述。

然后这里贴一下成果，刚好今天跑出来一个：

![图片](../images/img_28c0e25ccf.png)

然后是关于这个alpha的完整提示词（非探索阶段，这里的提示词是优化这个alpha）：

策划：

```
--- STEP 1 ANALYSIS ---You are a Senior Quantitative Analyst. Analyze the following Alpha.CURRENT ALPHA: `-abs(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d)))`PERFORMANCE: Sharpe: 1.92, Fitness: 1.6, Margin: 0.001389, Turnover: 0.452FAILED CHECKS: [{"name": "CONCENTRATED_WEIGHT", "result": "WARNING", "value": null}]DATASET CONTEXT:- Name: Short Selling Model- Description: The dataset combines features obtained from short contracts to estimate risk-neutral returns. The factors include loan rates, volume of loans, age of loans, their differences over time, and so on, and derived factors. The method of combination differs among different type of contracts and regions, and all factors are standardized to percentile. The combined score is a strong alpha mode.FIELDS CURRENTLY USED:- shrt7_shortlasso1d: Model score using current day's data- shrt7_shortlasso30d: Model score using last 30 days of dataTASK:1. Briefly analyze the economic logic of this Alpha and why it might be failing or performing as it is.2. Decide on a strategy: Should we focus on 'OPTIMIZE' (fixing flaws, reducing turnover, winsorizing) or 'VARIANT' (exploring new logical directions, adding new signals)?3. Justify your choice in one sentence.Your response MUST conclude with either "DECISION: OPTIMIZE" or "DECISION: VARIANT".--- STEP 1 RESPONSE ---## Analysis**Economic Logic:**This alpha captures *convergence divergence* between short-term (1d) and long-term (30d) short-selling model scores. The intuition is that when these two signals align closely (small absolute difference → less negative output → smaller positions), the short-selling signal is more stable and reliable. When they diverge sharply, the alpha takes larger short positions expecting mean reversion.**Why It May Be Failing:**The `CONCENTRATED_WEIGHT` warning suggests the `-abs()` transformation creates extreme values when the two signals diverge, leading to outsized, concentrated bets on those divergence events. Additionally, the high turnover (0.452) indicates frequent rebalancing that may erode the thin margin (0.001389). The economic premise—that divergence predicts reversion—may also be flawed for short-selling signals, where divergence could persist or indicate structural regime changes rather than mean reversion.**Strategy Decision:**The alpha demonstrates solid raw performance (Sharpe 1.92) with a reasonable fitness score, but the concentrated positions and turnover issues are addressable structural flaws rather than fundamental logical errors.DECISION: OPTIMIZE--- STEP 2 STRATEGY ---Based on your previous decision, we need to define the technical requirements:TASK:1. Do we need NEW data fields from the dataset to achieve this? Answer true or false.2. If true, provide a "search_query" (a short descriptive sentence) that we can use to find relevant new fields via semantic search. (e.g., "earnings growth and profitability revision metrics"). Leave empty string if false.3. Which operator categories are most needed for this modification? Choose from: Arithmetic, Cross Sectional, Group, Logical, Time Series, Transformational, Vector.RESPONSE FORMAT:You MUST output ONLY a valid JSON object. Do not wrap it in markdown block.{  "needs_new_fields": true/false,  "search_query": "Your descriptive search string here or empty string",  "required_categories": ["Category1", "Category2"],  "reasoning": "Short explanation."}
```

操作符选择：

```
### FINAL TOOLKIT SELECTIONBased on the analysis and the strategy we just defined, please finalize the selection of fields and operators for implementation.### 1. PRECISION FIELD POOLThe following fields are the ones currently used by the Alpha (since you decided new fields are not required):#### [VECTOR Fields]- shrt7_shortlasso1d: Model score using current day's data- shrt7_shortlasso30d: Model score using last 30 days of data### 2. CANDIDATE OPERATORS (From Selected Categories)[Category: Arithmetic]- add(x, y, filter = false), x + y: Adds two or more inputs element wise. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before summing.- multiply(x ,y, ... , filter=false), x * y: Multiplies two or more inputs element wise. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before multiplication- sign(x): Returns the sign of a number: +1 for positive, -1 for negative, and 0 for zero. If the input is NaN, returns NaN.Input: Value of 7 instruments at day t: (2, -3, 5, 6, 3, NaN, -10)Output: (1, -1, 1, 1, 1, NaN, -1)- subtract(x, y, filter=false), x - y: Subtracts inputs left to right: x ? y ? … Supports two or more inputs. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before subtraction.- pasteurize(x): Set to NaN if x is INF or if the underlying instrument is not in the Alpha universe. This operator may help reduce outliers.Input: Value of 7 instruments at day t: (2, 3, 5, INF, 3, 8, 10), where value 10 does not belong in Alpha universeOutput: (2, 3, 5, NaN, 3, 8, NaN)- log(x): Calculates the natural logarithm of the input value. Commonly used to transform data that has positive values.- purify(x): Clear infinities (+inf, -inf) by replacing with NaN- arc_tan(x): This operator does inverse tangent of input- max(x, y, ..): Maximum value of all inputs. At least 2 inputs are required- to_nan(x, value=0, reverse=false): Convert value to NaN or NaN to value if reverse=true- abs(x): Returns the absolute value of a number, removing any negative sign.- round_down(x, f=1): Round input to greatest multiple of f less than input;Input: Value of 3 instruments at day t: (2.5, 3.2, 5.4), f: 1Output: (2, 3, 5)- divide(x, y), x / y: x / y- min(x, y ..): Minimum value of all inputs. At least 2 inputs are required- nan_mask(x, y): replace input with NAN if input's corresponding mask value or the second input here, is negative- signed_power(x, y): x raised to the power of y such that final result preserves sign of x- inverse(x): 1 / x- round(x): Round input to closest integer.- sqrt(x): Returns the non negative square root of x. Equivalent to power(x, 0.5); for signed roots use signed_power(x, 0.5).- reverse(x):  - x- power(x, y): x ^ y- densify(x): Converts a grouping field of many buckets into lesser number of only available buckets so as to make working with grouping fields computationally efficient[Category: Time Series]- ts_corr(x, y, d): Returns correlation of x and y for the past d days- ts_zscore(x, d): Z-score is a numerical measurement that describes a value's relationship to the mean of a group of values. Z-score is measured in terms of standard deviations from the mean: (x - tsmean(x,d)) / tsstddev(x,d). This operator may help reduce outliers and drawdown.Input: Value of 1 instrument in past 5 days where first element is the latest: (100, 0, 50, 60, 25), d: 5Output: (100-47)/33.7 = 1.57 from SD: 33.7, mean = 47- ts_returns (x, d, mode = 1): Returns the relative change in the x value- ts_product(x, d): Returns product of x for the past d days- ts_std_dev(x, d): Returns standard deviation of x for the past d days- ts_triple_corr(x, y, z, d): Returns triple correlation of x, y, z for the past d days- ts_backfill(x, d): Returns the first valid value of the input x by looking through lookback days (d). This operator can be used to backfill missing data.- days_from_last_change(x): Amount of days since last change of xInput: Value of 1 instrument in past 7 days where first element is the latest: (2, 2, 2, 7, 5, 16, 1)Output: 3- last_diff_value(x, d): Returns last x value not equal to current x value from last d days- ts_scale(x, d, constant = 0): Returns (x - ts_min(x, d)) / (ts_max(x, d) - ts_min(x, d)) + constant. This operator is similar to scale down operator but acts in time series space- ts_vector_neut(x,y,d): Returns x- ts_vector_proj(x,y,d)- ts_step(1): Returns days' counter- ts_sum(x, d): Sum values of x for the past d days.- ts_av_diff(x, d): Returns x - tsmean(x, d), but deals with NaNs carefully. That is NaNs are ignored during mean computation- ts_rank_gmean_amean_diff(input1, input2, input3,...,d): Returns Geometric Mean of ts_rank(input,d) of all input - Arithmetic Mean of ts_rank(input,d) of all input. This is similar to rank_gmean_amean_diff operator but in time-series space- ts_kurtosis(x, d): Returns kurtosis of x for the last d days- ts_mean(x, d): Returns average value of x for the past d days.- ts_arg_max(x, d): Returns the relative index of the max value in the time series for the past d days. If the current day has the max value for the past d days, it returns 0. If previous day has the max value for the past d days, it returns 1- ts_max(x, d): Returns max value of x for the past d days- ts_rank(x, d, constant = 0): Rank the values of x for each instrument over the past d days, then return the rank of the current value + constant. If not specified, by default, constant = 0.Input: Value of 1 instrument in past 3 days where first element is the latest: (100, 0, 200), d: 3Output: 0.5- ts_ir(x, d): Return information ratio ts_mean(x, d) / ts_std_dev(x, d)- ts_delay(x, d): Returns x value d days agoInput: Value of 1 instrument in past 7 days where first element is the latest: (2, 3, 5, 6, 3, 8, 10), d: 6Output: Value 6 days ago = 10- ts_quantile(x,d, driver="gaussian" ): It calculates ts_rank and apply to its value an inverse cumulative density function from driver distribution. Possible values of driver (optional ) are "gaussian", "uniform", "cauchy" distribution where "gaussian" is the default.Input: Value of 1 instrument in past 7 days where first element is the latest: (8, 10, 4, 6, 5, 3, 2), d: 7, driver: ’gaussian’Output: quantile = 0.82 from SD = 2.82, mean = 5.43, zscore = 0.911- ts_min(x, d): Returns min value of x for the past d days- ts_count_nans(x ,d): Returns the number of NaN values in x for the past d daysInput: Value of 1 instrument in past 4 days where first element is the latest: (100, NaN, NaN, 200), d: 4Output: Number of NaN in 4 days = 2- ts_covariance(y, x, d): Returns covariance of y and x for the past d days- ts_co_skewness(y, x, d): Returns coskewness of y and x for the past d days- ts_decay_linear(x, d, dense = false): Returns the linear decay on x for the past d days. Dense parameter=false means operator works in sparse mode and we treat NaN as 0. In dense mode we do not.- jump_decay(x, d, sensitivity=0.5, force=0.1): If there is a huge jump in current data compare to previous one- ts_arg_min(x, d): Returns the relative index of the min value in the time series for the past d days; If the current day has the min value for the past d days, it returns 0; If previous day has the min value for the past d days, it returns 1.- ts_regression(y, x, d, lag = 0, rettype = 0): Returns various parameters related to regression function- ts_max_diff(x, d): Returns x - ts_max(x, d)- kth_element(x, d, k): Returns K-th valid value of input by looking through lookback days. This operator can be used to backfill missing data if k=1- hump(x, hump = 0.01): Limits amount and magnitude of changes in input (thus reducing turnover)- ts_delta(x, d): Returns x - ts_delay(x, d)Input: Value of 1 instrument in past 7 days where first element is the latest: (2, 3, 5, 6, 3, 8, 10), d: 6Output: Value today – value 6 days ago = 2 - 10 = -8- ts_vector_proj(x,y,d): Returns vector projection of x onto y in time-series space.- ts_target_tvr_decay(x, lambda_min=0, lambda_max=1, target_tvr=0.1): Tune "ts_decay" to have a turnover equal to a certain target, with optimization weight range between lambda_min, lambda_max- ts_target_tvr_delta_limit(x, y, lambda_min=0, lambda_max=1, target_tvr=0.1): Tune "ts_delta_limit" to have a turnover equal to a certain target with optimization weight range between lambda_min, lambda_max. Also, please be aware of the scaling for x and y. Besides setting y as adv20 or volume related data, you can also set y as a constant.- ts_target_tvr_hump(x, lambda_min=0, lambda_max=1, target_tvr=0.1): Tune "hump" to have a turnover equal to a certain target with optimization weight range between lambda_min, lambda_max.[Category: Cross Sectional]- winsorize(x, std=4): Winsorizes x to make sure that all values in x are between the lower and upper limits, which are specified as multiple of std.Input: Value of 7 instruments at day t: (2, 4, 5, 6, 3, 8, 10), std: 1Output: (2.81, 4, 5, 6, 3, 8, 8.03) from SD. = 2.61, mean = 5.42- rank(x, rate=2): Ranks the input among all the instruments and returns an equally distributed number between 0.0 and 1.0. For precise sort, use the rate as 0- vector_neut(x, y): For given vectors x and y, it finds a new vector x* (output) such that x* is orthogonal to y- zscore(x): Z-score is a numerical measurement that describes a value's relationship to the mean of a group of values. Z-score is measured in terms of standard deviations from the mean- scale_down(x,constant=0): Scales all values in each day proportionately between 0 and 1 such that minimum value maps to 0 and maximum value maps to 1. Constant is the offset by which final result is subtracted- scale(x, scale=1, longscale=1, shortscale=1): Scales input to booksize. We can also scale the long positions and short positions to separate scales by mentioning additional parameters to the operator- normalize(x, useStd = false, limit = 0.0): Calculates the mean value of all valid alpha values for a certain date, then subtracts that mean from each element- quantile(x, driver = gaussian, sigma = 1.0): Rank the raw vector, shift the ranked Alpha vector, apply distribution (gaussian, cauchy, uniform). If driver is uniform, it simply subtract each Alpha value with the mean of all Alpha values in the Alpha vector[Category: Transformational]- bucket(rank(x), range="0, 1, 0.1" or buckets = "2,5,6,7,10"): Convert float values into indexes for user-specified buckets. Bucket is useful for creating group values, which can be passed to GROUP as input- tail(x, lower = 0, upper = 0, newval = 0): If (x > lower AND x < upper) return newval, else return x. Lower, upper, newval should be constants- filter(x, h = "1, 2, 3, 4", t="0.5"): Used to filter the value and allows to create filters like linear or exponential decay- clamp(x, lower = 0, upper = 0, inverse = False, mask = ): Limits input value between lower and upper bound in inverse = false mode (which is default). Alternatively, when inverse = true, values between bounds are replaced with mask, while values outside bounds are left as is- keep(x, f, period = 5): This operator outputs value x when f changes and continues to do that for "period" days after f stopped changing. After "period" days since last change of f, NaN is output- trade_when(x, y, z): Used in order to change Alpha values only under a specified condition and to hold Alpha values in other cases. It also allows to close Alpha positions (assign NaN values) under a specified condition---### YOUR TASK1. Confirm the original FIELDS you are keeping to be part of the final formula.2. Pick 5-8 OPERATORS that best facilitate the planned logical improvements.### RESPONSE FORMATSELECTED_FIELDS: field_id1, field_id2...SELECTED_OPERATORS: op_name1, op_name2...

You are an Expert Quantitative Tuner. Your goal is to MICRO-OPTIMIZE an already strong Alpha expression.### TARGET FOR OPTIMIZATION:- ORIGINAL ALPHA: `-abs(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d)))`- CURRENT PERFORMANCE: Sharpe: 1.92, Fitness: 1.6### SELECTED FIELDS FOR USE:#### [VECTOR Fields]- shrt7_shortlasso1d: Model score using current day's data- shrt7_shortlasso30d: Model score using last 30 days of data### SELECTED OPERATORS (Strict Constraint):- add(x, y, filter = false), x + y: Adds two or more inputs element wise. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before summing.- multiply(x ,y, ... , filter=false), x * y: Multiplies two or more inputs element wise. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before multiplication- sign(x): Returns the sign of a number: +1 for positive, -1 for negative, and 0 for zero. If the input is NaN, returns NaN.Input: Value of 7 instruments at day t: (2, -3, 5, 6, 3, NaN, -10)Output: (1, -1, 1, 1, 1, NaN, -1)- subtract(x, y, filter=false), x - y: Subtracts inputs left to right: x ? y ? … Supports two or more inputs. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before subtraction.- pasteurize(x): Set to NaN if x is INF or if the underlying instrument is not in the Alpha universe. This operator may help reduce outliers.Input: Value of 7 instruments at day t: (2, 3, 5, INF, 3, 8, 10), where value 10 does not belong in Alpha universeOutput: (2, 3, 5, NaN, 3, 8, NaN)- log(x): Calculates the natural logarithm of the input value. Commonly used to transform data that has positive values.- purify(x): Clear infinities (+inf, -inf) by replacing with NaN- arc_tan(x): This operator does inverse tangent of input- max(x, y, ..): Maximum value of all inputs. At least 2 inputs are required- to_nan(x, value=0, reverse=false): Convert value to NaN or NaN to value if reverse=true- abs(x): Returns the absolute value of a number, removing any negative sign.- round_down(x, f=1): Round input to greatest multiple of f less than input;Input: Value of 3 instruments at day t: (2.5, 3.2, 5.4), f: 1Output: (2, 3, 5)### OPERATOR USAGE GUIDELINES:VECTOR OPERATOR SPECIAL RULES:- **STRICT INPUT TYPE**: Vector operators (vec_*) can ONLY take fields from the **[VECTOR Fields]** section as input. Passing [MATRIX Fields] to these operators is FORBIDDEN and causes type mismatch.- **Mandatory Wrapping**: Vector fields MUST be wrapped by a Vector operator FIRST before any other operators process them.- **Correct Nesting**: Vector operators MUST wrap the vector field directly.    - CORRECT: `abs(vec_max(vector_field))`    - WRONG: `vec_max(abs(vector_field))`### NEUTRALIZATION:NEUTRALIZATION STRATEGY:- FIXED STRATEGY: FAST (Do not recommend other strategies)### DECAY:- Integer n >= 0. Used to reduce turnover by combining current values with previous days.### STRICT RULES:- **NO SINGLE-FIELD ALPHAS**: Every Alpha expression MUST contain at least one operator or involve multiple fields. Bare field names (e.g., `field_id`) are FORBIDDEN.- Syntax: Strictly follow parameter order (e.g., `winsorize(x, std=4)` is CORRECT, while `winsorize(std=4, x)` is WRONG). Always put the data field `x` as the first argument.- LOOKBACK/DAYS: All window parameters (e.g., `ts_mean(x, 20)`) MUST be positive integers. Using 0 is FORBIDDEN as it causes simulation errors.- NO SCIENTIFIC NOTATION: Do NOT use scientific notation (e.g., `1e-6`). Use full decimal expansion like `0.000001` or integers.- Balanced parentheses: Ensure every open parenthesis has a matching closing one.### FINAL TASK:1. Generate **as many high-quality PARAMETER TUNED variants as possible (up to 20)** of the original expression.2. DO NOT change the core structure radically. Focus on:   - Sweeping parameter windows (e.g., changing lookback days from 20 to 10 or 60).   - Addressing explicit issues (like adding winsorization to fix extreme values).   - Tuning decay to reduce turnover.3. You MUST rely on the SELECTED FIELDS and SELECTED OPERATORS provided above.4. **NO SINGLE-FIELD EXPRESSIONS**: Bare fields are FORBIDDEN.5. For each variant, include a recommended the best `decay` (0-20). The `neutralization` strategy is FIXED to: **FAST**.Output EXACTLY this JSON format (description < 15 words):[  {    "template": "...",     "fields": ["field_id1", "field_id2"],     "description": "...",    "decay": 10,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  }]

abs(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d))), filter=true)",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Use explicit multiply with filter",    "decay": 10,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "-max(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d))",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Use max instead of subtract for max blend",    "decay": 15,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "-abs(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d)))",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Use 5d as short instead of 1d",    "decay": 12,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "-abs(round_down(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d)), f=0.01))",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Round down to reduce noise precision",    "decay": 10,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "-abs(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d))) + subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d))",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Combine abs and signed signal",    "decay": 8,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "-abs(to_nan(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d)), value=0))",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Convert zeros to NaN for filtering",    "decay": 10,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "-abs(pasteurize(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d))))",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Pasteurize with 15d medium window",    "decay": 10,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "sign(abs(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d))))",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Double sign for volatility signal",    "decay": 6,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "-abs(add(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d), filter=true))",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Add instead of subtract for convergence",    "decay": 10,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  },  {    "template": "-abs(subtract(vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso1d), vec_avg(shrt7_shortlasso30d))) * -1",    "fields": ["shrt7_shortlasso1d", "shrt7_shortlasso30d"],    "description": "Explicit multiply by -1 for polarity",    "decay": 10,    "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"  }]```### AVAILABLE FIELDS:#### [VECTOR Fields]- shrt7_shortlasso1d: Model score using current day's data- shrt7_shortlasso30d: Model score using last 30 days of data### OPERATOR DEFINITIONS:- add(x, y, filter = false), x + y: Adds two or more inputs element wise. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before summing.- multiply(x ,y, ... , filter=false), x * y: Multiplies two or more inputs element wise. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before multiplication- sign(x): Returns the sign of a number: +1 for positive, -1 for negative, and 0 for zero. If the input is NaN, returns NaN.Input: Value of 7 instruments at day t: (2, -3, 5, 6, 3, NaN, -10)Output: (1, -1, 1, 1, 1, NaN, -1)- subtract(x, y, filter=false), x - y: Subtracts inputs left to right: x ? y ? … Supports two or more inputs. Set filter=true to treat NaNs as 0 before subtraction.- pasteurize(x): Set to NaN if x is INF or if the underlying instrument is not in the Alpha universe. This operator may help reduce outliers.Input: Value of 7 instruments at day t: (2, 3, 5, INF, 3, 8, 10), where value 10 does not belong in Alpha universeOutput: (2, 3, 5, NaN, 3, 8, NaN)- purify(x): Clear infinities (+inf, -inf) by replacing with NaN- arc_tan(x): This operator does inverse tangent of input- max(x, y, ..): Maximum value of all inputs. At least 2 inputs are required- to_nan(x, value=0, reverse=false): Convert value to NaN or NaN to value if reverse=true- abs(x): Returns the absolute value of a number, removing any negative sign.- round_down(x, f=1): Round input to greatest multiple of f less than input;Input: Value of 3 instruments at day t: (2.5, 3.2, 5.4), f: 1Output: (2, 3, 5)### FINAL REVIEW CHECKLIST & ACTION:Please review the content above and correct any syntax or logic errors based on the provided fields and operator definitions.0. **Field Authorization**: Every field ID used in the formulas MUST exist in the [AVAILABLE FIELDS] list. If a template uses a hallucinated field, DELETE that object.1. **Strict Parameter Syntax**: Your call MUST match the OPERATOR DEFINITION exactly (Named vs Positional).   - **Named Parameters**: (e.g., `rank(x, rate=2)`). Shorthand like `rank(x, 2)` is WRONG.   - **Positional Parameters**: (e.g., `ts_mean(x, 20)`). Using `days=20` is WRONG.2. **Number of Arguments**: Ensure the total number of arguments matches the definition.3. **Window Constants**: All 'lookback' or 'days' parameters MUST be positive integers. Using 0 is FORBIDDEN.4. **No Invalid Math**: Ensure NO use of %, ==, !=, &&, ||, +=.5. **Parentheses**: Are they strictly balanced?6. **Vector Audit**: Ensure all VECTOR fields (if any) are wrapped in a Vector operator (vec_avg, vec_sum, vec_stddev). **STRICT**: These operators MUST ONLY accept fields from the [VECTOR Fields] section; never pass Matrix fields into them.If you find ANY issues (especially extra parameters in rank/zscore/etc.), fix them and output the CORRECTED JSON list.If you find NO issues, simply output the original JSON list.Output ONLY the final JSON list.
```

### 评论 #4 (作者: ZH87224, 时间: 6个月前)

分享一个AIAC2.0比赛中打磨出来的Prompt，用来压缩数据集，哪怕数据集有上千个字段，也可以一股脑喂给大模型了，只需要喂给大模型之前用这个 Prompt 压缩一下：

```
你是一位专业的投资经理和数据架构师，你的任务是以某数据集为核心构造搭建投资因子库。你需要分析数据集中的字段命名规律，将其归纳为通用的正则表达式模式。例如：pv87 数据集字段模式示例：```pv87_2_[indicator]_[freq]_matrix_[scope]_[metric]_[stat]```其中：- indicator: affops, bps, capex, cfps, csh, dps 等财务指标- freq: af (Annual), qf (Quarterly) 等频率标识- scope: all, p1, p1_b 等数据范围- metric: null (原始值), chngratio (变化率) 等数据类型- stat: high, low, mean, median, dts, number, std 等统计量请基于字段的**经济学含义**和**命名构成模式**，分析归纳其中的规律。**重要规则**:1. 每组正则表达式必须**互斥**(一个字段只能匹配一个regex)2. 如果新规律能与已有规律合并，请合并后输出（优化通用性）3. 特殊字段(Special)是那些不符合任何通用模式的字段4. 正则表达式必须足够精确，避免过度匹配5. 不可以使用通配符代表有经济含义指标，例如不可以使用[a-z0-9]+来表示财务指标、频率等内容，必须使用或"|"来枚举所有经济对象这段Prompt的输入仍然是原始数据字段信息，所以需要分片输入，通过工程代码来实现：正则汇总 -> 正则匹配 -> 正则筛选，最终剩下的字段（没有被压缩规则扫描到）继续迭代：
```

```
{dataset_report}
## 当前已归纳的模式（已处理字段）

```python
regexes = {regexes_prompt}

specials = {specials_prompt}

```

## 本轮待处理的字段（已过滤掉上述已匹配字段）

本轮共有 {len(fields_left)} 个字段需要分析：

{fields_prompt}

## 任务要求

1. 分析上述字段的命名规律，输出新的正则表达式
2. 如果有无法归纳的特殊字段，放入 specials 列表
3. **关键** ：如果新发现的规律与"当前已归纳的模式"可以合并(例如只是扩展了可选值), 请直接在已有regex基础上修改并合并输出
4. 确保正则之间互斥

## 输出格式

请严格按照以格式输出JSON字符串:

```
{{
    "regexes": [
    {{
        "regex": "^pattern_here$",
        "definition": "该模式的业务含义解释",
        "enums": "该模式内的经济指标含义, 例如 dps, cfps 等财务指标, p1, y1 等期限指标等, 需要根据字段明细进行枚举, 不可使用太通用的通配符例如 _[a-z0-9]+",
        "examples": ["example1", "example2"]  # 从输入字段中选取的典型示例
    }},
    # ... 其他模式
    ],
    "specials": ["field1", "field2"],  # 无法归纳的特殊字段，如果没有则留空列表,
    "reasoning": "简要说明本轮分析的思路和发现的关键模式"
}}

```

**特别注意** :

1. regexes列表应该包含 **所有** 有效的模式（包括与已有模式合并后的新版本）。
2. regexes列表应该简化, 合并其中能够合并的项
3. 待处理的字段都是 regexes 无法匹配的字段, 这一点非常明确, 已通过代码验证, 如果你认为待处理字段符合某个已有模式, 请仔细分析正则表达式是否正确
4. 已知部分字段会有数字后缀, 例如 _[0-9]+, 该后缀一般没有实际的经济含义
5. 除了无实际经济含义的枚举项, 其余组成部分不可以使用太通用的通配符, 例如_[a-z0-9]+, 你必须要在 enums 进行枚举, 例如 cps (cash per share), ent (earnings net taxes), fcff (free cash flow)

```

### 评论 #5 (作者: HA11071, 时间: 6个月前)

分享一个自用skill

```
本技能旨在指导大模型生成符合 WorldQuant 平台规范的 Alpha 表达式。  
Alpha 是一种用于预测股票未来收益的数学公式，基于基础因子和运算符构建，需满足严格的语法、逻辑和实用性约束。

## 1. Alpha 表达式基本格式

WorldQuant 的 Alpha 表达式使用**逆波兰表达式（RPN）**或**类 C 语言语法**（取决于平台版本）。  
常见版本采用 **Python 风格函数调用**，但核心是**表达式树**结构，其中：

- 基础因子：如 `close`、`open`、`volume`、`vwap` 等，可能带字段后缀（如 `close_0` 表示今日收盘价，`close_5` 表示 5 日前收盘价）。
- 运算符：`+`、`-`、`*`、`/`、`^`（幂）、`max`、`min`、`rank`、`scale`、`delay`、`correlation`、`covariance`、`ts_sum`、`ts_mean`、`ts_stddev`、`ts_min`、`ts_max`、`ts_argmax`、`ts_argmin`、`ts_rank` 等。
- 条件运算符：`if`、`>、<、>=、<=、==、!=`。
- 聚合函数：`group_sum`、`group_mean`、`group_std` 等（按行业/市值分组）。

```

```
**示例**
```

```
rank(ts_sum(volume, 5)) * -1 * delay(close, 1)
```

```

### 评论 #7 (作者: YL27037, 时间: 6个月前)

## 技能：brain-alpha-data-analysis
用于辅助分析 .ods（LibreOffice表格）格式的Alpha回测结果。

### 工作流程
1. **数据转换**：通过 `ods_to_json.py` 将表格转换为LLM优化的JSON格式
2. **数据分析**：数据量大时使用 `analyze_alpha_data.py` 进行统计分析；数据量小时可直接让LLM读取JSON

### 💡 设计思路
虽然AI可以处理多种表格格式，但当数据量较大时，
直接让LLM读取原始表格往往只能看到头部数据。
本skill的核心价值是：
1. 将表格预处理为LLM优化的JSON格式
2. 通过Python脚本进行完整的统计分析
3. 生成结构化的分析报告供AI参考
这样既保证了数据处理的完整性，也提升了AI的分析效率。

# SKILL.md
```
---
name: brain-alpha-data-analysis
description: Alpha 数据分析工具，用于分析和解析从 ODS 格式导出的 Alpha 回测结果
license: MIT
compatibility: opencode
metadata:
  domain: quantitative-finance
  platform: worldquant-brain
  category: analysis
  language: python
  dependencies:
    - pandas
    - odfpy
---

# Alpha 数据分析

当您需要分析 Alpha 回测结果、筛选高潜力因子或生成性能报告时，使用此 skill。

## 功能

1. **ODS 转 JSON**: 将 ODS 格式的 Alpha 数据转换为 JSON 格式
2. **数据分析**: 对 Alpha 数据进行统计分析、性能评估和排名
3. **智能体支持**: 生成适合智能体分析的数据格式

## 依赖安装

```bash
pip install pandas odfpy
```

## 使用方法

### 1. 转换 ODS 文件

```bash
cd .iflow/skills/brain-alpha-data-analysis
python scripts/ods_to_json.py <path_to_ods_file>
```

**示例**:
```bash
python scripts/ods_to_json.py ../../../tmp/回测优秀待优化.ods
```

这将生成 `回测优秀待优化.json` 文件。

### 2. 分析数据

```bash
python scripts/analyze_alpha_data.py <path_to_json_file>
```

**示例**:
```bash
python scripts/analyze_alpha_data.py ../../../tmp/回测优秀待优化.json
```

这将生成两个文件：
- `回测优秀待优化_analysis_report.txt` - 详细分析报告
- `回测优秀待优化_agent_data.json` - 智能体友好的数据格式

## 分析报告内容

生成的报告包含以下信息：

1. **字段统计信息**
   - 数量、平均值、中位数、最小值、最大值、标准差

2. **性能排名**
   - Sharpe Top 10
   - Fitness Top 10
   - Returns Top 10

3. **高潜力 Alpha**
   - Sharpe > 1.0 的所有 Alpha
   - 包含完整性能指标

## 智能体数据格式

导出的智能体数据包含：

```json
{
  "summary": {
    "total_alphas": 378,
    "field_statistics": {...}
  },
  "rankings": {
    "top_by_sharpe": [...],
    "top_by_fitness": [...]
  },
  "high_potential": [...],
  "all_alphas": [...]
}
```

## 数据字段说明

常见的 Alpha 数据字段：

| 字段 | 说明 | 类型 |
|------|------|------|
| id | Alpha ID | 数值 |
| expression | Alpha 表达式 | 文本 |
| sharpe | Sharpe 比率 | 数值 |
| fitness | Fitness 指标 | 数值 |
| returns | 收益率 | 数值 |
| turnover | 换手率 | 数值 |
| drawdown | 回撤 | 数值 |
| margin | 利润率 | 数值 |
| long_count | 做多数量 | 数值 |
| short_count | 做空数量 | 数值 |
| region | 市场区域 | 文本 |
| universe | 股票宇宙 | 文本 |
| delay | 数据延迟 | 数值 |

## 高级用法

### 筛选高 Sharpe Alpha

```python
from scripts.analyze_alpha_data import AlphaAnalyzer

analyzer = AlphaAnalyzer('回测优秀待优化.json')
analyzer.load_data()

# 筛选 Sharpe > 1.5 的 Alpha
high_sharpe = analyzer.filter_alphas({"sharpe": {"min": 1.5}})
print(f"找到 {len(high_sharpe)} 个高 Sharpe Alpha")
```

### 获取排名

```python
# 获取 Sharpe 前 20
top_20 = analyzer.get_top_alphas('sharpe', 20)

# 获取 Fitness 后 10
bottom_10 = analyzer.get_bottom_alphas('fitness', 10)
```

### 获取统计信息

```python
# Sharpe 统计
sharpe_stats = analyzer.get_statistics('sharpe')
print(f"平均 Sharpe: {sharpe_stats['mean']}")
print(f"最高 Sharpe: {sharpe_stats['max']}")
```

## 注意事项

1. 确保 ODS 文件格式正确，包含有效的表格数据
2. 数值字段应为数字类型，字符串可能导致分析错误
3. JSON 文件使用 UTF-8 编码
4. 大数据集可能需要较长的处理时间

## 性能指标说明

- **Sharpe**: 风险调整后的收益比率，越高越好
- **Fitness**: 综合性能指标，考虑多个因素
- **Returns**: 总收益率
- **Turnover**: 换手率，影响交易成本
- **Drawdown**: 最大回撤，越小越好
- **Margin**: 利润率

## 完整工作流示例

```bash
# 1. 转换 ODS 为 JSON
python scripts/ods_to_json.py ../../../tmp/回测优秀待优化.ods

# 2. 分析数据
python scripts/analyze_alpha_data.py ../../../tmp/回测优秀待优化.json

# 3. 查看结果
# - 回测优秀待优化.json: 原始数据
# - 回测优秀待优化_analysis_report.txt: 分析报告
# - 回测优秀待优化_agent_data.json: 智能体数据
```

## 故障排除

### 问题: "pandas 未安装"
**解决**: 运行 `pip install pandas odfpy`

### 问题: "无法读取 ODS 文件"
**解决**: 检查文件路径是否正确，确保文件未被损坏

### 问题: "JSON 解析失败"
**解决**: 确保 JSON 文件格式正确，可以尝试重新生成
```

# scripts/ods_to_json.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
ODS to JSON 转换脚本
将 ODS 文件中的 Alpha 信息转换为 JSON 格式，便于后续分析和处理
"""

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

class ODSToJSONConverter:
    """ODS 到 JSON 转换器"""

def __init__(self, ods_file_path: str):
        """
        初始化转换器

Args:
            ods_file_path: ODS 文件路径
        """
        self.ods_file_path = Path(ods_file_path)
        self.data: Dict[str, Any] = {}

def serialize_value(self, value: Any) -> Any:
        """
        将值序列化为JSON兼容格式

Args:
            value: 要序列化的值

Returns:
            JSON兼容的值
        """
        if value is None:
            return None

# 处理 pandas Timestamp
        if hasattr(value, 'strftime'):
            return value.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')

# 处理 numpy 类型
        try:
            import numpy as np
            if isinstance(value, (np.integer, np.int64, np.int32)):
                return int(value)
            elif isinstance(value, (np.floating, np.float64, np.float32)):
                return float(value)
            elif isinstance(value, np.bool_):
                return bool(value)
        except ImportError:
            pass

# 处理 datetime
        try:
            from datetime import datetime, date
            if isinstance(value, (datetime, date)):
                return value.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')
        except ImportError:
            pass

# 处理其他类型
        try:
            return float(value)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            try:
                return int(value)
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                return str(value)

def read_ods_with_pandas(self) -> Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """
        使用 pandas 读取 ODS 文件

Returns:
            包含表格数据的字典列表，每个字典代表一行数据
        """
        try:
            import pandas as pd
            df = pd.read_excel(self.ods_file_path, engine='odf')

# 将 DataFrame 转换为字典列表
            data_list = df.to_dict('records')

# 清理数据：移除 NaN 值，并序列化所有值
            cleaned_data = []
            for row in data_list:
                cleaned_row = {}
                for key, value in row.items():
                    if pd.notna(value):
                        cleaned_row[key] = self.serialize_value(value)
                    else:
                        cleaned_row[key] = None
                cleaned_data.append(cleaned_row)

return cleaned_data

except ImportError:
            print("错误: pandas 未安装。请运行: pip install pandas odfpy")
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"读取 ODS 文件时出错: {str(e)}")
            return None

def read_ods_with_pyodf(self) -> Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """
        使用 pyodf 读取 ODS 文件

Returns:
            包含表格数据的字典列表
        """
        try:
            from odf.opendocument import load
            from odf.table import Table, TableRow, TableCell

doc = load(self.ods_file_path)
            tables = doc.getElementsByType(Table)

if not tables:
                print("警告: ODS 文件中没有找到表格")
                return None

# 读取第一个表格
            table = tables[0]
            rows = table.getElementsByType(TableRow)

data_list = []
            headers = []

# 第一行作为标题
            if rows:
                header_row = rows[0]
                cells = header_row.getElementsByType(TableCell)
                headers = [str(cell) for cell in cells]

# 读取数据行
                for row in rows[1:]:
                    cells = row.getElementsByType(TableCell)
                    row_data = {}

for i, cell in enumerate(cells):
                        if i < len(headers):
                            value = str(cell).strip()
                            # 转换数值类型
                            if value.replace('.', '', 1).replace('-', '', 1).isdigit():
                                if '.' in value:
                                    row_data[headers[i]] = float(value)
                                else:
                                    row_data[headers[i]] = int(value)
                            else:
                                row_data[headers[i]] = value if value else None

if row_data:  # 只添加非空行
                        data_list.append(row_data)

return data_list

except ImportError:
            print("错误: pyodf 未安装。请运行: pip install odfpy")
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"使用 pyodf 读取 ODS 文件时出错: {str(e)}")
            return None

def convert(self, output_json_path: Optional[str] = None) -> bool:
        """
        执行转换

Args:
            output_json_path: 输出 JSON 文件路径，如果为 None 则使用默认路径

Returns:
            转换是否成功
        """
        print(f"正在读取 ODS 文件: {self.ods_file_path}")

# 尝试使用 pandas 读取
        data_list = self.read_ods_with_pandas()

# 如果 pandas 失败，尝试使用 pyodf
        if data_list is None:
            data_list = self.read_ods_with_pyodf()

if data_list is None:
            print("错误: 无法读取 ODS 文件")
            return False

print(f"成功读取 {len(data_list)} 行数据")

# 构建输出数据结构
        self.data = {
            "source_file": str(self.ods_file_path),
            "total_records": len(data_list),
            "alphas": data_list
        }

# 设置输出路径
        if output_json_path is None:
            output_json_path = self.ods_file_path.parent / f"{self.ods_file_path.stem}.json"
        else:
            output_json_path = Path(output_json_path)

# 写入 JSON 文件
        try:
            with open(output_json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(self.data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

print(f"成功转换，输出文件: {output_json_path}")
            print(f"总记录数: {len(data_list)}")

# 显示前几条记录作为预览
            if data_list:
                print("\n数据预览（前3条）:")
                for i, record in enumerate(data_list[:3], 1):
                    print(f"\n记录 {i}:")
                    for key, value in record.items():
                        print(f"  {key}: {value}")

return True

except Exception as e:
            print(f"写入 JSON 文件时出错: {str(e)}")
            return False

def main():
    """主函数"""
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("用法: python ods_to_json.py <ods_file_path> [output_json_path]")
        print("示例: python ods_to_json.py 回测优秀待优化.ods")
        sys.exit(1)

ods_file = sys.argv[1]
    output_json = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else None

converter = ODSToJSONConverter(ods_file)
    success = converter.convert(output_json)

sys.exit(0 if success else 1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

# script/analyze_alpha_data.py
```
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Alpha 数据分析脚本
用于分析和解析从 ODS 转换的 Alpha 数据，便于智能体进行优化决策
"""

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from statistics import mean, median, stdev
from collections import Counter

class AlphaAnalyzer:
    """Alpha 数据分析器"""

# 关键性能指标阈值
    PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS = {
        "sharpe": {"excellent": 1.5, "good": 1.0, "acceptable": 0.5},
        "fitness": {"excellent": 0.7, "good": 0.5, "acceptable": 0.3},
        "returns": {"excellent": 0.15, "good": 0.10, "acceptable": 0.05},
        "turnover": {"high": 0.8, "medium": 0.5, "low": 0.3},
        "drawdown": {"high": -0.20, "medium": -0.10, "low": -0.05},
        "margin": {"high": 0.05, "medium": 0.03, "low": 0.01},
        "correlation": {"high": 0.7, "medium": 0.5, "low": 0.3}
    }

def __init__(self, json_file_path: str):
        """
        初始化分析器

Args:
            json_file_path: JSON 文件路径
        """
        self.json_file_path = Path(json_file_path)
        self.data: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.alphas: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

@staticmethod
    def format_value(value: Optional[float], decimals: int = 4) -> str:
        """
        格式化数值，处理None值

Args:
            value: 要格式化的数值
            decimals: 小数位数

Returns:
            格式化后的字符串
        """
        if value is None:
            return "N/A"
        return f"{value:.{decimals}f}"
        """
        初始化分析器

Args:
            json_file_path: JSON 文件路径
        """
        self.json_file_path = Path(json_file_path)
        self.data: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.alphas: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

def load_data(self) -> bool:
        """
        加载 JSON 数据

Returns:
            是否成功加载
        """
        try:
            with open(self.json_file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                self.data = json.load(f)

self.alphas = self.data.get('alphas', [])
            print(f"成功加载 {len(self.alphas)} 条 Alpha 记录")
            return True

except FileNotFoundError:
            print(f"错误: 文件不存在 - {self.json_file_path}")
            return False
        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            print(f"错误: JSON 解析失败 - {str(e)}")
            return False
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"错误: 加载数据时出错 - {str(e)}")
            return False

def get_numeric_field(self, alpha: Dict[str, Any], field_name: str) -> Optional[float]:
        """
        安全地获取数值字段

Args:
            alpha: Alpha 数据字典
            field_name: 字段名

Returns:
            数值或 None
        """
        value = alpha.get(field_name)
        if value is None:
            return None

try:
            return float(value)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return None

def evaluate_performance(self, alpha: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """
        评估单个 Alpha 的性能

Args:
            alpha: Alpha 数据字典

Returns:
            性能评估结果
        """
        evaluation = {}

sharpe = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'sharpe')
        if sharpe is not None:
            if sharpe >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['sharpe']['excellent']:
                evaluation['sharpe'] = 'excellent'
            elif sharpe >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['sharpe']['good']:
                evaluation['sharpe'] = 'good'
            elif sharpe >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['sharpe']['acceptable']:
                evaluation['sharpe'] = 'acceptable'
            else:
                evaluation['sharpe'] = 'poor'

fitness = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'fitness')
        if fitness is not None:
            if fitness >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['fitness']['excellent']:
                evaluation['fitness'] = 'excellent'
            elif fitness >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['fitness']['good']:
                evaluation['fitness'] = 'good'
            elif fitness >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['fitness']['acceptable']:
                evaluation['fitness'] = 'acceptable'
            else:
                evaluation['fitness'] = 'poor'

returns = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'returns')
        if returns is not None:
            if returns >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['returns']['excellent']:
                evaluation['returns'] = 'excellent'
            elif returns >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['returns']['good']:
                evaluation['returns'] = 'good'
            elif returns >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['returns']['acceptable']:
                evaluation['returns'] = 'acceptable'
            else:
                evaluation['returns'] = 'poor'

turnover = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'turnover')
        if turnover is not None:
            if turnover >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['turnover']['high']:
                evaluation['turnover'] = 'high'
            elif turnover >= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['turnover']['medium']:
                evaluation['turnover'] = 'medium'
            else:
                evaluation['turnover'] = 'low'

drawdown = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'drawdown')
        if drawdown is not None:
            if drawdown <= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['drawdown']['high']:
                evaluation['drawdown'] = 'high_risk'
            elif drawdown <= self.PERFORMANCE_THRESHOLDS['drawdown']['medium']:
                evaluation['drawdown'] = 'medium_risk'
            else:
                evaluation['drawdown'] = 'low_risk'

return evaluation

def get_statistics(self, field_name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, float]]:
        """
        获取指定字段的统计信息

Args:
            field_name: 字段名

Returns:
            统计信息字典
        """
        values = []
        for alpha in self.alphas:
            value = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, field_name)
            if value is not None:
                values.append(value)

if not values:
            return None

return {
            "count": len(values),
            "mean": mean(values),
            "median": median(values),
            "min": min(values),
            "max": max(values),
            "stdev": stdev(values) if len(values) > 1 else 0
        }

def filter_alphas(self, filters: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        根据条件筛选 Alpha

Args:
            filters: 筛选条件字典，例如 {"sharpe": {"min": 1.0, "max": 2.0}}

Returns:
            筛选后的 Alpha 列表
        """
        filtered = []

for alpha in self.alphas:
            match = True

for field, conditions in filters.items():
                value = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, field)

if value is None:
                    match = False
                    break

if "min" in conditions and value < conditions["min"]:
                    match = False
                    break

if "max" in conditions and value > conditions["max"]:
                    match = False
                    break

if "equals" in conditions and value != conditions["equals"]:
                    match = False
                    break

if match:
                filtered.append(alpha)

return filtered

def rank_alphas(self, field_name: str, ascending: bool = False) -> List[Tuple[int, Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """
        根据指定字段对 Alpha 排序

Args:
            field_name: 排序字段
            ascending: 是否升序

Returns:
            排序后的 (索引, Alpha) 列表
        """
        indexed_alphas = [(i, alpha) for i, alpha in enumerate(self.alphas)]

def sort_key(item):
            _, alpha = item
            value = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, field_name)
            return value if value is not None else float('-inf' if ascending else 'inf')

sorted_alphas = sorted(indexed_alphas, key=sort_key, reverse=not ascending)
        return sorted_alphas

def get_top_alphas(self, field_name: str, top_n: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        获取指定字段的前 N 个 Alpha

Args:
            field_name: 排序字段
            top_n: 返回数量

Returns:
            前 N 个 Alpha 列表
        """
        sorted_alphas = self.rank_alphas(field_name, ascending=False)
        return [alpha for _, alpha in sorted_alphas[:top_n]]

def get_bottom_alphas(self, field_name: str, bottom_n: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        获取指定字段的后 N 个 Alpha

Args:
            field_name: 排序字段
            bottom_n: 返回数量

Returns:
            后 N 个 Alpha 列表
        """
        sorted_alphas = self.rank_alphas(field_name, ascending=True)
        return [alpha for _, alpha in sorted_alphas[:bottom_n]]

def analyze_fields(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        分析所有字段的统计信息

Returns:
            字段统计信息字典
        """
        if not self.alphas:
            return {}

# 获取所有字段名
        all_fields = set()
        for alpha in self.alphas:
            all_fields.update(alpha.keys())

analysis = {}

for field in sorted(all_fields):
            stats = self.get_statistics(field)
            if stats:
                analysis[field] = stats

return analysis

def generate_report(self, output_file: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        """
        生成分析报告

Args:
            output_file: 输出文件路径，如果为 None 则返回字符串

Returns:
            报告内容
        """
        report_lines = []

report_lines.append("=" * 80)
        report_lines.append("Alpha 数据分析报告")
        report_lines.append("=" * 80)
        report_lines.append(f"数据源: {self.json_file_path}")
        report_lines.append(f"总记录数: {len(self.alphas)}")
        report_lines.append("=" * 80)
        report_lines.append("")

# 字段统计
        report_lines.append("## 字段统计信息")
        report_lines.append("-" * 80)

field_stats = self.analyze_fields()

for field, stats in sorted(field_stats.items()):
            report_lines.append(f"\n### {field}")
            report_lines.append(f"  数量: {stats['count']}")
            report_lines.append(f"  平均值: {stats['mean']:.4f}")
            report_lines.append(f"  中位数: {stats['median']:.4f}")
            report_lines.append(f"  最小值: {stats['min']:.4f}")
            report_lines.append(f"  最大值: {stats['max']:.4f}")
            if stats['stdev'] > 0:
                report_lines.append(f"  标准差: {stats['stdev']:.4f}")

report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append("=" * 80)
        report_lines.append("## 性能排名")
        report_lines.append("-" * 80)

# Sharpe 排名
        report_lines.append("\n### Sharpe Top 10")
        top_sharpe = self.get_top_alphas('sharpe', 10)
        for i, alpha in enumerate(top_sharpe, 1):
            sharpe = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'sharpe')
            alpha_id = alpha.get('id', alpha.get('alpha_id', 'N/A'))
            report_lines.append(f"  {i}. ID={alpha_id}, Sharpe={self.format_value(sharpe)}")

report_lines.append("\n### Fitness Top 10")
        top_fitness = self.get_top_alphas('fitness', 10)
        for i, alpha in enumerate(top_fitness, 1):
            fitness = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'fitness')
            alpha_id = alpha.get('id', alpha.get('alpha_id', 'N/A'))
            report_lines.append(f"  {i}. ID={alpha_id}, Fitness={self.format_value(fitness)}")

report_lines.append("\n### Returns Top 10")
        top_returns = self.get_top_alphas('returns', 10)
        for i, alpha in enumerate(top_returns, 1):
            returns = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'returns')
            alpha_id = alpha.get('id', alpha.get('alpha_id', 'N/A'))
            report_lines.append(f"  {i}. ID={alpha_id}, Returns={self.format_value(returns)}")

report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append("=" * 80)
        report_lines.append("## 高潜力 Alpha（Sharpe > 1.0）")
        report_lines.append("-" * 80)

high_potential = self.filter_alphas({"sharpe": {"min": 1.0}})
        for alpha in high_potential:
            sharpe = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'sharpe')
            fitness = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'fitness')
            returns = self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'returns')
            alpha_id = alpha.get('id', alpha.get('alpha_id', 'N/A'))
            report_lines.append(f"  ID={alpha_id}, Sharpe={self.format_value(sharpe)}, Fitness={self.format_value(fitness)}, Returns={self.format_value(returns)}")

report_lines.append("")
        report_lines.append("=" * 80)
        report_lines.append(f"找到 {len(high_potential)} 个高潜力 Alpha")
        report_lines.append("=" * 80)

report_content = "\n".join(report_lines)

if output_file:
            try:
                with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    f.write(report_content)
                print(f"报告已保存到: {output_file}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"保存报告时出错: {str(e)}")

return report_content

def export_for_agent(self, output_file: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        导出适合智能体分析的数据格式

Args:
            output_file: 输出文件路径

Returns:
            适合智能体的数据字典
        """
        agent_data = {
            "summary": {
                "total_alphas": len(self.alphas),
                "field_statistics": self.analyze_fields()
            },
            "rankings": {
                "top_by_sharpe": [
                    {
                        "id": alpha.get('id', alpha.get('alpha_id', 'N/A')),
                        "sharpe": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'sharpe'),
                        "fitness": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'fitness'),
                        "returns": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'returns')
                    }
                    for alpha in self.get_top_alphas('sharpe', 20)
                ],
                "top_by_fitness": [
                    {
                        "id": alpha.get('id', alpha.get('alpha_id', 'N/A')),
                        "sharpe": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'sharpe'),
                        "fitness": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'fitness'),
                        "returns": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'returns')
                    }
                    for alpha in self.get_top_alphas('fitness', 20)
                ]
            },
            "high_potential": [
                {
                    "id": alpha.get('id', alpha.get('alpha_id', 'N/A')),
                    "sharpe": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'sharpe'),
                    "fitness": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'fitness'),
                    "returns": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'returns'),
                    "turnover": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'turnover'),
                    "drawdown": self.get_numeric_field(alpha, 'drawdown'),
                    "evaluation": self.evaluate_performance(alpha)
                }
                for alpha in self.filter_alphas({"sharpe": {"min": 1.0}})
            ],
            "all_alphas": self.alphas
        }

if output_file:
            try:
                with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(agent_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
                print(f"智能体数据已导出到: {output_file}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"导出数据时出错: {str(e)}")

return agent_data

def main():
    """主函数"""
    if len(sys.argv) < 2:
        print("用法: python analyze_alpha_data.py <json_file_path> [output_file]")
        print("示例: python analyze_alpha_data.py 回测优秀待优化.json")
        print("")
        print("功能:")
        print("  1. 生成分析报告")
        print("  2. 导出智能体格式数据")
        print("  3. 统计分析")
        sys.exit(1)

json_file = sys.argv[1]
    analyzer = AlphaAnalyzer(json_file)

if not analyzer.load_data():
        sys.exit(1)

# 生成报告
    report_file = json_file.replace('.json', '_analysis_report.txt')
    analyzer.generate_report(report_file)

# 导出智能体数据
    agent_data_file = json_file.replace('.json', '_agent_data.json')
    analyzer.export_for_agent(agent_data_file)

print("\n分析完成！")
    print(f"  分析报告: {report_file}")
    print(f"  智能体数据: {agent_data_file}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

# 🚀 高效 Alpha 研发自动化指令 (Alpha Factory Workflow v3.0)

**角色设定**：你现在是一名资深量化研究员，精通 WorldQuant BRAIN 平台。你的任务是针对指定数据集，通过“硬性指标筛选 + 语义逻辑构建 + 信号效能审计 + 深度诊断优化”的流程，高效产出具有实盘价值的 Alpha。

### 📍 目标配置

* **Region**: [EUR]

* **Dataset ID**: [model26]

* **Universe**: [TOP2500]

* **Delay**: [1]

* **Neutralization**: [Fast] 说明：中性化方式必须使用FAST。

---

### 🛠 执行流程（严格按顺序执行）

#### 1. 自动化数据审计 (Data Audit)

* **动作**：调用 `get_datafields` 获取该数据集下所有字段。

* **硬性筛选标准**：

1. **覆盖率**：过滤 `coverage < 0.6` 的字段。

2. **数据类型**：识别连续型（适合 `ts_`）与离散型字段。

3. **活跃度**：激活 `brain-datafield-exploration-general` 采样检查更新频率，剔除“僵尸字段”。

* **产出**：保存“高精炼字段表”至 `E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\优质数据字段采集\EUR-TOP2500-model26.md`，将字段划分为价值、动量、质量、情绪、分析师行为等类别。

1.csv`,将字段划分为价值、动量、质量、情绪、分析师行为等类别。

#### 2. 语义分析与特征工程 (Feature Engineering)

* **动作**：激活 `brain-data-feature-engineering` 技能自动生成的策略构思。

* **逻辑构建**：将字段划分为价值、动量、质量、情绪、分析师行为等类别。

* **创意生成**：单次创造性生成变体 **6 个** 差异化 Alpha 表达式。

* **降维要求**：VECTOR 类型字段必须先使用 `vec_avg`, `vec_sum` 等算子降维。

* **逻辑生成**：根据`brain-data-feature-engineering` 技能，生成一份针对指定数据集的思考，例@E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\output_report\USA_1_analyst4_ideas.md，保存在E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\output_report，每个表达式配有一句话金融逻辑解释。

* 强烈建议：在**创意生成**alpha表达式时，先学习@E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\成功alpha经验，然后再思考目标数据字段，理解经济学逻辑，构建alpha表达式的时候，学习的内容仅作为参考，不要局限，这一个步骤非常关键，决定了alpha质量的好坏，所以在这一步骤你要花费更多时间去思考构建。

#### 3. 批量并行回测 (Massive Multi-Sim)

* **动作**：调用 `create_multiSim` 工具。

* **语法校准**：参考 `E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\167操作符全示例.md`，确保 100% 正确。

* **参数配置**：`neutralization` (根据字段特性), `decay` (根据字段特性), `truncation` (根据字段特性)。

* **监控**：提取 `Sharpe`, `Fitness`, `Turnover`, `Returns`, `Margin` 以及 `Prod Correlation`。

#### 4. 信号审计、过滤与深度诊断 (Evaluation & Diagnostic Optimization)

* **动作**：执行双重过滤逻辑与问题诊断：

> > **第一步：效能与拥挤度过滤**

首先在 **逻辑生成** 这一步对指定数据集下产生的ideas产生的文件里的表达式进行回测探索，观察每个表达式的Sharpe和Margin 指标：

> * 若 **$|Sharpe| < 0.7$** ：判定信号太弱，如果Margin > 万10，同时phl曲线有上升趋势且较规律直接进入@E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\点塔策略.md。

> * 若 **$|Sharpe| > 0.7$** ：判定为“高价值种子”，进入第二步优化环节。

> **第二步：数据字段质量过滤**

> *若在**第一步：效能与拥挤度过滤** 没有产生|Sharpe| > 0.7的alpha表达式，那么返回**逻辑生成**步骤，重新思考再生成一份新的Ideas文件，然后继续回测；

> *如果存在|Sharpe| > 0.7的alpha表达式，立即检测Prod Correlation：

如果**`Prod Correlation > 0.7`**：判定为过度拥挤，直接进入@E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\点塔策略.md，

如果**`Prod Correlation < 0.7`**：判定为“高价值种子”，进入第三步优化环节

> **第三步：常规优化 (Standard Optimization)**

> * 激活 `brain-improve-alpha-performance` 尝试微调参数，目标：$Sharpe > 1.58$ 且 $Fitness > 1$、$Margin > 10‱。

>

>

> **第四步：问题诊断与深度优化 (Diagnostic Phase)**

> * 如果常规优化后指标仍未达标，必须**观察因子回测报告中的问题点**：

> * **重点排查**：检查是否存在 **Sub-universe Sharpe** 问题（即 Alpha 在不同子行业、子板块或市值分组中表现差异过大，导致整体 Sharpe 不稳）。

> * **知识挂载**：若存在该问题，必须调用并深入学习文档：`E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\知识库\Sub-universe Sharpe .md`。

> * **针对性解决**：经过多轮优化后，仍无法达标，进入**第五步：进入系统级优化**。

>**第五步：进入系统级优化**

> * 此步骤是优化步骤，@E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\优化指令.md，严格按照优化文档指令流程执行。

* **产出与保存**：

1. **Alpha指标要求**：

Alpha指标要求：

l核心指标：

- Sharpe: 2.23 (目标 > 1)

- Fitness: 1.37 (目标 > 0.5)

- Margin: 18.33‱ (目标 > 10‱)

- Power Pool Correlation: 0.42 (目标 < 0.5)

- Sub-universe Sharpe of 1.48 is above cutoff of 1.43.

- 2 year Sharpe of 1.89 is above cutoff of 1.58.

2.**Alpha操作符要求**

成功优化的alpha最后一步必须进行操作符的简化操作，具体参考@E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\简化操作符操作.md

保存至 `E:\cnhkmcp-3.1.5\优质Alpha产出\exclent_alpha.md`。

3. **迭代循环**：若本批次全部失败，返回第 2 步重新构思逻辑。

---

**下一步请执行**：开始第 1 步：自动化数据审计，扫描 `model26` 数据集时，必须指定地区EUR,delay=1` 数据集时，必须指定地区EUR,delay=1,universe=TOP2500。

数据集和地区手动改，需要在gemini cli中的GEMINI.md文件里添加限制，防止AI幻觉：

Gemini Added Memories
核心策略审计协议 (Alpha Audit Protocol)
触发条件：
当对话涉及 “Alpha”、“策略回测”、“策略检查”，或提供包含 checks 列表的数据块时，立即启动。
核心审计逻辑（双重否决制）：
结果项一票否决： 深度扫描 checks 列表中的 result 列。只要包含任何一个 "FAIL" 字样（不区分大小写），该 Alpha 即刻判定为 【不达标】。
风险项强制拦截： 深度扫描 WARNING（或警告）内容。若出现以下关键词，即使 result 全部为 PASS，也必须判定为 【不达标】：
Weight concentration (权重集中)
2 year Sharpe (两年夏普)
IS ladder Sharpe
确认达标门槛： 只有当 result 列全为 PASS/SUCCESS/OK，且 WARNING 项中不含上述特定风险关键词时，才允许确认“策略达标”。
强制审计报告格式：
[审计状态]：明确标出 【不达标】 或 【已通过】。
[核查详情]：列出包含 check_item、result 及 WARNING 的 Markdown 表格。
[红线警告]：
若因 FAIL 失败：加粗列出所有含有 "FAIL" 的具体项目。
若因 WARNING 拦截：加粗指出触发拦截的风险点（如：检测到 Weight concentration，强制拦截）。
[禁止项]：禁止进行任何“整体尚可”、“虽然有风险但潜力大”的粉饰性评价。有红线即为失败，首要任务是揭示风险。
触发条件：
当需要去调用MCP去平台查找数据字段时，必须指定地区，delay和Universe，只有先明确这三个，才能准确查到指定的数据字段，例如（明确指定EUR,delay=1,TOPCS1600）。

---
name: automated-pyramid-hunter-gemini-cli
description: Gemini CLI专用版全自动五段式金字塔猎人工作流 - 强制检查清单与阻断机制（完整操作符规范版）
