### 评论 #16 (作者: MM38095, 时间: 4个月前)

## **开箱即用：本地多槽并发回测skill** 

---

name: "多槽并发回测"

description: "WorldQuant BRAIN平台1-8槽×2-10个Alpha灵活并发回测工具。Invoke when user needs batch alpha simulation, multi-slot backtesting, or running multiple alpha variants concurrently."

---

**# 多槽并发回测**

WorldQuant BRAIN平台的灵活并发回测工具，支持 **1-8槽 × 2-10个Alpha** 的任意组合。

**## 使用场景**

- 小规模迭代：1-4槽×2-3个Alpha，快速验证变体

- 中等规模：4-8槽×5个Alpha，参数扫描

- 大批量筛选：8槽×10个Alpha，一次性回测80个表达式

- 需要快速获取回测结果(IS统计数据+checks)

**## 工作流程**

### 第1步：准备SLOT_CONFIGS

用户需要定义1-8个槽位配置，每个槽位包含：

- `name`: 槽位描述

- `expressions`: 2-10个FASTEXPR表达式列表

- `cm`: 每个表达式的设置映射(可选)，格式: `{0: {d, decay, neut, trunc, mt}, 1: {...}, ...}`

- `settings`: 默认设置(当cm未指定时使用)

**关键设置参数**:

- `d`: delay (0或1)

- `decay`: 衰减值 (0-12)

- `neut`: 中性化 (SUBINDUSTRY/INDUSTRY/SECTOR/MARKET/STATISTICAL/NONE等)（可自由增减）

- `trunc`: 截断值 (0.001-0.01)

- `mt`: maxTrade (ON/OFF)

**中性化要求**: 必须每个中性化都先尝试一遍！优先尝试SUBINDUSTRY > INDUSTRY > SECTOR > MARKET，不到万不得已不用NONE。

**批量回测模式**: 当表达式数量超过单批容量(槽数×每槽表达式数)时，自动分批提交。例如9642条表达式用8槽×10模式，需分121批。

### 第2步：生成Python回测脚本

使用以下模板生成脚本，保存到工作目录并执行：

```python

#!/usr/bin/env python3

"""R{round}: {desc} — {num_slots}槽x{per_slot}个Alpha并发回测"""

import json, time, requests, base64, threading, csv, re, shutil, tempfile

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed

from pathlib import Path

BASE_URL = " [https://api.worldquantbrain.com](https://api.worldquantbrain.com) "

# === 用户自定义区域 ===

SLOT_CONFIGS = [

# 1-8个槽位配置，每槽2-10个表达式...

]

# 批量回测模式：从JSON文件加载表达式列表，自动分批

BATCH_MODE = False  # 设为True启用批量模式

BATCH_EXPR_FILE = ""  # JSON表达式文件路径

BATCH_CSV_FILE = ""  # 字段信息CSV路径

BATCH_OUT_DIR = ""  # 输出目录

BATCH_NUM_SLOTS = 8  # 批量模式槽数

BATCH_PER_SLOT = 10  # 批量模式每槽表达式数

BATCH_DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {

"instrumentType": "EQUITY", "region": "USA", "universe": "TOP3000",

"delay": 0, "decay": 0, "neutralization": "STATISTICAL",

"truncation": 0.01, "pasteurization": "ON", "unitHandling": "VERIFY",

"nanHandling": "ON", "visualization": False, "language": "FASTEXPR",

"testPeriod": "P0Y0M", "maxTrade": "ON",

}

BATCH_FILTER = {"sharpe": 0.9, "fitness": 0.5}  # 筛选阈值

BATCH_TAG = "first_round"  # 通过筛选的标签名

BATCH_TAG_COLOR = "#FFFF00"  # 标签颜色

# === 核心框架（不要修改） ===

def lc():

for cp in [Path(r"d:\VScode\MCP-Brain\learn-3\AIworker\user_config.json"),Path.home()/".brain_mcp_config.json"]:

if cp.exists():

with open(cp,'r',encoding='utf-8') as f: return json.load(f)

return {}

def auth(s,e,p):

r=s.post(f'{BASE_URL}/authentication',headers={'Authorization':f'Basic {base64.b64encode(f"{e}:{p}".encode()).decode()}'})

return r.status_code in [200,201]

class SP:

def __init__(self,e,p,sz=10):

self._p=[]; self._l=threading.Lock(); self._e=e; self._pw=p

for _ in range(sz):

s=requests.Session(); s.headers.update({'User-Agent':'Mozilla/5.0'})

if auth(s,e,p): self._p.append(s)

print(f"  SP:{len(self._p)}/{sz}")

def b(self):

with self._l:

if self._p: return self._p.pop()

s=requests.Session(); s.headers.update({'User-Agent':'Mozilla/5.0'}); auth(s,self._e,self._pw); return s

def r(self,s):

with self._l:

if len(self._p)<20: self._p.append(s)

def sub(s,exprs,si,settings=None):

md=[]

for expr in exprs:

st=settings or BATCH_DEFAULT_SETTINGS

md.append({'type':'REGULAR','settings':#可自由更改 {'instrumentType':st.get('instrumentType','EQUITY'),'region':st.get('region','USA'),'universe':st.get('universe','TOP3000'),'delay':st.get('delay',0),'decay':st.get('decay',0),'neutralization':st.get('neutralization','STATISTICAL'),'truncation':st.get('truncation',0.01),'pasteurization':st.get('pasteurization','ON'),'unitHandling':st.get('unitHandling','VERIFY'),'nanHandling':st.get('nanHandling','ON'),'visualization':st.get('visualization',False),'language':st.get('language','FASTEXPR'),'testPeriod':st.get('testPeriod','P0Y0M'),'maxTrade':st.get('maxTrade','ON')},'regular':expr})

r=s.post(f"{BASE_URL}/simulations",json=md)

if r.status_code!=201: print(f"  S{si}: F{r.status_code} {r.text[:100]}"); return None

print(f"  S{si}: OK({len(md)})"); return r.headers.get('Location','')

def sub_slot(s,sc,si):

exprs=sc["expressions"]; cm=sc.get("cm"); ds=sc.get("settings",{}); md=[]

for i,expr in enumerate(exprs):

st=cm.get(i,ds) if cm else ds

md.append({'type':'REGULAR','settings':{'instrumentType':'EQUITY','region':'USA','universe':'TOP3000','delay':st['d'],'decay':st['decay'],'neutralization':st['neut'],'truncation':st['trunc'],'pasteurization':st.get('pasteurization','ON'),'unitHandling':'VERIFY','nanHandling':st.get('nanHandling','ON'),'visualization':False,'language':'FASTEXPR','testPeriod':'P0Y0M','maxTrade':st.get('mt','ON')},'regular':expr})

r=s.post(f"{BASE_URL}/simulations",json=md)

if r.status_code!=201: print(f"  S{si}: F{r.status_code}"); return None

print(f"  S{si}: OK({len(md)})"); return r.headers.get('Location','')

def wfc(s,loc,si,to=600):

st=time.time()

while time.time()-st<to:

try:

r=s.get(loc)

if r.status_code==200:

c=r.json().get('children',[])

if c: return c

time.sleep(float(r.headers.get("Retry-After",5)))

else: time.sleep(5)

except: time.sleep(5)

return []

def gad(s,aid):

try:

r=s.get(f"{BASE_URL}/alphas/{aid}")

if r.status_code==200:

a=r.json(); isd=a.get('is',{}) or {}; cs=isd.get('checks',[]); fl=[c for c in cs if c.get('result')=='FAIL']

pc_check=next((c for c in cs if c.get('name')=='PROD_CORRELATION'),{})

pc_val=pc_check.get('value')

rv_check=next((c for c in cs if c.get('name')=='REVERSION_COMPONENT'),{})

return {'alpha_id':aid,'status':'SUCCESS','sharpe':isd.get('sharpe'),'fitness':isd.get('fitness'),'turnover':isd.get('turnover'),'margin':isd.get('margin'),'sp':next((c for c in cs if c.get('name')=='LOW_SHARPE'),{}).get('result')!='FAIL','fp':next((c for c in cs if c.get('name')=='LOW_FITNESS'),{}).get('result')!='FAIL','lp':next((c for c in cs if c.get('name')=='IS_LADDER_SHARPE'),{}).get('result')=='PASS','su':next((c for c in cs if c.get('name')=='LOW_SUB_UNIVERSE_SHARPE'),{}).get('result')=='PASS','rv':rv_check.get('result','N/A') if rv_check.get('result') else 'N/A','neut':a.get('settings',{}).get('neutralization','?'),'dec':a.get('settings',{}).get('decay',0),'nfail':len(fl),'pc':pc_val}

except: pass

return None

def pc(sp,cid,expr,si,ci):

s=sp.b()

try:

for _ in range(120):

try:

r=s.get(f"{BASE_URL}/simulations/{cid}")

if r.status_code==200:

ad=r.json()

if ad.get('status') in ['ERROR','CANCELLED']: return {'expr':expr[:60],'status':'ERROR','err':ad.get('message','?'),'slot':si}

if float(r.headers.get("Retry-After",0))==0:

aid=ad.get("alpha")

if aid:

res=gad(s,aid)

if res: res['slot']=si; res['expression']=expr; return res

return {'expr':expr[:60],'status':'ERROR','slot':si}

time.sleep(float(r.headers.get("Retry-After",5)))

else: time.sleep(2)

except: time.sleep(2)

return {'expr':expr[:60],'status':'TIMEOUT','slot':si}

finally: sp.r(s)

def tag_alpha(s,aid,tag,color="#FFFF00"):

try:

r=s.put(f"{BASE_URL}/alphas/{aid}/tags/{tag}",json={"color":color,"name":tag})

return r.status_code in [200,201]

except: return False

def run_batch(sp,exprs,batch_label,settings=None):

ns=min(BATCH_NUM_SLOTS,(len(exprs)+BATCH_PER_SLOT-1)//BATCH_PER_SLOT)

slots=[]

for i in range(ns):

s=i*BATCH_PER_SLOT; e=s+BATCH_PER_SLOT; b=exprs[s:e]

if b: slots.append((i,b))

ss=[sp.b() for _ in range(len(slots))]; sl={}

with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(slots)) as ex:

ff={ex.submit(sub,ss[i],b,i,settings):i for i,b in slots}

for f in as_completed(ff):

loc=f.result()

if loc: sl[ff[f]]=loc

sc={}

with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(sl)) as ex:

ff={ex.submit(wfc,ss[i],sl[i],i):i for i in sl}

for f in as_completed(ff):

i=ff[f]; c=f.result()

if isinstance(c,list):

bexprs=next((b for (s,b) in slots if s==i),[])

sc[i]=[(c[j],bexprs[j]) for j in range(min(len(c),len(bexprs)))]

else: sc[i]=[]

for s in ss: sp.r(s)

tc=sum(len(v) for v in sc.values()); ar=[]

with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=min(tc,18)) as ex:

ff={}

for si,cl in sc.items():

for ci,(cid,expr) in enumerate(cl):

ff[ex.submit(pc,sp,cid,expr,si,ci)]=(si,ci)

dc=0

for f in as_completed(ff):

r=f.result(); ar.append(r); dc+=1

if r.get('status')=='SUCCESS':

aid=r.get('alpha_id','?'); s=r.get('sharpe',0) or 0

t=r.get('turnover',0) or 0; m=r.get('margin',0) or 0

print(f"    [{dc}/{tc}] S{r.get('slot','?')}: {aid} S={s:.2f} F={r.get('fitness',0) or 0:.2f} T={t:.2%}")

else: print(f"    [{dc}/{tc}] S{r.get('slot','?')}: ERR {r.get('err','')[:60]}")

return ar

def main():

cfg=lc(); cr=cfg.get("credentials",{})

e,p=cr.get("email"),cr.get("password")

if not e or not p: print("No creds!"); return

if BATCH_MODE:

print("="*70)

print(f"批量并发回测: {BATCH_NUM_SLOTS}槽x{BATCH_PER_SLOT}个Alpha")

print(f"筛选: |sharpe|>{BATCH_FILTER['sharpe']} |fitness|>{BATCH_FILTER['fitness']}")

print("="*70)

with open(BATCH_EXPR_FILE,'r',encoding='utf-8') as f: exprs=json.load(f)

print(f"  加载{len(exprs)}条表达式")

sp=SP(e,p,sz=max(BATCH_NUM_SLOTS+8,14))

bsz=BATCH_NUM_SLOTS*BATCH_PER_SLOT; nb=(len(exprs)+bsz-1)//bsz

all_results=[]

for bi in range(nb):

s=bi*bsz; e_=min(s+bsz,len(exprs)); batch=exprs[s:e_]

label=f"Batch{bi+1}/{nb}"

print(f"\n  {label}: [{s}:{e_}]({len(batch)}条)")

br=run_batch(sp,batch,label)

all_results.extend(br)

succ=[r for r in all_results if r.get('status')=='SUCCESS']

filt_s=BATCH_FILTER['sharpe']; filt_f=BATCH_FILTER['fitness']

qual=[r for r in succ if (abs(r.get('sharpe',0) or 0)>filt_s and abs(r.get('fitness',0) or 0)>filt_f)]

print(f"\n  筛选结果: {len(qual)}/{len(succ)}通过(|S|>{filt_s},|F|>{filt_f})")

ts=sp.b(); tagged=0

for r in qual:

if tag_alpha(ts,r.get('alpha_id'),BATCH_TAG,BATCH_TAG_COLOR): tagged+=1

sp.r(ts)

print(f"  标签: {tagged}/{len(qual)} tagged '{BATCH_TAG}'")

csv_path=Path(BATCH_OUT_DIR)/"初步筛选结果.csv"

with open(csv_path,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='') as f:

w=csv.writer(f); w.writerow(['alphaID','sharp_value','fitness_value'])

for r in qual: w.writerow([r.get('alpha_id',''),r.get('sharpe',0),r.get('fitness',0)])

print(f"  Saved {csv_path}")

out=Path(BATCH_OUT_DIR)/"round1_backtest_results.json"

with open(out,'w',encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump({'alpha_results':all_results},f,indent=2,default=str)

print(f"  Saved {out}")

print(f"\n第一轮完成! {len(qual)}/{len(succ)}通过筛选")

else:

ns=len(SLOT_CONFIGS); ta=sum(len(sc['expressions']) for sc in SLOT_CONFIGS)

print(f"{ns}槽x{ta//ns if ns else 0}个Alpha = {ta}并发回测")

sp=SP(e,p,sz=max(ns+8,16))

t0=time.time(); sl={}; ss=[sp.b() for _ in range(ns)]

with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=ns) as ex:

ff={ex.submit(sub_slot,ss[i],SLOT_CONFIGS[i],i):i for i in range(ns)}

for f in as_completed(ff):

loc=f.result()

if loc: sl[ff[f]]=loc

print(f"  Sub:{time.time()-t0:.1f}s")

t0=time.time(); sc={}

with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=ns) as ex:

ff={ex.submit(wfc,ss[i],sl[i],i):i for i in sl}

for f in as_completed(ff):

i=ff[f]; c=f.result()

exprs=SLOT_CONFIGS[i]["expressions"]

sc[i]=[(c[j],exprs[j]) for j in range(min(len(c),len(exprs)))]

for s in ss: sp.r(s)

print(f"  Wait:{time.time()-t0:.1f}s")

tc=sum(len(v) for v in sc.values()); t0=time.time(); ar=[]

with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=min(tc,20)) as ex:

ff={}

for si,cl in sc.items():

for ci,(cid,expr) in enumerate(cl):

ff[ex.submit(pc,sp,cid,expr,si,ci)]=(si,ci)

dc=0

for f in as_completed(ff):

r=f.result(); ar.append(r); dc+=1

if r.get('status')=='SUCCESS':

aid=r.get('alpha_id','?'); s=r.get('sharpe',0) or 0

t=r.get('turnover',0) or 0; m=r.get('margin',0) or 0

pc_v=r.get('pc')

pc_s=f" PC={pc_v:.4f}" if pc_v is not None else ""

print(f"  [{dc}/{tc}] S{r.get('slot','?')}: {aid} S={s:.2f} T={t:.2%} M={m:.6f}{pc_s}")

print(f"  Poll:{time.time()-t0:.1f}s")

succ=[r for r in ar if r.get('status')=='SUCCESS']

print("\nSUMMARY(Margin↓)")

for r in sorted(succ,key=lambda x:x.get('margin',0) or 0,reverse=True):

s=r.get('sharpe',0) or 0; t=r.get('turnover',0) or 0; m=r.get('margin',0) or 0

pc_v=r.get('pc'); slot=r.get('slot','?'); nf=r.get('nfail',0)

sp_='PASS' if r.get('sp') else 'FAIL'; lp_='PASS' if r.get('lp') else 'FAIL'

rv=r.get('rv','?'); dec=r.get('dec',0); ms='OK' if m>0.0015 else 'LOW'

mk="***CAND***" if (nf==0 and m>0.0015 and rv!='WARNING') else ""

pc_s=f" PC={pc_v:.4f}" if pc_v is not None else " PC=?"

print(f" S{slot} {r.get('alpha_id','?')}: S={s:.2f}[{sp_}] T={t:.2%} M={m:.6f}({ms}) D={dec}{pc_s} {mk}")

for r in ar:

if r.get('status')!='SUCCESS': print(f" S{r.get('slot','?')} E:{r.get('err','?')}")

out=Path(__file__).parent/f"batch_results.json"

with open(out,'w',encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump({'alpha_results':ar},f,indent=2,default=str)

topn=min(5,len(succ))

clean=[r for r in succ if r.get('sp') and r.get('fp') and r.get('lp') and r.get('nfail',0)==0 and r.get('rv')!='WARNING']

top=sorted(clean,key=lambda x:x.get('margin',0) or 0,reverse=True)[:topn]

if not top: top=sorted(succ,key=lambda x:x.get('margin',0) or 0,reverse=True)[:topn]

print(f"\n--- TOP {topn} for PC CHECK ---")

for r in top:

aid=r.get('alpha_id'); s=r.get('sharpe',0) or 0; m=r.get('margin',0) or 0

print(f"  {aid} (S={s:.2f} M={m:.6f})")

if __name__=="__main__": main()

```

### 第3步：执行脚本

```bash

python "脚本路径.py"

```

### 第4步：结果分析

脚本输出包含：

1. 每个alpha的IS统计(Sharpe, Fitness, Turnover, Margin)

2. checks结果(LOW_SHARPE, LOW_FITNESS, IS_LADDER_SHARPE等)

3. TOP N候选alpha列表 → 交给"PC快速检测"skill检查

4. 批量模式下自动生成`初步筛选结果.csv`和`round1_backtest_results.json`

## 两种使用模式

### 模式A：SLOT_CONFIGS模式（小规模迭代优化）

- 设置`BATCH_MODE = False`

- 在`SLOT_CONFIGS`中定义1-8个槽位，每槽2-10个表达式

- 每个槽位可有不同的设置参数（delay, decay, neutralization等）

- 适用于：Alpha变体优化、参数扫描

### 模式B：批量模式（大批量筛选）

- 设置`BATCH_MODE = True`

- 指定`BATCH_EXPR_FILE`（JSON表达式文件路径）

- 指定`BATCH_NUM_SLOTS`和`BATCH_PER_SLOT`

- 自动分批提交，自动筛选和打标签

- 适用于：从表达式文件批量回测、第一轮筛选

## 注意事项

1. **中性化优先级**: SUBINDUSTRY > INDUSTRY > SECTOR > MARKET > NONE

2. **超时熔断**: 单轮回测超过15分钟视为僵尸模拟

3. **错误诊断**: 若整体失败，必须查看具体错误信息

4. **并发限制**: 平台有并发模拟数限制，8槽已是最优

5. **SessionPool**: 使用线程安全的Session池管理API连接

6. **批量模式分批**: 表达式超过单批容量时自动分批，每批间无间隔

7. **Windows长路径**: 批量模式下表达式文件路径过长时，需先复制到临时目录

### 评论 #17 (作者: JX98676, 时间: 4个月前)

关于回测8*10的代码，经过我多次的验证以及实际的测试，最高一个小时能模拟将近600个策略

如果不会用，就丢给AI打工人，让他做

大大提高了回测的速度，以下是代码：

# 8x10 并发代码说明

说明：

- 你要求的文件名是 `8*10并发.md`，但 Windows 文件名不允许包含 `*`。
- 因此实际保存文件名为 `8x10并发.md`。
- 这里整理的是 `C:\Users\33146\Desktop\生态链0409 - 副本` 中与 `Multi-sim并发 = 8`、`每槽Alpha数 = 10` 直接相关的完整代码链。

## 一、代码作用概述

这组参数的调用链是：

1. `一键启动.py`
   在 GUI 中设置默认值：
   - `Multi-sim并发 = 8`
   - `每槽Alpha数 = 10`

2. `Alpha挖掘生态链.py`
   读取 GUI 配置，把 Alpha 列表按每槽 `10` 个切分，再以最多 `8` 个槽并发提交。

3. `ace_lib.py`
   真正向平台提交 multi-sim 任务，并轮询等待结果返回。

## 二、运行含义

- `Multi-sim并发 = 8`
  表示最多同时提交 `8` 个 multi-sim 槽任务。

- `每槽Alpha数 = 10`
  表示每个 multi-sim 槽里最多放 `10` 个 Alpha。

- 例如有 `587` 个 Alpha：
  会先切成 `59` 个槽；
  前 `58` 个槽每槽 `10` 个；
  最后 `1` 个槽放剩余的 `7` 个；
  最多同时跑 `8` 个槽。

## 三、相关文件

- `一键启动.py`
- `Alpha挖掘生态链.py`
- `ace_lib.py`

### 2. Alpha挖掘生态链.py

```python
    def build_simulation_data_list(self, alphas: List[str], config: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """批量构建 multi-sim 所需的 simulation_data 列表。"""
        return [self.build_simulation_data(alpha, config) for alpha in alphas]

@staticmethod
    def clamp_multi_sim_concurrency(concurrent_count: int) -> int:
        """将 multi-sim 并发限制在 ace_lib 支持的 1..8 范围。"""
        try:
            concurrent_count = int(concurrent_count)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            concurrent_count = 8

if concurrent_count < 1:
            logger.warning("multi-sim 并发数量小于 1，已自动调整为 1")
            return 1
        if concurrent_count > 8:
            logger.warning("multi-sim 并发数量超过 8，已自动调整为 8")
            return 8
        return concurrent_count

@staticmethod
    def clamp_multi_sim_slot_count(alpha_count_per_slot: int) -> int:
        """将 multi-sim 每槽 alpha 数量限制在 ace_lib 支持的 2..10 范围。"""
        try:
            alpha_count_per_slot = int(alpha_count_per_slot)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            alpha_count_per_slot = 10

if alpha_count_per_slot < 2:
            logger.warning("multi-sim 每槽 alpha 数量小于 2，已自动调整为 2")
            return 2
        if alpha_count_per_slot > 10:
            logger.warning("multi-sim 每槽 alpha 数量超过 10，已自动调整为 10")
            return 10
        return alpha_count_per_slot

def submit_multi_simulation(self, alphas: List[str], config: Dict) -> Tuple[Optional[dict], int]:
        """提交一批 REGULAR Alpha 的 multi-sim 任务。"""
        simulate_data_list = self.build_simulation_data_list(alphas, config)
        if not simulate_data_list:
            return None, 0

if len(simulate_data_list) == 1:
            progress_url, status_code = self.submit_simulation(alphas[0], config)
            if status_code != 201 or not progress_url:
                return None, status_code
            return {
                'mode': 'single',
                'progress_url': progress_url,
                'simulate_data_list': simulate_data_list,
            }, 201

task_handle = submit_multi_simulation_task(self.session, simulate_data_list)
        self.session = task_handle.get('session', self.session)
        simulate_response = task_handle.get('simulate_response')
        status_code = getattr(simulate_response, 'status_code', 0)
        return task_handle, status_code
```

```python
    def evaluate_fitness(self, alphas: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """评估Alpha适应度，支持 single-sim 与 multi-sim 两种回测路径。"""
        max_w = self.concurrent_manager.max_workers
        requested_multi_sim = bool(self.config.get('use_multi_sim', True))
        alpha_type = str(self.config.get('alpha_type', 'REGULAR') or 'REGULAR').upper()
        use_multi_sim = requested_multi_sim and alpha_type != 'SUPER'
        alpha_count_per_slot = self.concurrent_manager.clamp_multi_sim_slot_count(
            self.config.get('alpha_count_per_slot', 10)
        )
        multi_sim_concurrency = self.concurrent_manager.clamp_multi_sim_concurrency(
            self.config.get('multi_sim_concurrency', 8)
        )

if requested_multi_sim and alpha_type == 'SUPER':
            logger.warning("检测到 SUPER Alpha 配置，multi-sim 不支持，已自动降级为单 alpha 并发回测")

if use_multi_sim:
            logger.info(
                f"评估 {len(alphas)} 个Alpha（multi-sim，批并发={multi_sim_concurrency}，每槽={alpha_count_per_slot}）"
            )
        else:
            logger.info(f"评估 {len(alphas)} 个Alpha（单 alpha 并发，最大={max_w}）")

fitness_scores = {}

to_simulate = []
        batch_seen = set()
        cache_hits = 0
        batch_duplicates = 0
        for alpha in alphas:
            if alpha in self.simulation_cache:
                fitness_scores[alpha] = self.simulation_cache[alpha]
                cache_hits += 1
            elif alpha not in batch_seen:
                to_simulate.append(alpha)
                batch_seen.add(alpha)
            else:
                batch_duplicates += 1

if not to_simulate:
            logger.info("全部命中缓存或批内去重，无需模拟")
            self.fitness_scores = fitness_scores
            return fitness_scores

logger.info(
            f"  缓存命中: {cache_hits}, 批内去重: {batch_duplicates}, 需模拟: {len(to_simulate)}"
        )

if use_multi_sim and len(to_simulate) >= 2:
            new_scores = self._evaluate_fitness_multi_sim(
                to_simulate,
                concurrency=multi_sim_concurrency,
                alpha_count_per_slot=alpha_count_per_slot,
            )
        else:
            new_scores = self._evaluate_fitness_single_sim(to_simulate, max_w=max_w)

for alpha in to_simulate:
            score = new_scores.get(alpha, 0.0)
            fitness_scores[alpha] = score
            self.simulation_cache[alpha] = score

self.fitness_scores = fitness_scores
        logger.info(f"评估完成！{len(alphas)} 个Alpha已处理")
        return fitness_scores

def _evaluate_fitness_single_sim(self, to_simulate: List[str], max_w: int) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """单 alpha 并发评估路径。"""
        fitness_scores = {}
        completed = 0
        total = len(to_simulate)

sem = Semaphore(max_w)

def _run_one(alpha, idx):
            sem.acquire()
            try:
                return self._submit_and_wait_for_simulation(alpha, idx, total)
            finally:
                sem.release()

with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_w) as executor:
            futures = {}
            for idx, alpha in enumerate(to_simulate):
                future = executor.submit(_run_one, alpha, idx + 1)
                futures[future] = alpha

for future in as_completed(futures):
                if self.gui_ref and not self.gui_ref.running:
                    for f in futures:
                        f.cancel()
                    executor.shutdown(wait=False)
                    logger.info("  检测到停止信号，中断评估")
                    break
                alpha = futures[future]
                try:
                    score = future.result()
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"  获取结果异常: {e}")
                    score = 0.0

fitness_scores[alpha] = score
                completed += 1
                self._log_evaluation_progress(completed, total, max_w)

return fitness_scores

def _evaluate_fitness_multi_sim(
        self,
        to_simulate: List[str],
        concurrency: int,
        alpha_count_per_slot: int,
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """multi-sim 批量评估路径。"""
        fitness_scores = {}
        total = len(to_simulate)
        completed = 0
        tasks = [
            to_simulate[i:i + alpha_count_per_slot]
            for i in range(0, total, alpha_count_per_slot)
        ]

logger.info(
            f"  multi-sim 分批完成：共 {len(tasks)} 槽，批并发={concurrency}，每槽最多 {alpha_count_per_slot} 个Alpha"
        )

sem = Semaphore(concurrency)

def _run_batch(batch_alphas, slot_idx):
            sem.acquire()
            try:
                return slot_idx, self._submit_and_wait_for_multi_simulation(batch_alphas, slot_idx, len(tasks))
            finally:
                sem.release()

with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=concurrency) as executor:
            futures = {}
            for slot_idx, batch_alphas in enumerate(tasks, start=1):
                future = executor.submit(_run_batch, batch_alphas, slot_idx)
                futures[future] = batch_alphas

for future in as_completed(futures):
                if self.gui_ref and not self.gui_ref.running:
                    for f in futures:
                        f.cancel()
                    executor.shutdown(wait=False)
                    logger.info("  检测到停止信号，中断 multi-sim 评估")
                    break

batch_alphas = futures[future]
                try:
                    slot_idx, batch_scores = future.result()
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"  multi-sim 槽结果异常: {e}")
                    slot_idx, batch_scores = 0, {alpha: 0.0 for alpha in batch_alphas}

for alpha in batch_alphas:
                    fitness_scores[alpha] = batch_scores.get(alpha, 0.0)
                completed += len(batch_alphas)
                if slot_idx:
                    logger.info(f"  multi-sim 槽进度: {slot_idx}/{len(tasks)}，已完成 alpha {completed}/{total}")
                self._log_evaluation_progress(completed, total, concurrency)

return fitness_scores

def _log_evaluation_progress(self, completed: int, total: int, log_step: int):
        if completed % max(1, log_step) == 0 or completed == total:
            logger.info(f"  进度: {completed}/{total} 完成")
        if self.gui_ref and hasattr(self.gui_ref, 'update_progress'):
            self.gui_ref.update_progress(
                gen=self.generation,
                total_gens=self.config.get('num_generations') or 0,
                done=completed,
                total=total
            )

@staticmethod
    def _calculate_fitness_score(is_data: dict) -> float:
        sharpe = float(is_data.get('sharpe', 0) or 0)
        fitness = float(is_data.get('fitness', 0) or 0)
        turnover = float(is_data.get('turnover', 0) or 0)
        returns_val = float(is_data.get('returns', 0) or 0)
        drawdown = float(is_data.get('drawdown', 0) or 0)

sharpe_score = sharpe * 0.4
        fitness_score = fitness * 0.3
        turnover_penalty = max(0, (turnover - 0.7)) * 0.5 if turnover > 0 else 0
        returns_score = returns_val * 0.15
        drawdown_penalty = abs(drawdown) * 0.15
        return sharpe_score + fitness_score + returns_score - turnover_penalty - drawdown_penalty

def _fetch_alpha_is_details(self, alpha_id: str) -> Optional[dict]:
        """拉取单个 alpha 的 IS 指标。"""
        for _ in range(36):
            try:
                alpha_url = f"{brain_api_url}/alphas/{alpha_id}"
                alpha_resp = self.session.get(alpha_url, timeout=10)

if 'Retry-After' in alpha_resp.headers:
                    time.sleep(min(float(alpha_resp.headers['Retry-After']), 10))
                    continue

if alpha_resp.status_code == 200:
                    alpha_data = alpha_resp.json()
                    is_data = alpha_data.get('is', {})
                    if is_data and is_data.get('fitness') is not None:
                        return {
                            'sharpe': float(is_data.get('sharpe', 0) or 0),
                            'fitness': float(is_data.get('fitness', 0) or 0),
                            'turnover': float(is_data.get('turnover', 0) or 0),
                            'returns': float(is_data.get('returns', 0) or 0),
                            'drawdown': float(is_data.get('drawdown', 0) or 0),
                        }

elif alpha_resp.status_code == 401:
                    logger.warning("  IS数据获取401，正在重新登录...")
                    self._ensure_session()
                    time.sleep(2)
                    continue
            except Exception:
                pass

time.sleep(5)

return None

def _submit_and_wait_for_multi_simulation(
        self,
        alphas: List[str],
        slot_idx: int,
        total_slots: int,
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """提交一个 multi-sim 槽并等待所有 alpha 结果。"""
        scores = {alpha: 0.0 for alpha in alphas}
        max_retries = 3

if not self._check_stop_and_wait_if_paused():
            logger.info(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] 检测到停止信号，终止该槽提交")
            return scores

if len(alphas) < 2:
            alpha = alphas[0]
            scores[alpha] = self._submit_and_wait_for_simulation(alpha, slot_idx, total_slots)
            return scores

for attempt in range(max_retries):
            try:
                task_handle, status_code = self.concurrent_manager.submit_multi_simulation(alphas, self.config)
                if status_code == 201 and task_handle:
                    logger.info(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] 已提交 multi-sim，包含 {len(alphas)} 个Alpha")
                    results = wait_multi_simulation_task(task_handle)
                    self.session = task_handle.get('session', self.session)
                    self.concurrent_manager.session = self.session
                    return self._process_multi_sim_results(alphas, results, slot_idx, total_slots)

if status_code in (401,):
                    logger.warning(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] multi-sim 会话过期，正在重新登录...")
                    self._ensure_session()
                    time.sleep(2)
                    continue

if status_code in (429, 0):
                    wait = 5 * (attempt + 1)
                    logger.info(
                        f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] multi-sim 提交受限(状态{status_code})，{wait}秒后重试({attempt + 1}/{max_retries})"
                    )
                    time.sleep(wait)
                    continue

logger.warning(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] multi-sim 提交失败(状态{status_code})")
                return scores
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] multi-sim 异常: {e}")
                if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                    time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))
                    continue
                return scores

logger.warning(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] multi-sim 重试仍失败")
        return scores

def _process_multi_sim_results(
        self,
        alphas: List[str],
        results: List[Dict],
        slot_idx: int,
        total_slots: int,
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """处理 wait_multi_simulation_task 返回的结果并转换为适应度。"""
        scores = {alpha: 0.0 for alpha in alphas}
        result_map = {}

for item in results or []:
            simulate_data = item.get('simulate_data') or {}
            alpha_expr = simulate_data.get('regular')
            if alpha_expr and alpha_expr not in result_map:
                result_map[alpha_expr] = item

for alpha_expr in alphas:
            item = result_map.get(alpha_expr)
            if not item:
                logger.warning(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] 未返回结果: {alpha_expr[:50]}...")
                continue

alpha_id = item.get('alpha_id')
            if not alpha_id:
                logger.warning(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] alpha_id 为空: {alpha_expr[:50]}...")
                continue

is_details = self._fetch_alpha_is_details(alpha_id)
            if not is_details:
                logger.warning(f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] IS数据获取超时: {alpha_id}")
                continue

self.is_details_cache[alpha_id] = is_details
            self.alpha_id_map[alpha_expr] = alpha_id
            score = self._calculate_fitness_score(is_details)
            scores[alpha_expr] = score

logger.info(
                f"  [槽{slot_idx}/{total_slots}] 完成 alpha_id={alpha_id} - "
                f"Sharpe:{is_details['sharpe']:.4f} Fitness:{is_details['fitness']:.4f} "
                f"Turnover:{is_details['turnover']:.4f} Returns:{is_details['returns']:.4f} "
                f"Drawdown:{is_details['drawdown']:.4f} => Score:{score:.4f}"
            )
            self._set_alpha_name(alpha_id, self.generation, 0, 'GA')

return scores
```

### 3. ace_lib.py

```python
def submit_multi_simulation_task(
    s: SingleSession,
    simulate_data_list: list,
) -> dict:
    s = check_session_and_relogin(s)
    max_submit_retries = 5
    last_response = None
    last_error = None

for attempt in range(max_submit_retries):
        try:
            response = start_simulation(s, simulate_data_list)
            last_response = response

if response.status_code == 401:
                logger.warning(f"多因子任务提交遇到 401，第{attempt + 1}次尝试重新登录")
                s = check_session_and_relogin(s)
                time.sleep(min(2 * (attempt + 1), 10))
                continue

if response.status_code == 429:
                retry_after = response.headers.get("Retry-After", 5)
                try:
                    retry_after = float(retry_after)
                except (TypeError, ValueError):
                    retry_after = 5.0
                backoff = min(retry_after * (2 ** attempt), 60)
                logger.warning(f"多因子任务提交触发 429，第{attempt + 1}次重试，等待 {backoff:.1f} 秒")
                time.sleep(backoff)
                continue

if response.status_code in [400, 403] and "CONCURRENT_SIMULATION_LIMIT_EXCEEDED" in response.text:
                backoff = min(10 * (attempt + 1), 60)
                logger.warning(
                    f"多因子任务提交触发并发限制，第{attempt + 1}次重试，等待 {backoff:.1f} 秒"
                )
                time.sleep(backoff)
                continue

if response.status_code >= 500:
                backoff = min(3 * (2 ** attempt), 30)
                logger.warning(
                    f"多因子任务提交遇到服务器错误 {response.status_code}，第{attempt + 1}次重试，等待 {backoff:.1f} 秒"
                )
                time.sleep(backoff)
                continue

return {
                "session": s,
                "simulate_response": response,
                "simulate_data_list": simulate_data_list,
                "submit_attempts": attempt + 1,
                "submit_error": None,
                "max_wait_seconds": 1800,
                "poll_retry_limit": 3,
            }
        except Exception as exc:
            last_error = exc
            backoff = min(3 * (2 ** attempt), 30)
            logger.warning(f"多因子任务提交异常，第{attempt + 1}次重试：{exc}，等待 {backoff:.1f} 秒")
            time.sleep(backoff)

if last_response is None:
        try:
            last_response = requests.Response()
            last_response.status_code = 599
            last_response._content = str(last_error or "submit_multi_simulation_task failed").encode("utf-8", errors="replace")
        except Exception:
            pass

return {
        "session": s,
        "simulate_response": last_response,
        "simulate_data_list": simulate_data_list,
        "submit_attempts": max_submit_retries,
        "submit_error": str(last_error) if last_error else None,
        "max_wait_seconds": 1800,
        "poll_retry_limit": 3,
    }
```

```python
def wait_multi_simulation_task(task_handle: dict) -> list[dict]:
    s = task_handle["session"]
    simulate_response = task_handle["simulate_response"]
    simulate_data_list = task_handle["simulate_data_list"]
    max_wait_seconds = int(task_handle.get("max_wait_seconds", 1800))
    poll_retry_limit = int(task_handle.get("poll_retry_limit", 3))

def build_failed_result() -> list[dict]:
        return [{"alpha_id": None, "simulate_data": x} for x in simulate_data_list]

if simulate_response.status_code // 100 != 2:
        logger.warning(f"多因子任务提交失败：{simulate_response.text}，状态码：{simulate_response.status_code}")
        return build_failed_result()

simulation_progress_url = simulate_response.headers.get("Location")
    if not simulation_progress_url:
        logger.warning("多因子任务缺少 Location 响应头，无法继续轮询")
        return build_failed_result()

start_time = time.monotonic()
    retry_count = 0
    last_payload = {}

while time.monotonic() - start_time < max_wait_seconds:
        try:
            simulation_progress_response = s.get(simulation_progress_url, timeout=(15, 60))
        except Exception as exc:
            retry_count += 1
            if retry_count > poll_retry_limit:
                logger.error(f"多因子任务轮询异常次数过多，终止等待：{exc}")
                return build_failed_result()
            sleep_seconds = min(5 * retry_count, 20)
            logger.warning(f"多因子任务轮询异常，第{retry_count}次重试：{exc}")
            time.sleep(sleep_seconds)
            continue

if simulation_progress_response.status_code == 401:
            retry_count += 1
            if retry_count > poll_retry_limit:
                logger.error("多因子任务轮询遇到 401 次数过多，终止等待")
                return build_failed_result()
            logger.warning(f"多因子任务轮询遇到 401，第{retry_count}次尝试重新登录")
            try:
                s = check_session_and_relogin(s)
                task_handle["session"] = s
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error(f"多因子任务重新登录失败：{exc}")
                return build_failed_result()
            time.sleep(min(2 * retry_count, 10))
            continue

if simulation_progress_response.status_code // 100 != 2:
            retry_count += 1
            if retry_count > poll_retry_limit:
                logger.error(
                    f"多因子任务轮询失败，状态码：{simulation_progress_response.status_code}，超过重试上限"
                )
                return build_failed_result()
            sleep_seconds = min(10 * retry_count, 30)
            logger.warning(
                f"多因子任务轮询返回状态码：{simulation_progress_response.status_code}，第{retry_count}次重试"
            )
            time.sleep(sleep_seconds)
            continue

try:
            payload = simulation_progress_response.json()
        except Exception as exc:
            retry_count += 1
            if retry_count > poll_retry_limit:
                logger.error(f"多因子任务轮询结果解析失败次数过多：{exc}")
                return build_failed_result()
            logger.warning(f"多因子任务轮询结果解析失败，第{retry_count}次重试：{exc}")
            time.sleep(min(3 * retry_count, 10))
            continue

last_payload = payload
        retry_count = 0

retry_after_value = simulation_progress_response.headers.get("Retry-After", 0)
        try:
            retry_after_value = float(retry_after_value)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            retry_after_value = 5.0

if retry_after_value > 0:
            remaining_time = max_wait_seconds - (time.monotonic() - start_time)
            if remaining_time <= 0:
                break
            time.sleep(min(retry_after_value, 30, remaining_time))
            continue

status = payload.get("status", "")
        children = payload.get("children") or []

if status in {"ERROR", "FAIL", "FAILED"}:
            if not children:
                logger.error(f"多因子任务失败：{payload}")
                return build_failed_result()
            for child in children:
                try:
                    child_progress = s.get(brain_api_url + "/simulations/" + child, timeout=(15, 60))
                    logger.error(f"多因子子任务失败：{child_progress.json()}")
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.error(f"读取失败的多因子子任务 {child} 详情时出错：{exc}")
            return build_failed_result()

if len(children) == 0:
            logger.warning(f"多因子任务未返回子任务结果：{payload}")
            return build_failed_result()

children_list = []
        for child in children:
            child_alpha = 0
            for child_attempt in range(poll_retry_limit + 1):
                try:
                    child_progress = s.get(brain_api_url + "/simulations/" + child, timeout=(15, 60))
                    if child_progress.status_code == 401:
                        s = check_session_and_relogin(s)
                        task_handle["session"] = s
                        time.sleep(min(2 * (child_attempt + 1), 10))
                        continue
                    if child_progress.status_code // 100 != 2:
                        time.sleep(min(3 * (child_attempt + 1), 10))
                        continue
                    child_payload = child_progress.json()
                    child_alpha = child_payload.get("alpha", 0)
                    if child_alpha != 0:
                        break
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.warning(f"读取多因子子任务 {child} 失败，第{child_attempt + 1}次重试：{exc}")
                    time.sleep(min(3 * (child_attempt + 1), 10))

if child_alpha == 0:
                logger.warning(f"多因子子任务 {child} 未返回 alpha_id，终止本次等待")
                return build_failed_result()

child_result = get_simulation_result_json(s, child_alpha)
            if len(child_result) == 0:
                logger.warning(f"多因子子任务 {child} 的 alpha {child_alpha} 结果为空")
                return build_failed_result()
            children_list.append(child_result)

return [
            {
                "alpha_id": x["id"],
                "simulate_data": {
                    "type": x["type"],
                    "settings": x["settings"],
                    "regular": x["regular"]["code"],
                },
            }
            for x in children_list
        ]

logger.warning(f"多因子任务等待超时（>{max_wait_seconds}秒）：{simulation_progress_url}，最后状态：{last_payload}")
    return build_failed_result()
```

```python
def simulate_alpha_list_multi(
    s: SingleSession,
    alpha_list: list,
    limit_of_concurrent_simulations: int = 3,
    limit_of_multi_simulations: int = 10,
    simulation_config: dict = DEFAULT_CONFIG,
) -> list:
    """
    Simulate a list of alphas using multi-simulation when possible.

Args:
        s (SingleSession): An authenticated session object.
        alpha_list (list): A list of alpha configurations to simulate.
        limit_of_concurrent_simulations (int, optional): The maximum number of concurrent simulation batches. Defaults to 3.
        limit_of_multi_simulations (int, optional): The maximum number of alphas in a multi-simulation. Defaults to 3.
        simulation_config (dict, optional): Configuration for the simulation. Defaults to DEFAULT_CONFIG.

Returns:
        list: A list of dictionaries containing simulation results for each alpha.

Raises:
        requests.exceptions.RequestException: If there's an error in the API requests.
    """
    if (limit_of_multi_simulations < 2) or (limit_of_multi_simulations > 10):
        logger.warning("Limit of multi-simulation should be 2..10, will be set to 10")
        limit_of_multi_simulations = 10
    if (limit_of_concurrent_simulations < 1) or (limit_of_concurrent_simulations > 8):
        logger.warning("Limit of concurrent simulation should be 1..8, will be set to 3")
        limit_of_concurrent_simulations = 3
    if any(d["type"] == "SUPER" for d in alpha_list):
        logger.warning("Multi-Simulation is not supported for SuperAlphas, single concurrent simulations will be used")
        return simulate_alpha_list(
            s,
            alpha_list,
            limit_of_concurrent_simulations=3,
            simulation_config=simulation_config,
        )

tasks = [
        alpha_list[i : i + limit_of_multi_simulations] for i in range(0, len(alpha_list), limit_of_multi_simulations)
    ]
    if not tasks:
        return []

task_queue = queue.Queue()
    result_queue = queue.Queue()
    for task in tasks:
        task_queue.put(task)

def worker():
        while True:
            try:
                task = task_queue.get_nowait()
            except queue.Empty:
                break

try:
                if len(task) == 1:
                    result = [simulate_single_alpha(s, task[0])]
                else:
                    task_handle = submit_multi_simulation_task(s, task)
                    result = wait_multi_simulation_task(task_handle)
            except Exception:
                logger.exception("Multi-simulation task failed")
                result = [{"alpha_id": None, "simulate_data": x} for x in task]
            finally:
                task_queue.task_done()

result_queue.put(result)

thread_count = min(limit_of_concurrent_simulations, len(tasks))
    threads = [threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True) for _ in range(thread_count)]
    for thread in threads:
        thread.start()

result_list = []
    with tqdm.tqdm(total=len(tasks)) as pbar:
        for _ in range(len(tasks)):
            result = result_queue.get()
            result_list.append(result)
            pbar.update()

for thread in threads:
        thread.join()

result_list_flat = [item for sublist in result_list for item in sublist]

stats_list_result = []

def func(x):
        return get_specified_alpha_stats(s, x["alpha_id"], x["simulate_data"], **simulation_config)

with ThreadPool(3) as pool:
        for result in pool.map(func, result_list_flat):
            stats_list_result.append(result)

return _delete_duplicates_from_result(stats_list_result)
```

## 五、结论

这套 `8x10` 并发机制的核心逻辑就是：

- GUI 默认给出 `8` 和 `10`
- 运行时在 `Alpha挖掘生态链.py` 中做边界限制
- 再把 Alpha 按每槽 `10` 个切分
- 用线程池最多同时跑 `8` 个槽
- 每个槽通过 `ace_lib.py` 提交 multi-sim
- 最后轮询拿回每个子任务的 alpha_id 和结果

如果你后面要，我还可以继续给你补一版：

- “按调用顺序画流程图的说明版”
- “只保留主流程、去掉无关日志的精简版”

---

### 评论 #18 (作者: ZZ44620, 时间: 4个月前)

用 LLM 生成表达式时，模型不知道这些类型约束。最重要的问题是，它经常把 VECTOR 字段直接塞进operator中，导致错误，反复试错成本极高。我目前使用的是提示词预防+数据字段语法检验拦截。

## 数据类型背景

WorldQuant BRAIN 平台的 Alpha 表达式是一个函数式 DSL，数据字段有三种类型，用法完全不同：

类型    维度                  说明                  使用规则
MATRIX  date × instrument     每日每标的1个数值     可直接使用
VECTOR  date × instrument × N 每日每标的N个数值     需先用vec_*降级为MATRIX类型
GROUP   标签型                分类标签，无法运算     仅可作为group_*分组参数使用

转换路径：

```
VECTOR ──vec_avg / vec_sum / vec_max──▶ MATRIX ──rank / ts_zscore──▶ 信号GROUP  ───────────────────────────────▶ group_neutralize(returns, sector) ▶ 信号
```

(matrix to group没说)

## 把类型约束写进提示词

类似RULES，构造提示词时，一开始就把数据字段按类型分类，每类标注使用规则，配上算子白名单和严格输出格式，以结构化 JSON 载荷交给 LLM。

代码分三步：

**Step 1 — System Prompt**  在角色定义里直接写死类型规则：MATRIX 直接用、VECTOR 必须 vec_* 降维、GROUP 只能做分组参数。同时约束"只用给定算子、只用给定字段、输出严格 JSON"——在 LLM 最底层的行为指令里就把类型系统植入进去。

**Step 2 — 构造 datafields 文本**  `_classify_fields()` 先把所有字段按 `type` 拆成 MATRIX / VECTOR / GROUP 三组，然后 `_format_datafields_text()` 给每组打上不同的使用说明标签：

- MATRIX 组标注 `(use directly)`
- VECTOR 组标注 `(MUST reduce with a vec_* op before use)`，并附上 7 个可选 vec_* 算子名
- GROUP 组在辅助阶段标注 `(use in group_* ops)`，(但group字段在一般数据字段中并不常见，所以没有对其专门构造提示词)

**Step 3 — 构建 JSON 载荷**  `build_main_payload()` 把分类好的 datafields 文本 + 算子白名单（definition + description）+ 输出 schema 搓成一个结构化 JSON。模型收到的是一个类型完备的、自解释的上下文，不需要自己推断任何规则。

```
from textwrap import dedentfrom typing import Anyimport json# ── System Prompt: type rules baked in ──MAIN_SYSTEM = dedent("""\    Role: Generate simple, economically interpretable alpha expressions.    Background:    - Fast Expression is a function-based DSL producing one value per instrument per day.    - The final expression must output a MATRIX (one value per date x instrument).    - MATRIX fields can be used directly in expressions.    - VECTOR fields contain multiple values per date x instrument. They MUST be reduced      to MATRIX first using a vec_* operator (vec_avg, vec_sum, vec_max, vec_min,      vec_stddev, vec_count, vec_range). Choose the vec_* op that best fits the      economic meaning of your signal.    Constraints (STRICT):    - Use ONLY operators from the provided operator list.    - Use ONLY datafields from the provided datafield list.    - Syntax: function(arg1, arg2, ...) or arithmetic + - * /.  Named args: param=value.    - Keep expressions SHORT: ideally 1-3 operators, at most 4.    - Do NOT chain many operators or repeat the same pattern with minor parameter tweaks.    Quality goals:    - Every expression MUST have a clear economic rationale.    - Prioritize under-explored intuitions: second-order corporate behavior effects,      distributional shape of revisions, balance-sheet slack, timing frictions.    Output contract:    - Return ONLY a valid JSON array. Each element is a single-key object:      the KEY is the expression, the VALUE is a brief economic idea.    - Example: [{"divide(fieldA, fieldB)": "profitability vs leverage"}, ...]    - No markdown, no code fences, no extra text.""")# ── Field classification: split by type, not by dataset ──def _classify_fields(    fields: list[dict[str, Any]],) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict], list[dict]]:    """Split fields into (matrix_fields, vector_fields, group_fields) by type."""    matrix_fields, vector_fields, group_fields = [], [], []    for f in fields:        ftype = (f.get("type") or f.get("dataType") or "MATRIX").upper()        entry = {"id": f.get("id", ""), "description": f.get("description", "")}        if ftype == "VECTOR":            vector_fields.append(entry)        elif ftype == "GROUP":            group_fields.append(entry)        else:            matrix_fields.append(entry)    return matrix_fields, vector_fields, group_fields# ── Format datafields with usage rules per type ──def _format_datafields_text(    matrix_fields: list[dict],    vector_fields: list[dict],) -> str:    """Format datafields text — MATRIX fields say 'use directly',    VECTOR fields say 'MUST reduce with vec_* first'."""    lines: list[str] = []    if matrix_fields:        lines.append("MATRIX datafields (use directly):")        for df in matrix_fields:            lines.append(f"  {df['id']}  —  {df['description']}")    if vector_fields:        if lines:            lines.append("")        lines.append("VECTOR datafields (MUST reduce with a vec_* op before use):")        for df in vector_fields:            lines.append(f"  {df['id']}  —  {df['description']}")        lines.append("")        lines.append("  vec_* operators: vec_avg, vec_sum, vec_max, vec_min,")        lines.append("    vec_stddev, vec_count, vec_range")        lines.append("  Choose the vec_* op that best fits the economic meaning.")    return "\n".join(lines)# ── Build the structured JSON payload for the LLM ──def build_main_payload(    fields: list[dict[str, Any]],    operators: list[dict[str, Any]],) -> dict[str, Any]:    """Build a structured JSON user message.    The payload separates fields by type and includes an operator whitelist.    This gives the LLM a complete "type-aware" context for generation.    """    matrix_fields, vector_fields, _ = _classify_fields(fields)    datafields_text = _format_datafields_text(matrix_fields, vector_fields)    ops_payload = [        {"definition": op.get("definition", "").strip(),         "description": op.get("description", "").strip()}        for op in operators    ]    return {        "task": "Generate 15-25 simple, economically meaningful alpha expressions",        "operators": ops_payload,        "datafields": datafields_text,        "output_schema": [{"<expression>": "<brief economic idea>"}],    }# ── Usage example ──if __name__ == "__main__":    # Use datafields from API    raw_fields = [        {"id": "close",           "description": "Daily closing price",        "type": "MATRIX"},        {"id": "oth384_qa_tone",  "description": "NLP tone scores for Q&A",    "type": "VECTOR"},        {"id": "sector",          "description": "Sector classification",      "type": "GROUP"},    ]    # Operator whitelist — examples    valid_operators = [        {"definition": "add(x, y, filter = false), x + y",         "description": "Adds two or more inputs element wise."},        {"definition": "vec_min(x)",         "description": "Minimum value from vector field x."},        ...    ]    payload = build_main_payload(raw_fields, valid_operators)    # This is what gets sent to the LLM    system_prompt = MAIN_SYSTEM    user_prompt = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)    print("=== SYSTEM PROMPT ===\n")    print(system_prompt)    print("\n=== USER PAYLOAD ===\n")    print(user_prompt)
```

## 语法检验

结构化提示词能从源头大幅降低错误率，但 LLM 毕竟不是编译器，偶尔还是会出错。所以在表达式被提交回测之前，再接一个能检查数据字段**语法检验器**做最后把关。语法检验代码比较长，我放在了最后

检验器内部是一个完整的 mini 编译器：

1.  **词法分析（Tokenizer）**  — 把表达式字符串拆成 token（数字、标识符、运算符、括号等）
2.  **语法分析（Parser）** — 构建 AST，支持函数调用 `rank(close, rate=2)`、算术运算 `A + B`、变量赋值 `$x = A; rank($x)` 等完整语法
3.  **语义分析（SemanticAnalyzer）** — 核心。拿到 AST 后，基于操作符规格（OperatorSpec）和字段类型做类型检查：

-  **VECTOR 直接误用** ：`rank(oth384_qa_tone)` → 报错 "VECTOR must be converted using vec_* operators first"，必须写成 `rank(vec_avg(oth384_qa_tone))`
   -  **GROUP 滥用** ：`close + sector` → 报错 "GROUP type cannot be used in binary operations"
   -  **未知算子** ：`power(close, 2)` → 报错 "Unknown operator: 'power'"，应该用 `signed_power`
   -  **参数类型不匹配** ：`group_neutralize(close, close)` → 报错第二个参数需要 GROUP 类型
   -  **操作符数量超限** ：超过 64 个操作符时报错
   -  **特殊规则** ：bucket 的 range 格式、rank 的 rate 只能为 0 或 2、ts_backfill 的参数互斥规则等

用法很简洁，和提示词共用字段记录

```
from expression_validator import validate_expression_with_fieldsraw_fields = [    {"id": "close",           "type": "MATRIX"},    {"id": "oth384_qa_tone",  "type": "VECTOR"},    {"id": "sector",          "type": "GROUP"},    ...]is_valid, errors = validate_expression_with_fields(    expression="rank(vec_avg(oth384_qa_tone))",    field_records=raw_fields,    allowed_operators={"rank", "vec_avg", "ts_zscore", ...}  # 与提示词的算子白名单一致)if not is_valid:    for err in errors:        print(f"  - {err}")
```

## 实测效果

使用模型：deepseek-v4-flash

数据集来自数据库的缓存，我先把wq数据集都存到了本地sqlite中避免获取数据的出错

数据集选择
- batch_loop-IND.log：IND / TOP500 / delay=1
  每轮从16个数据集随机选取analyst、fundamental类别各1个，单轮共2个，自动去重
  字段数量范围：12 ~ 546

- batch_loop-EUR.log：EUR / TOP2500 / delay=1
  每轮从15个数据集随机选取pv、risk类别各1个，部分轮次仅选取pv
  字段数量范围：15 ~ 1649

总结统计
日志                          区域   样本池        数据类别          轮数   合法数   总数     成功率
batch_loop-IND.log   IND     TOP500    analyst+fund     40     5975      6096    98.0%
batch_loop-EUR.log  EUR   TOP2500  pv+risk              22     2957      3336    88.6%
合计                —     —        —                                        62      8932      9432   94.7%

结果分析
1. IND/TOP500数据集成功率区间95%~100%，单轮最低89.0%
   分析师、基本面数据集字段规范，模型识别度高，提示约束效果良好

2. EUR/TOP2500整体成功率88.6%，多轮低成功率拉低整体水平

异常轮次明细
轮次  数据集  字段数  合法/总数  成功率
7     pv29     80      23/147    15.6%
9     pv20     1200    97/148    65.5%
11    pv173    81      107/155   69.0%
17    pv20     1200    115/154   74.7%
18    pv173    81      99/149    66.4%

我猜测失败的原因是，1.pv29都是group字段，我没对这个规则写到prompt中，因为觉得对大部分数据集该提示词比较“冗余”， 2. 数据集字段是庞大，比如pv20，因为我构造提示词中，数据字段是全提供的。 pv173我没分析到是什么原因，这个数据集全是vector。总体可行，另外这有一个好处，只需维护一段提示词和语法检验脚本即可，无需为了不同数据类型做代码层面的额外处理，比如先生成，然后检验字段类型，自动套vec_op；而且ai有时候会用不同的vec_op，根据他自己想的idea.

## 语法检验器

根据的是RM49262的  [本地表达式语法检测程序的初步尝试](../引用的关联帖L2/[L2] 本地表达式语法检测程序的初步尝试代码优化_36310472781463.md) 魔改， 表达式解析出数据字段参考的文姐的 [用python-ast提取表达式中operator和datafield的方法](../引用的关联帖L2/[L2] 用python ast提取表达式中operator和datafield的方法代码优化_30870358077463.md)

使用时需要提供相应格式的数据字段，以及可以根据自身等级权限提供部分operators

```
"""Alpha表达式验证器用于验证WorldQuant BRAIN alpha表达式的语法和语义正确性by RM49262"""from enum import Enumfrom typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional, Any, Callablefrom dataclasses import dataclass# ============================================================================# 第一部分：词法分析器（Tokenizer）# ============================================================================class TokenType(Enum):    """Token类型枚举"""    # 字面量    NUMBER = "NUMBER"           # 123, 0.5, 1.23    STRING = "STRING"           # "gaussian", "NAN"    BOOL = "BOOL"              # true, false    NAN = "NAN"                # nan    # 标识符    IDENTIFIER = "IDENTIFIER"   # close, open, rank, my_var    # 运算符    PLUS = "PLUS"              # +    MINUS = "MINUS"            # -    MULTIPLY = "MULTIPLY"      # *    DIVIDE = "DIVIDE"          # /    LESS = "LESS"              # <    LESS_EQUAL = "LESS_EQUAL"  # <=    GREATER = "GREATER"        # >    GREATER_EQUAL = "GREATER_EQUAL"  # >=    EQUAL = "EQUAL"            # ==    NOT_EQUAL = "NOT_EQUAL"    # !=    # 分隔符    LPAREN = "LPAREN"          # (    RPAREN = "RPAREN"          # )    COMMA = "COMMA"            # ,    SEMICOLON = "SEMICOLON"    # ;    ASSIGN = "ASSIGN"          # =    # 特殊    EOF = "EOF"                # 结束符@dataclassclass Token:    """Token数据类"""    type: TokenType    value: Any    position: int = 0class Tokenizer:    """词法分析器"""    def __init__(self, expression: str):        self.expression = expression        self.pos = 0        self.current_char = expression[0] if expression else None    def error(self, msg: str):        """抛出词法错误"""        raise SyntaxError(f"Lexical error at position {self.pos}: {msg}")    def advance(self):        """前进到下一个字符"""        self.pos += 1        if self.pos < len(self.expression):            self.current_char = self.expression[self.pos]        else:            self.current_char = None    def peek(self, offset: int = 1) -> Optional[str]:        """向前查看字符，不移动位置"""        peek_pos = self.pos + offset        if peek_pos < len(self.expression):            return self.expression[peek_pos]        return None    def skip_whitespace(self):        """跳过空白字符"""        while self.current_char is not None and self.current_char.isspace():            self.advance()    def read_number(self) -> Token:        """读取数字（整数或浮点数）"""        start_pos = self.pos        num_str = ''        while self.current_char is not None and (self.current_char.isdigit() or self.current_char == '.'):            num_str += self.current_char            self.advance()        try:            value = float(num_str) if '.' in num_str else int(num_str)            return Token(TokenType.NUMBER, value, start_pos)        except ValueError:            self.error(f"Invalid number: {num_str}")    def read_identifier(self) -> Token:        """读取标识符或关键字"""        start_pos = self.pos        ident = ''        # 标识符可以包含字母、数字、下划线        while self.current_char is not None and (self.current_char.isalnum() or self.current_char == '_'):            ident += self.current_char            self.advance()        # 检查是否是关键字（不区分大小写）        ident_lower = ident.lower()        if ident_lower == 'true':            return Token(TokenType.BOOL, True, start_pos)        elif ident_lower == 'false':            return Token(TokenType.BOOL, False, start_pos)        elif ident_lower == 'nan':            return Token(TokenType.NAN, float('nan'), start_pos)        else:            return Token(TokenType.IDENTIFIER, ident, start_pos)    def read_string(self, quote_char: str) -> Token:        """读取字符串（支持单引号和双引号）        Args:            quote_char: 引号字符（' 或 "）        """        start_pos = self.pos        self.advance()  # 跳过开始的引号        string_val = ''        while self.current_char is not None and self.current_char != quote_char:            string_val += self.current_char            self.advance()        if self.current_char != quote_char:            self.error(f"Unterminated string (expected closing {quote_char})")        self.advance()  # 跳过结束的引号        return Token(TokenType.STRING, string_val, start_pos)    def tokenize(self) -> List[Token]:        """将表达式转换为token列表"""        tokens = []        while self.current_char is not None:            # 跳过空白字符            if self.current_char.isspace():                self.skip_whitespace()                continue            # 数字（包括负数）            # 检测负数：如果是'-'后跟数字，且前面是开始/左括号/逗号，则读取为负数            if self.current_char == '-':                next_char = self.peek()                if next_char and (next_char.isdigit() or next_char == '.'):                    # 检查'-'前面的token，判断是否应该作为负号                    if not tokens or tokens[-1].type in [TokenType.LPAREN, TokenType.COMMA]:                        # 这是负数，不是减号                        self.advance()  # 跳过'-'                        num_token = self.read_number()                        # 将数字值设为负数                        num_token.value = -num_token.value                        tokens.append(num_token)                        continue            if self.current_char.isdigit() or (self.current_char == '.' and self.peek() and self.peek().isdigit()):                tokens.append(self.read_number())                continue            # 标识符或关键字            if self.current_char.isalpha() or self.current_char == '_':                tokens.append(self.read_identifier())                continue            # 字符串（支持单引号和双引号）            if self.current_char == '"':                tokens.append(self.read_string('"'))                continue            if self.current_char == "'":                tokens.append(self.read_string("'"))                continue            # 双字符运算符            if self.current_char == '<' and self.peek() == '=':                tokens.append(Token(TokenType.LESS_EQUAL, '<=', self.pos))                self.advance()                self.advance()                continue            if self.current_char == '>' and self.peek() == '=':                tokens.append(Token(TokenType.GREATER_EQUAL, '>=', self.pos))                self.advance()                self.advance()                continue            if self.current_char == '=' and self.peek() == '=':                tokens.append(Token(TokenType.EQUAL, '==', self.pos))                self.advance()                self.advance()                continue            if self.current_char == '!' and self.peek() == '=':                tokens.append(Token(TokenType.NOT_EQUAL, '!=', self.pos))                self.advance()                self.advance()                continue            # 单字符运算符和分隔符            char_map = {                '+': TokenType.PLUS,                '-': TokenType.MINUS,                '*': TokenType.MULTIPLY,                '/': TokenType.DIVIDE,                '<': TokenType.LESS,                '>': TokenType.GREATER,                '(': TokenType.LPAREN,                ')': TokenType.RPAREN,                ',': TokenType.COMMA,                ';': TokenType.SEMICOLON,                '=': TokenType.ASSIGN,            }            if self.current_char in char_map:                token_type = char_map[self.current_char]                tokens.append(Token(token_type, self.current_char, self.pos))                self.advance()                continue            # 未知字符            self.error(f"Unknown character: '{self.current_char}'")        tokens.append(Token(TokenType.EOF, None, self.pos))        return tokens# ============================================================================# 第二部分：抽象语法树（AST）节点# ============================================================================class ASTNode:    """AST节点基类"""    passclass NumberNode(ASTNode):    """数字节点"""    def __init__(self, value: float):        self.value = value    def __repr__(self):        return f"NumberNode({self.value})"class StringNode(ASTNode):    """字符串节点"""    def __init__(self, value: str):        self.value = value    def __repr__(self):        return f"StringNode('{self.value}')"class BoolNode(ASTNode):    """布尔节点"""    def __init__(self, value: bool):        self.value = value    def __repr__(self):        return f"BoolNode({self.value})"class NanNode(ASTNode):    """NaN节点"""    def __repr__(self):        return "NanNode()"class IdentifierNode(ASTNode):    """标识符节点（可能是数据字段或变量）"""    def __init__(self, name: str):        self.name = name    def __repr__(self):        return f"IdentifierNode('{self.name}')"class BinaryOpNode(ASTNode):    """二元运算符节点"""    def __init__(self, left: ASTNode, op: str, right: ASTNode):        self.left = left        self.op = op        self.right = right    def __repr__(self):        return f"BinaryOpNode({self.left} {self.op} {self.right})"class UnaryOpNode(ASTNode):    """一元运算符节点"""    def __init__(self, op: str, operand: ASTNode):        self.op = op        self.operand = operand    def __repr__(self):        return f"UnaryOpNode({self.op}{self.operand})"class FunctionCallNode(ASTNode):    """函数调用节点"""    def __init__(self, name: str, args: List[ASTNode], kwargs: Dict[str, ASTNode]):        self.name = name        self.args = args        self.kwargs = kwargs    def __repr__(self):        args_str = ', '.join(repr(arg) for arg in self.args)        kwargs_str = ', '.join(f"{k}={repr(v)}" for k, v in self.kwargs.items())        all_args = ', '.join(filter(None, [args_str, kwargs_str]))        return f"FunctionCallNode({self.name}({all_args}))"class AssignmentNode(ASTNode):    """赋值节点"""    def __init__(self, var_name: str, value: ASTNode):        self.var_name = var_name        self.value = value    def __repr__(self):        return f"AssignmentNode({self.var_name} = {self.value})"class ProgramNode(ASTNode):    """程序节点（整个表达式）"""    def __init__(self, statements: List[AssignmentNode], final_expr: ASTNode):        self.statements = statements        self.final_expr = final_expr    def __repr__(self):        stmts = '; '.join(repr(s) for s in self.statements)        return f"ProgramNode({stmts}; {self.final_expr})"# ============================================================================# 第三部分：语法分析器（Parser）# ============================================================================class Parser:    """语法分析器"""    def __init__(self, tokens: List[Token]):        self.tokens = tokens        self.pos = 0        self.current_token = tokens[0] if tokens else Token(TokenType.EOF, None)    def error(self, msg: str):        """抛出语法错误"""        raise SyntaxError(f"Syntax error at position {self.current_token.position}: {msg}")    def advance(self):        """前进到下一个token"""        self.pos += 1        if self.pos < len(self.tokens):            self.current_token = self.tokens[self.pos]        else:            self.current_token = Token(TokenType.EOF, None)    def peek(self, offset: int = 1) -> Token:        """向前查看token"""        peek_pos = self.pos + offset        if peek_pos < len(self.tokens):            return self.tokens[peek_pos]        return Token(TokenType.EOF, None)    def expect(self, token_type: TokenType):        """期望特定类型的token"""        if self.current_token.type != token_type:            self.error(f"Expected {token_type}, got {self.current_token.type}")        self.advance()    def is_assignment(self) -> bool:        """检查当前是否是赋值语句"""        if self.current_token.type == TokenType.IDENTIFIER:            next_token = self.peek()            return next_token.type == TokenType.ASSIGN        return False    def parse(self) -> ProgramNode:        """解析整个程序"""        statements = []        # 解析赋值语句        while self.current_token.type != TokenType.EOF and self.is_assignment():            stmt = self.parse_assignment()            statements.append(stmt)            # 赋值语句后必须跟分号            if self.current_token.type == TokenType.SEMICOLON:                self.advance()            else:                self.error("Expected ';' after assignment")        # 解析最终表达式        if self.current_token.type == TokenType.EOF:            self.error("Expected expression after assignments")        final_expr = self.parse_expression()        # 最终表达式后不能有分号        if self.current_token.type == TokenType.SEMICOLON:            self.error("Final expression cannot end with semicolon")        # 检查是否到达结尾        if self.current_token.type != TokenType.EOF:            self.error(f"Unexpected token: {self.current_token.type}")        return ProgramNode(statements, final_expr)    def parse_assignment(self) -> AssignmentNode:        """解析赋值语句"""        var_name = self.current_token.value        self.advance()        self.expect(TokenType.ASSIGN)        value = self.parse_expression()        return AssignmentNode(var_name, value)    def parse_expression(self) -> ASTNode:        """解析表达式（处理比较运算符）"""        return self.parse_comparison()    def parse_comparison(self) -> ASTNode:        """解析比较运算"""        left = self.parse_additive()        while self.current_token.type in [TokenType.LESS, TokenType.LESS_EQUAL,                                          TokenType.GREATER, TokenType.GREATER_EQUAL,                                          TokenType.EQUAL, TokenType.NOT_EQUAL]:            op_token = self.current_token            self.advance()            right = self.parse_additive()            left = BinaryOpNode(left, op_token.value, right)        return left    def parse_additive(self) -> ASTNode:        """解析加减运算"""        left = self.parse_multiplicative()        while self.current_token.type in [TokenType.PLUS, TokenType.MINUS]:            op_token = self.current_token            self.advance()            right = self.parse_multiplicative()            left = BinaryOpNode(left, op_token.value, right)        return left    def parse_multiplicative(self) -> ASTNode:        """解析乘除运算"""        left = self.parse_unary()        while self.current_token.type in [TokenType.MULTIPLY, TokenType.DIVIDE]:            op_token = self.current_token            self.advance()            right = self.parse_unary()            left = BinaryOpNode(left, op_token.value, right)        return left    def parse_unary(self) -> ASTNode:        """解析一元运算（如负号）"""        # 检查是否是一元负号        if self.current_token.type == TokenType.MINUS:            op_token = self.current_token            self.advance()            # 递归解析操作数            operand = self.parse_unary()            return UnaryOpNode(op_token.value, operand)        # 不是一元运算符，继续解析主表达式        return self.parse_primary()    def parse_primary(self) -> ASTNode:        """解析主表达式"""        token = self.current_token        # 数字        if token.type == TokenType.NUMBER:            self.advance()            return NumberNode(token.value)        # 字符串        if token.type == TokenType.STRING:            self.advance()            return StringNode(token.value)        # 布尔值        if token.type == TokenType.BOOL:            self.advance()            return BoolNode(token.value)        # NaN        if token.type == TokenType.NAN:            self.advance()            return NanNode()        # 标识符（可能是函数调用、数据字段或变量）        if token.type == TokenType.IDENTIFIER:            name = token.value            self.advance()            # 检查是否是函数调用            if self.current_token.type == TokenType.LPAREN:                return self.parse_function_call(name)            else:                # 否则是数据字段或变量引用                return IdentifierNode(name)        # 括号表达式        if token.type == TokenType.LPAREN:            self.advance()            expr = self.parse_expression()            self.expect(TokenType.RPAREN)            return expr        self.error(f"Unexpected token: {token.type}")    def parse_function_call(self, func_name: str) -> FunctionCallNode:        """解析函数调用"""        self.expect(TokenType.LPAREN)        args = []        kwargs = {}        # 解析参数        while self.current_token.type != TokenType.RPAREN:            # 检查是否是关键字参数            if self.current_token.type == TokenType.IDENTIFIER and self.peek().type == TokenType.ASSIGN:                key = self.current_token.value                self.advance()                self.expect(TokenType.ASSIGN)                value = self.parse_expression()                kwargs[key] = value            else:                # 位置参数                args.append(self.parse_expression())            # 检查是否有更多参数            if self.current_token.type == TokenType.COMMA:                self.advance()            elif self.current_token.type != TokenType.RPAREN:                self.error("Expected ',' or ')' in function call")        self.expect(TokenType.RPAREN)        return FunctionCallNode(func_name, args, kwargs)# ============================================================================# 第四部分：操作符规格系统# ============================================================================class ParamType(Enum):    """参数类型枚举"""    MATRIX = "MATRIX"    VECTOR = "VECTOR"    GROUP = "GROUP"    INT = "INT"    FLOAT = "FLOAT"    BOOL = "BOOL"    STRING = "STRING"    ANY = "ANY"  # 任意类型@dataclassclass ParamSpec:    """参数规格"""    name: str    param_type: ParamType    optional: bool = False    default_value: Any = None    value_constraint: Optional[Callable[[Any], bool]] = None    def validate_value(self, value: Any) -> Tuple[bool, str]:        """验证参数值"""        if self.value_constraint and not self.value_constraint(value):            return False, f"Value {value} does not meet constraint for parameter '{self.name}'"        return True, ""@dataclassclass OperatorSpec:    """操作符规格"""    name: str    positional_params: List[ParamSpec]    keyword_params: Dict[str, ParamSpec]    variadic: bool = False    return_type: ParamType = ParamType.MATRIX    min_args: int = 0  # 可变参数的最小数量class OperatorSpecBuilder:    """操作符规格构建器 - 根据operators.json和特殊规则构建操作符规格"""    @staticmethod    def build_all_specs() -> Dict[str, OperatorSpec]:        """构建所有操作符的规格"""        specs = {}        # Arithmetic operators        specs.update(OperatorSpecBuilder._build_arithmetic_specs())        # Logical operators        specs.update(OperatorSpecBuilder._build_logical_specs())        # Time Series operators        specs.update(OperatorSpecBuilder._build_time_series_specs())        # Cross Sectional operators        specs.update(OperatorSpecBuilder._build_cross_sectional_specs())        # Vector operators        specs.update(OperatorSpecBuilder._build_vector_specs())        # Transformational operators        specs.update(OperatorSpecBuilder._build_transformational_specs())        # Group operators        specs.update(OperatorSpecBuilder._build_group_specs())        # Special operators        specs.update(OperatorSpecBuilder._build_special_specs())        return specs    @staticmethod    def _build_arithmetic_specs() -> Dict[str, OperatorSpec]:        """构建算术运算符规格"""        return {            'add': OperatorSpec(                name='add',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('y', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={'filter': ParamSpec('filter', ParamType.BOOL, optional=True, default_value=False)},                variadic=True,                min_args=2            ),            'multiply': OperatorSpec(                name='multiply',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('y', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={'filter': ParamSpec('filter', ParamType.BOOL, optional=True, default_value=False)},                variadic=True,                min_args=2            ),            'sign': OperatorSpec(                name='sign',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'subtract': OperatorSpec(                name='subtract',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('y', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={'filter': ParamSpec('filter', ParamType.BOOL, optional=True, default_value=False)}            ),            'log': OperatorSpec(                name='log',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'max': OperatorSpec(                name='max',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('y', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={},                variadic=True,                min_args=2            ),            'to_nan': OperatorSpec(                name='to_nan',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={                    'value': ParamSpec('value', ParamType.FLOAT, optional=True, default_value=0),                    'reverse': ParamSpec('reverse', ParamType.BOOL, optional=True, default_value=False)                }            ),            'abs': OperatorSpec(                name='abs',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'divide': OperatorSpec(                name='divide',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('y', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={}            ),            'min': OperatorSpec(                name='min',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('y', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={},                variadic=True,                min_args=2            ),            'signed_power': OperatorSpec(                name='signed_power',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('y', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={}            ),            'inverse': OperatorSpec(                name='inverse',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'sqrt': OperatorSpec(                name='sqrt',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'reverse': OperatorSpec(                name='reverse',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'power': OperatorSpec(                name='power',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('y', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={}            ),            'densify': OperatorSpec(                name='densify',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.GROUP)],                keyword_params={},                return_type=ParamType.GROUP            ),            'arc_sin': OperatorSpec(                name='arc_sin',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'arc_cos': OperatorSpec(                name='arc_cos',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            's_log_1p': OperatorSpec(                name='s_log_1p',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'replace': OperatorSpec(                name='replace',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={                    'target': ParamSpec('target', ParamType.STRING, optional=False),                    'dest': ParamSpec('dest', ParamType.STRING, optional=False)                }            ),            'log_diff': OperatorSpec(                name='log_diff',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'floor': OperatorSpec(                name='floor',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),            'ceiling': OperatorSpec(                name='ceiling',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={}            ),        }    @staticmethod    def _build_logical_specs() -> Dict[str, OperatorSpec]:        """构建逻辑运算符规格"""        return {            'or': OperatorSpec(                name='or',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('input1', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('input2', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={},                return_type=ParamType.MATRIX            ),            'and': OperatorSpec(                name='and',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('input1', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('input2', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={},                return_type=ParamType.MATRIX            ),            'not': OperatorSpec(                name='not',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={},                return_type=ParamType.MATRIX            ),            'is_nan': OperatorSpec(                name='is_nan',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('input', ParamType.ANY)],                keyword_params={},                return_type=ParamType.MATRIX            ),            'if_else': OperatorSpec(                name='if_else',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('input1', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('input2', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('input3', ParamType.ANY)                ],                keyword_params={},                return_type=ParamType.MATRIX            ),            # 比较运算符的函数形式（也支持中缀形式 <, <=, >, >=, ==, !=）            'less': OperatorSpec(                name='less',                positional_params=[                    ParamSpec('input1', ParamType.ANY),                    ParamSpec('input2',
```
