{            'convert': OperatorSpec(                name='convert',                positional_params=[ParamSpec('x', ParamType.MATRIX)],                keyword_params={                    'mode': ParamSpec('mode', ParamType.STRING, optional=True, default_value='dollar2share',                                     value_constraint=lambda v: v in ['dollar2share', 'share2dollar'])                }            ),        }# ============================================================================# 第六部分：语义分析器（Semantic Analyzer）# ============================================================================class SemanticAnalyzer:    """语义分析器 - 执行类型检查、参数验证等"""    # BRAIN 操作符数量限制    MAX_OPERATORS = 64    def __init__(self, context: Any):        self.context = context        self.defined_vars = {}  # {var_name: ParamType}        self.used_vars = set()        self.errors = []        self.operator_count = 0  # 操作符计数器    def _node_repr(self, node: ASTNode) -> str:        """生成 AST 节点的可读字符串表示"""        if isinstance(node, IdentifierNode):            return node.name        elif isinstance(node, NumberNode):            return str(node.value)        elif isinstance(node, StringNode):            return f'"{node.value}"'        elif isinstance(node, BoolNode):            return str(node.value)        elif isinstance(node, NanNode):            return "NaN"        elif isinstance(node, FunctionCallNode):            return f"{node.name}(...)"        elif isinstance(node, BinaryOpNode):            return f"({self._node_repr(node.left)} {node.op} {self._node_repr(node.right)})"        elif isinstance(node, UnaryOpNode):            return f"{node.op}{self._node_repr(node.operand)}"        else:            return "<expr>"    def _count_operators(self, node: ASTNode) -> int:        """递归统计 AST 中的操作符数量"""        count = 0                if isinstance(node, FunctionCallNode):            count += 1            for arg in node.args:                count += self._count_operators(arg)            for kwarg in node.kwargs.values():                count += self._count_operators(kwarg)        elif isinstance(node, BinaryOpNode):            # 二元运算符也算一个操作符            count += 1            count += self._count_operators(node.left)            count += self._count_operators(node.right)        elif isinstance(node, UnaryOpNode):            count += 1            count += self._count_operators(node.operand)        elif isinstance(node, AssignmentNode):            count += self._count_operators(node.value)                return count    def analyze(self, ast: ProgramNode) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:        """分析AST，返回(是否通过, 错误列表)"""        self.errors = []        self.defined_vars = {}        self.used_vars = set()        self.operator_count = 0        # 首先检查操作符数量        total_ops = 0        for stmt in ast.statements:            total_ops += self._count_operators(stmt)        total_ops += self._count_operators(ast.final_expr)                if total_ops > self.MAX_OPERATORS:            self.errors.append(                f"Expression with {total_ops} operators exceeds limit of {self.MAX_OPERATORS}"            )            # 继续分析以收集其他错误        # 分析赋值语句        for stmt in ast.statements:            self._analyze_assignment(stmt)        # 分析最终表达式        final_expr_type = self._analyze_expression(ast.final_expr)        # 检查未使用的变量        self._check_unused_variables()        # 检查caution3规则：最终表达式不能是赋值        if isinstance(ast.final_expr, AssignmentNode):            self.errors.append("Final expression cannot be an assignment (caution3)")        # 检查最终表达式不能是GROUP类型        if final_expr_type == ParamType.GROUP:            self.errors.append("Final expression cannot return GROUP type. GROUP values must be used with group_* operators only")        return (len(self.errors) == 0, self.errors)    def _analyze_assignment(self, node: AssignmentNode):        """分析赋值语句"""        var_name = node.var_name        var_name_lower = var_name.lower()        # 检查变量名冲突（大小写不敏感）- BRAIN 使用大小写不敏感的变量名        for existing_var in self.defined_vars:            if existing_var.lower() == var_name_lower:                if existing_var == var_name:                    # 完全相同的变量名 - 重复赋值                    self.errors.append(                        f"Attempted to redefine variable \"{var_name}\" "                        f"(variable reassignment is not allowed in BRAIN)"                    )                else:                    # 大小写不同但视为相同 - 大小写冲突                    self.errors.append(                        f"Variable name \"{var_name}\" conflicts with existing variable \"{existing_var}\" "                        f"(BRAIN uses case-insensitive variable names)"                    )                return        # 检查变量名冲突        if self.context.is_operator(var_name):            self.errors.append(f"Variable name '{var_name}' conflicts with operator")            return        if self.context.is_datafield(var_name):            self.errors.append(f"Variable name '{var_name}' conflicts with datafield")            return        # 检查禁用的变量名（不区分大小写）        forbidden_names = {'delta', 'sum', 'covariance', 'delay'}        if var_name.lower() in forbidden_names:            self.errors.append(f"Variable name '{var_name}' is reserved and cannot be used")            return        # 分析右侧表达式        expr_type = self._analyze_expression(node.value)        # 记录变量类型        self.defined_vars[var_name] = expr_type    def _analyze_expression(self, node: ASTNode) -> ParamType:        """分析表达式，返回表达式的类型"""        if isinstance(node, NumberNode):            if isinstance(node.value, int):                return ParamType.INT            return ParamType.FLOAT        elif isinstance(node, StringNode):            return ParamType.STRING        elif isinstance(node, BoolNode):            return ParamType.BOOL        elif isinstance(node, NanNode):            return ParamType.FLOAT        elif isinstance(node, IdentifierNode):            return self._analyze_identifier(node)        elif isinstance(node, FunctionCallNode):            return self._analyze_function_call(node)        elif isinstance(node, BinaryOpNode):            return self._analyze_binary_op(node)        elif isinstance(node, UnaryOpNode):            return self._analyze_unary_op(node)        else:            self.errors.append(f"Unknown node type: {type(node).__name__}")            return ParamType.ANY    def _analyze_identifier(self, node: IdentifierNode) -> ParamType:        """分析标识符（数据字段或变量）"""        name = node.name        name_lower = name.lower()        # 检查是否是变量（大小写不敏感）        for var_name, var_type in self.defined_vars.items():            if var_name.lower() == name_lower:                self.used_vars.add(var_name)  # 使用原始定义的名称                return var_type        # 检查是否是数据字段        if self.context.is_datafield(name):            return self.context.get_datafield_type(name)        # 豁免字段：常见的 GROUP 类型字段总是被视为有效的        if name_lower in ['industry', 'sector', 'subindustry', 'market', 'country', 'exchange']:            return ParamType.GROUP        # 在 operator_only 模式下，假设未知标识符是有效的数据字段        if hasattr(self.context, 'operator_only') and self.context.operator_only:            return ParamType.MATRIX        # 未定义的标识符        self.errors.append(f"Undefined identifier '{name}'")        return ParamType.ANY    def _analyze_binary_op(self, node: BinaryOpNode) -> ParamType:        """分析二元运算"""        left_type = self._analyze_expression(node.left)        right_type = self._analyze_expression(node.right)        # GROUP 类型不能用于二元运算        if left_type == ParamType.GROUP or right_type == ParamType.GROUP:            op_name = "equal" if node.op == "==" else node.op            self.errors.append(                f"Incompatible unit for input of \"{op_name}\": GROUP type cannot be used in binary operations"            )        # STRING 类型不能用于二元运算        if left_type == ParamType.STRING or right_type == ParamType.STRING:            op_name = "equal" if node.op == "==" else node.op            self.errors.append(                f"Incompatible unit for input of \"{op_name}\": STRING type cannot be used in binary operations"            )        # VECTOR 类型不能直接用于二元运算，必须先通过 vec_* 转换        if left_type == ParamType.VECTOR:            left_repr = self._node_repr(node.left)            self.errors.append(                f"VECTOR field '{left_repr}' cannot be used directly in binary operation '{node.op}'. "                f"Use vec_* operators (e.g., vec_avg, vec_sum) to convert to MATRIX first."            )        if right_type == ParamType.VECTOR:            right_repr = self._node_repr(node.right)            self.errors.append(                f"VECTOR field '{right_repr}' cannot be used directly in binary operation '{node.op}'. "                f"Use vec_* operators (e.g., vec_avg, vec_sum) to convert to MATRIX first."            )        # 算术运算和比较运算通常返回MATRIX类型        return ParamType.MATRIX    def _analyze_unary_op(self, node: UnaryOpNode) -> ParamType:        """分析一元运算"""        operand_type = self._analyze_expression(node.operand)        # VECTOR 类型不能直接用于一元运算        if operand_type == ParamType.VECTOR:            operand_repr = self._node_repr(node.operand)            self.errors.append(                f"VECTOR field '{operand_repr}' cannot be used directly in unary operation '{node.op}'. "                f"Use vec_* operators (e.g., vec_avg, vec_sum) to convert to MATRIX first."            )        # 一元负号运算返回与操作数相同的类型（或MATRIX类型）        # 如果操作数是数值类型，保持其类型；否则返回MATRIX        if operand_type in [ParamType.INT, ParamType.FLOAT]:            return operand_type        return ParamType.MATRIX    def _analyze_function_call(self, node: FunctionCallNode) -> ParamType:        """分析函数调用"""        func_name = node.name        # 检查操作符是否存在        if not self.context.is_operator(func_name):            self.errors.append(f"Unknown operator: '{func_name}'")            return ParamType.ANY        spec = self.context.get_operator_spec(func_name)        # 检查参数数量        self._check_param_count(node, spec)        # 分析并检查位置参数类型        arg_types = []        for i, arg in enumerate(node.args):            arg_type = self._analyze_expression(arg)            arg_types.append(arg_type)            # 检查参数类型            if i < len(spec.positional_params):                param_spec = spec.positional_params[i]                self._check_param_type(arg, arg_type, param_spec, func_name, i)                                # 检查 lookback 参数（时间序列操作符的第二个参数通常是 lookback）                self._check_lookback_param(func_name, param_spec.name, arg, i)        # 分析并检查关键字参数        for key, value in node.kwargs.items():            if key not in spec.keyword_params:                self.errors.append(f"Unknown keyword argument '{key}' for operator '{func_name}'")                continue            param_spec = spec.keyword_params[key]            value_type = self._analyze_expression(value)            self._check_param_type(value, value_type, param_spec, func_name, key)            # 检查参数值约束            if isinstance(value, (NumberNode, StringNode, BoolNode)):                is_valid, msg = param_spec.validate_value(value.value)                if not is_valid:                    self.errors.append(f"{func_name}: {msg}")                        # 检查 lookback 类型的关键字参数            self._check_lookback_param(func_name, key, value, key)        # 检查必需的关键字参数是否都被提供        for key, param_spec in spec.keyword_params.items():            # 如果参数不是可选的，且没有默认值，则必须提供            if not param_spec.optional and param_spec.default_value is None:                if key not in node.kwargs:                    self.errors.append(                        f"{func_name}: Missing required keyword argument '{key}'"                    )        # 检查关键字参数是否使用了位置传递        self._check_keyword_params_format(node, spec)        # 特殊规则检查        self._check_special_rules(node, spec, arg_types)        # 返回函数返回类型        return spec.return_type    def _check_lookback_param(self, func_name: str, param_name: str, arg: ASTNode, param_index):        """检查 lookback 类型参数必须为正整数 (>=1)"""        # 常见的 lookback 参数名        lookback_param_names = {'lookback', 'days', 'period', 'window', 'n', 'd'}                # 时间序列操作符，第二个位置参数通常是 lookback        ts_operators_with_lookback = {            'ts_delay', 'ts_delta', 'ts_sum', 'ts_mean', 'ts_product',            'ts_std_dev', 'ts_min', 'ts_max', 'ts_rank', 'ts_arg_max',            'ts_arg_min', 'ts_covariance', 'ts_corr', 'ts_skewness',            'ts_kurtosis', 'ts_regression', 'ts_decay_linear',             'ts_decay_exp_window', 'ts_zscore', 'ts_backfill',            'ts_av_diff', 'ts_entropy', 'ts_hump', 'ts_moment',            'ts_partial_corr', 'ts_percentage', 'ts_returns',            'ts_scale', 'ts_theilsen', 'ts_weighted_mean'        }                # 检查是否是时间序列操作符的 lookback 参数        is_lookback = False                # 通过参数名判断        if isinstance(param_name, str) and param_name.lower() in lookback_param_names:            is_lookback = True                # 通过位置判断（时间序列操作符的第二个参数）        if func_name.lower() in ts_operators_with_lookback and param_index == 1:            is_lookback = True                if is_lookback and isinstance(arg, NumberNode):            value = arg.value            if not isinstance(value, int) or value < 1:                self.errors.append(                    f"Got invalid value \"{value}\" for attribute \"{param_name}\", "                    f"should be a positive integer"                )    def _check_param_count(self, node: FunctionCallNode, spec: OperatorSpec):        """检查参数数量"""        func_name = node.name        arg_count = len(node.args)        if spec.variadic:            # 可变参数，检查最小数量            if arg_count < spec.min_args:                self.errors.append(                    f"{func_name}: Expected at least {spec.min_args} arguments, got {arg_count}"                )        else:            # 固定参数，考虑可选参数            # 计算必需参数数量（optional=False）            required_count = sum(1 for p in spec.positional_params if not p.optional)            total_count = len(spec.positional_params)            if arg_count < required_count:                self.errors.append(                    f"{func_name}: Expected at least {required_count} positional arguments, got {arg_count}"                )            elif arg_count > total_count:                self.errors.append(                    f"{func_name}: Expected at most {total_count} positional arguments, got {arg_count}"                )    def _check_param_type(self, arg: ASTNode, arg_type: ParamType,                         param_spec: ParamSpec, func_name: str, param_index):        """检查参数类型是否匹配"""        expected_type = param_spec.param_type        # ANY类型接受任何类型，但不包括GROUP、STRING和VECTOR        if expected_type == ParamType.ANY:            if arg_type == ParamType.GROUP:                self.errors.append(                    f"{func_name}: Parameter {param_index} cannot accept GROUP type. "                    f"GROUP fields can only be used with operators that explicitly accept GROUP parameters"                )            elif arg_type == ParamType.STRING:                self.errors.append(                    f"{func_name}: Parameter {param_index} cannot accept STRING type. "                    f"STRING values can only be used with operators that explicitly accept STRING parameters"                )            elif arg_type == ParamType.VECTOR:                self.errors.append(                    f"{func_name}: Parameter {param_index} cannot accept VECTOR type. "                    f"VECTOR fields must be converted to MATRIX using vec_* operators first"                )            return        # VECTOR类型必须先通过vec_*操作符转换为MATRIX        if expected_type == ParamType.MATRIX and arg_type == ParamType.VECTOR:            # 检查是否是vec_*操作符的调用            if not (isinstance(arg, FunctionCallNode) and arg.name.startswith('vec_')):                self.errors.append(                    f"{func_name}: Parameter {param_index} requires MATRIX type, "                    f"but got VECTOR. VECTOR fields must be converted using vec_* operators"                )            return        # GROUP类型只能用于group参数        if expected_type == ParamType.GROUP and arg_type != ParamType.GROUP:            self.errors.append(                f"{func_name}: Parameter '{param_spec.name}' requires GROUP type, got {arg_type.value}"            )            return        # 类型匹配检查（允许INT作为FLOAT使用）        if expected_type != arg_type:            if not (expected_type == ParamType.FLOAT and arg_type == ParamType.INT):                if arg_type != ParamType.ANY:  # ANY类型跳过检查                    self.errors.append(                        f"{func_name}: Parameter {param_index} type mismatch - "                        f"expected {expected_type.value}, got {arg_type.value}"                    )    def _check_keyword_params_format(self, node: FunctionCallNode, spec: OperatorSpec):        """检查关键字参数是否正确使用name=value格式"""        func_name = node.name        # 检查位置参数中是否误用了关键字参数的名称        for i, arg in enumerate(node.args):            # 如果位置参数超过了定义的位置参数数量，且有对应的关键字参数            if i >= len(spec.positional_params):                # 检查是否应该使用关键字参数                if isinstance(arg, IdentifierNode) and arg.name in spec.keyword_params:                    self.errors.append(                        f"{func_name}: Parameter '{arg.name}' should be passed as keyword argument"                    )    def _check_special_rules(self, node: FunctionCallNode, spec: OperatorSpec, arg_types: List[ParamType]):        """检查特殊规则"""        func_name = node.name        # 特殊规则1: ts_backfill参数使用检查        if func_name == 'ts_backfill':            # 检查1a: 不允许使用ignore参数            if 'ignore' in node.kwargs:                self.errors.append(f"ts_backfill: 'ignore' parameter is not allowed")            # 检查1b: 参数使用方式 - 要么用 ts_backfill(x, d)，要么用 ts_backfill(x, lookback=d)            has_second_positional = len(node.args) >= 2            has_lookback_keyword = 'lookback' in node.kwargs            if has_second_positional and has_lookback_keyword:                self.errors.append(                    "ts_backfill: Cannot use both positional parameter 'd' and keyword parameter 'lookback'. "                    "Use either ts_backfill(x, d) or ts_backfill(x, lookback=d)"                )            elif not has_second_positional and not has_lookback_keyword:                self.errors.append(                    "ts_backfill: Must provide either second positional parameter or 'lookback' keyword parameter. "                    "Use either ts_backfill(x, d) or ts_backfill(x, lookback=d)"                )        # 特殊规则2: bucket的range参数格式检查        if func_name == 'bucket' and 'range' in node.kwargs:            range_node = node.kwargs['range']            if isinstance(range_node, StringNode):                range_val = range_node.value                # 检查格式：应该是 "a, b, c" 形式                parts = range_val.split(',')                if len(parts) != 3:                    self.errors.append(f"bucket: range parameter must be in format 'a,b,c'")                else:                    try:                        a, b, c = [float(p.strip()) for p in parts]                        # 检查c能否被1整除                        if c <= 0 or 1 % c != 0:                            # 应该是1可以被c整除                            if abs(round(1 / c) * c - 1) > 0.001:                                self.errors.append(f"bucket: range parameter c={c} should divide 1 evenly")                    except ValueError:                        self.errors.append(f"bucket: range parameter must contain numeric values")        # 特殊规则3: rank的rate参数检查        if func_name == 'rank' and 'rate' in node.kwargs:            rate_node = node.kwargs['rate']            if isinstance(rate_node, NumberNode):                if rate_node.value not in [0, 2]:                    self.errors.append(f"rank: rate parameter must be 0 or 2 (or omitted)")        # 特殊规则4: lambda_min < lambda_max 检查        if func_name in ['ts_target_tvr_decay', 'ts_target_tvr_delta_limit']:            if 'lambda_min' in node.kwargs and 'lambda_max' in node.kwargs:                min_node = node.kwargs['lambda_min']                max_node = node.kwargs['lambda_max']                if isinstance(min_node, NumberNode) and isinstance(max_node, NumberNode):                    if min_node.value >= max_node.value:                        self.errors.append(                            f"{func_name}: lambda_min must be less than lambda_max"                        )    def _check_unused_variables(self):        """检查未使用的变量"""        unused = set(self.defined_vars.keys()) - self.used_vars        for var in unused:            self.errors.append(f"Variable '{var}' is defined but never used")# ============================================================================# 第八部分：动态数据上下文（支持工作流集成）# ============================================================================class DynamicDataContext:    """动态数据上下文 - 支持从工作流状态动态构建字段列表        与 DataContext 不同，此类不需要 CSV 文件，而是直接接受字段列表。    适用于工作流中已验证的数据字段。    """    def __init__(self, field_records: List[Dict[str, Any]], operator_only: bool = False,                 allowed_operators: Optional[set] = None):        """初始化动态数据上下文        Args:            field_records: 字段记录列表，每条记录应包含 'id' 和 'type' 键                          例如: [{'id': 'close', 'type': 'MATRIX'}, ...]            operator_only: 如果为 True，则只验证操作符，不验证数据字段            allowed_operators: 可用操作符名称白名单（如来自本地 operator JSON）。                              若提供，则只保留白名单中的操作符，其余视为不可用。        """        self.operator_only = operator_only        self.datafields = self._build_datafields(field_records)        all_ops = OperatorSpecBuilder.build_all_specs()        if allowed_operators is not None:            whitelist = {name.lower() for name in allowed_operators}            self.operators = {k: v for k, v in all_ops.items() if k.lower() in whitelist}        else:            self.operators = all_ops    def _build_datafields(self, field_records: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Dict]:        """从字段记录列表构建数据字段字典"""        datafields = {}        for record in field_records:            if not isinstance(record, dict):                continue            field_id = record.get('id')            if not field_id:                continue            field_type = record.get('type', 'MATRIX').upper()            # 标准化类型名称            if field_type not in ('MATRIX', 'VECTOR', 'GROUP'):                field_type = 'MATRIX'            datafields[field_id] = {                'type': field_type,                'description': record.get('description', '')            }        return datafields    def is_datafield(self, name: str) -> bool:        """检查是否是数据字段（不区分大小写）"""        if self.operator_only:            # 在 operator_only 模式下，不进行数据字段验证            # 返回 False 以避免变量名与"假想的数据字段"冲突            return False        name_lower = name.lower()        return any(field.lower() == name_lower for field in self.datafields)    def get_datafield_type(self, name: str) -> Optional[ParamType]:        """获取数据字段类型（不区分大小写）"""        if self.operator_only:            # 在 operator_only 模式下，假设所有字段都是 MATRIX 类型            return ParamType.MATRIX        name_lower = name.lower()        for field_name, field_info in self.datafields.items():            if field_name.lower() == name_lower:                type_str = field_info['type']                try:                    return ParamType[type_str]                except KeyError:                    return ParamType.MATRIX        return None    def is_operator(self, name: str) -> bool:        """检查是否是操作符（不区分大小写）"""        name_lower = name.lower()        return any(op.lower() == name_lower for op in self.operators)    def get_operator_spec(self, name: str) -> Optional[OperatorSpec]:        """获取操作符规格（不区分大小写）"""        name_lower = name.lower()        for op_name, op_spec in self.operators.items():            if op_name.lower() == name_lower:                return op_spec        return Nonedef validate_expression_with_fields(    expression: str,    field_records: List[Dict[str, Any]],    operator_only: bool = False,    allowed_operators: Optional[set] = None,) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:    """    使用动态字段列表验证 Alpha 表达式    专为工作流集成设计，不需要 CSV 文件。    Args:        expression: Alpha 表达式字符串        field_records: 字段记录列表，每条记录应包含 'id' 和 'type' 键        operator_only: 如果为 True，则只验证操作符语法，跳过字段验证        allowed_operators: 可用操作符名称白名单。若提供，使用不在白名单中的                          操作符将被视为 "Unknown operator" 错误。    Returns:        (是否通过验证, 错误列表)    """    try:        # 1. 词法分析        tokenizer = Tokenizer(expression)        tokens = tokenizer.tokenize()        # 2. 语法分析        parser = Parser(tokens)        ast = parser.parse()        # 3. 构建动态数据上下文        context = DynamicDataContext(field_records, operator_only=operator_only,                                    allowed_operators=allowed_operators)        # 4. 语义分析        analyzer = SemanticAnalyzer(context)        is_valid, errors = analyzer.analyze(ast)        return is_valid, errors    except SyntaxError as e:        return False, [f"Syntax error: {str(e)}"]    except Exception as e:        return False, [f"Validation error: {str(e)}"]def validate_operators_only(expression: str) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:    """    仅验证表达式中的操作符语法，不验证数据字段        适用于工作流中字段已经被验证的场景，只需要检查操作符使用是否正确。        Args:        expression: Alpha 表达式字符串        Returns:        (是否通过验证, 错误列表)    """    return validate_expression_with_fields(expression, [], operator_only=True)# ============================================================================# 测试和调试工具# ============================================================================
```

### 评论 #19 (作者: JX98676, 时间: 4个月前)

感谢上次的代码能够得到版主的认可。这次我分享的是优化的。

意思就是找到相似的因子，分成一个组，设定一个条件，一般来说Sharpe Fitness Margin数据都不错但是PC比较高。但是我们可以让AI去学习做高数据的经验，然后降低PC，取长补短，更好地做出优质的数据。我的思路具体如下：

## 一、工具能解决什么问题

WorldQuant BRAIN 平台上，研究员每天会产出大量在样本内（IS）通过、但因 RA 检查（Risk Analysis）失败而无法提交的 Alpha。这些 Alpha 通常"有信号、欠优化"，人工逐个修改成本极高。

本工具围绕一条主线工作：

```
拉 IS_FAIL Alpha → 过滤指标 → 相似度分组 → 让 AI 生成变异表达式 → 回测 → 分类成功/待优化
```

并把整条管线 GUI 化、自动化（含与 Claude Code CLI 的自动协作）。

**重要约束**：工具只做拉取、模拟、分析，**绝不自动提交 Alpha 到生产**。一切 submit 动作交还研究员人工决策。

---

## 二、核心逻辑全景图

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    1) 登录 & 参数配置                            │
│   LoginDialog → sign_in() 拿到 BRAIN requests.Session          │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              ↓
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│   2) 筛选 Alpha (fetch_screened_alphas)                         │
│   - 拉 status=UNSUBMITTED + IS_FAIL                             │
│   - 过滤 Failed RA = 0（只保留所有 RA 检查通过的）                │
│   - 应用 Sharpe/Fitness/Margin/Turnover 阈值                    │
│   - 解析 Pyramid 层级 → dataset_hint                           │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              ↓
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│   3) 自相关计算 (calc_self_corr)                                │
│   - 下载/加载 OS Alpha PnL → 计算 returns                       │
│   - 用最近 4 年收益与每个候选 Alpha 求 max corr                  │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              ↓
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│   4) 双层分组 (group_alphas_by_region_dataset_expr)             │
│   一级 key: (region, dataset_hint)                              │
│   二级:    表达式 Jaccard 相似度 ≥ 阈值                          │
│   每个子组取 Sharpe 最高者作为"组代表 best"                      │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              ↓
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│   5) AI 闭环 (双阶段，文件通信)                                  │
│                                                                │
│   阶段 A 分析：                                                 │
│     主程序写 task_<id>.json                                     │
│     → 自动启动 cmd + claude CLI                                 │
│     → 粘贴"分析提示词"                                          │
│     → 等待 analysis_<id>.json 产生                              │
│                                                                │
│   阶段 B 回测：                                                 │
│     主程序基于 directions 构造"回测提示词"                       │
│     → 新开窗口 + 粘贴                                           │
│     → Claude 通过 brain-mcp create_simulation 跑模拟            │
│     → 写 result_<id>.json                                       │
│     → 主程序读取并分类                                          │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                              ↓
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│   6) 分类 & 持久化                                              │
│   达标 → success_alphas（成功列表）                              │
│   未达标 → pending_alphas（待优化列表）                          │
│   全部写入 results_log.json 历史档案                             │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 三、模块清单（按文件内顺序）

| 区段 | 函数/类 | 职责 |
|------|---------|------|
| 任务缓存 | `write_task_file` / `read_result_file` / `read_analysis_file` / `cleanup_task_files` | 与 Claude Code 之间的 JSON 文件通信 |
| 凭证 | `load_credentials` / `save_credentials` | BRAIN 邮箱密码本地保存 |
| 提示词 | 一组 `DEFAULT_*_PROMPT` + `load_prompts` / `save_prompts` | 7 套可热改提示词模板 |
| BRAIN API | `sign_in` / `wait_get` / `get_alpha_pnls` / `get_os_alphas` | 登录、限流重试、拉取 PnL、拉取 OS 列表 |
| 相关性 | `calc_self_corr` / `download_data` / `load_data` | 自相关 / PPAC 计算与本地缓存 |
| 趋势 | `get_alpha_yearly_stats` / `check_yearly_trend` | 近三年 Sharpe/Fitness/Margin 是否上升 |
| 工具函数 | `_extract_dataset_hint` / `_expr_similarity` / `build_corr_matrix` | 表达式解析、Jaccard、相关性矩阵 |
| 分组 | `group_alphas_by_region_dataset_expr` | 核心分组算法 |
| 筛选 | `fetch_screened_alphas` | 调 BRAIN 列表接口 + 过滤 |
| 模拟 | `simulate_expression` | POST /simulations 并轮询结果 |
| 解析 | `parse_claude_expressions` | 从 AI 回复抽取 `EXPR:` 行 |
| GUI | `PromptSettingsDialog` / `AlphaAnalysisDialog` / `ClaudeInteractDialog` / `SmartAlphaApp` / `LoginDialog` | Tkinter 多 Tab 主界面 |
| 入口 | `main` | 登录循环 + 启动主窗口 |

## 四、关键算法详解（含代码）

### 4.1 表达式相似度（Jaccard）

把表达式拆成"非算子标识符集合"，再用 Jaccard 系数衡量相似度。例如 `rank(close)` 与 `rank(ts_delay(close,5))` 的算子被忽略，只剩 `{close}` 与 `{close}`，相似度 = 1。

```python
_EXPR_OPS = {
    "rank", "ts_rank", "zscore", "ts_zscore", "signed_power", "log",
    "abs", "max", "min", "sum", "ts_sum", "ts_mean", "ts_std",
    "ts_max", "ts_min", "ts_delta", "ts_delay", "ts_corr", "ts_cov",
    "ts_decay_linear", "ts_argmax", "ts_argmin", "neutralize",
    "group_rank", "group_zscore", "group_neutralize", "indneutralize",
    "if", "else", "and", "or", "not", "True", "False"
}

def _expr_similarity(exp1, exp2):
    def get_tokens(exp):
        tokens = re.findall(r"[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*", exp or "")
        return set(t for t in tokens if t not in _EXPR_OPS)
    s1, s2 = get_tokens(exp1), get_tokens(exp2)
    if not s1 and not s2:
        return 1.0
    if not s1 or not s2:
        return 0.0
    return len(s1 & s2) / len(s1 | s2)
```

### 4.2 双层分组

先按 (Region, Dataset) 做粗粒度桶，再在桶内用相似度做"贪心聚类"，每个子组保留 Sharpe 最高者作为代表。

```python
def group_alphas_by_region_dataset_expr(alphas, expr_sim_threshold=0.5):
    primary = defaultdict(list)
    for a in alphas:
        key = (a.get("region", ""), a.get("dataset_hint", ""))
        primary[key].append(a)

groups = []
    for (region, dataset), members in primary.items():
        visited = set()
        for i, a in enumerate(members):
            if a["alpha_id"] in visited:
                continue
            sub_group = [a]
            visited.add(a["alpha_id"])
            for j, b in enumerate(members):
                if i == j or b["alpha_id"] in visited:
                    continue
                if _expr_similarity(a["exp"], b["exp"]) >= expr_sim_threshold:
                    sub_group.append(b)
                    visited.add(b["alpha_id"])
            best = max(sub_group, key=lambda x: x.get("sharpe") or 0)
            groups.append({
                "group_id":    len(groups) + 1,
                "region":      region,
                "dataset_hint": dataset,
                "best":        best,
                "members":     sub_group,
            })
    return groups
```

### 4.3 筛选 + Failed RA = 0

直接调用 BRAIN 的 `/users/self/alphas` 列表接口，URL 上加好 `status=UNSUBMITTED|IS_FAIL`、`is.sharpe>`、`is.fitness>`、`settings.region`，然后在客户端进一步过滤 RA 检查。

```python
RA_CHECKS = [
    "LOW_SHARPE", "LOW_FITNESS", "LOW_TURNOVER", "HIGH_TURNOVER",
    "LOW_MARGIN", "CONCENTRATED_WEIGHT", "CONCENTRATED_SECTOR",
    "CONCENTRATED_INDUSTRY", "CONCENTRATED_SUBINDUSTRY",
    "LOW_BOOKSIZE", "SHARPE_DRAWDOWN", "CORRELATION", "PROD_CORRELATION"
]

# fetch_screened_alphas 中的客户端过滤片段：
checks = alpha.get("is", {}).get("checks") or []
failed_ra = sum(
    1 for c in checks
    if c.get("name") in RA_CHECKS
    and c.get("result") not in ("PASS", "PENDING")
)
if failed_ra > 0:
    continue                       # 只要任一 RA 没过就丢弃
```

### 4.4 自相关计算（与全平台 OS Alpha 比对）

```python
def calc_self_corr(session, alpha_id, os_alpha_rets, os_alpha_ids,
                   alpha_result=None, alpha_pnls=None):
    if alpha_result is None:
        alpha_result = wait_get(
            session, f" [https://api.worldquantbrain.com/alphas/{alpha_id}](https://api.worldquantbrain.com/alphas/{alpha_id}) "
        ).json()
    if alpha_pnls is None:
        _, alpha_pnls = get_alpha_pnls(session, [alpha_result])
        alpha_pnls = alpha_pnls[alpha_id]
    rets = alpha_pnls - alpha_pnls.ffill().shift(1)
    cutoff = pd.to_datetime(rets.index).max() - pd.DateOffset(years=4)
    rets = rets[pd.to_datetime(rets.index) > cutoff]
    region = alpha_result["settings"]["region"]
    if region not in os_alpha_ids or not os_alpha_ids[region]:
        return 0.0
    sc = os_alpha_rets[os_alpha_ids[region]].corrwith(rets).max()
    return 0.0 if np.isnan(sc) else float(sc)
```

### 4.5 单 Alpha 模拟提交（不调用 /submit）

```python
def simulate_expression(session, base_alpha, new_expr, log_cb=None):
    payload = {
        "type": "REGULAR",
        "settings": {
            "instrumentType":  "EQUITY",
            "region":          base_alpha.get("region", "USA"),
            "universe":        base_alpha.get("universe", "TOP3000"),
            "delay":           1,
            "decay":           base_alpha.get("decay") or 4,
            "neutralization":  base_alpha.get("neutralization", "SUBINDUSTRY"),
            "truncation":      0.08,
            "unitHandling":    "VERIFY",
            "pasteurization":  "ON",
            "nanHandling":     "OFF",
            "language":        "FASTEXPR",
            "visualization":   False,
        },
        "regular": new_expr,
    }
    resp = session.post(" [https://api.worldquantbrain.com/simulations](https://api.worldquantbrain.com/simulations) ", json=payload)
    # ... 解析 Location 头取 sim_id，轮询 /alphas/{sim_id} 直到 status=UNSUBMITTED
```

### 4.6 AI 双阶段文件协议

工具与 Claude Code CLI 通过 3 类 JSON 文件交换信息：

| 文件 | 写入方 | 内容 |
|------|--------|------|
| `task_<id>.json`     | 主程序 | 任务元数据（任务 ID、来源 Alpha） |
| `analysis_<id>.json` | Claude | `{"summary": "...", "directions": ["方向1", ...]}` |
| `result_<id>.json`   | Claude | `{"alphas": [{"alpha_id":..., "sharpe":..., ...}]}` |

主程序阻塞读取的核心实现（前 2 分钟静默，之后每 30 秒轮询）：

```python
def read_result_file(task_id, timeout=None):
    path = TASK_DIR / f"result_{task_id}.json"
    time.sleep(120)                  # 初始静默
    while True:
        if path.exists():
            try:
                with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    return json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                pass
        time.sleep(30)
```

### 4.7 自动化粘贴提示词（Windows 专属）

最关键的小技巧：**用"窗口快照差集法"识别新打开的 cmd 窗口**，避免按标题搜索的不可靠性。

```python
# 1) 启动前快照
snap_before = set()
def _snap(h, _):
    if win32gui.IsWindowVisible(h):
        snap_before.add(h)
win32gui.EnumWindows(_snap, None)

# 2) 启动 claude CLI
subprocess.Popen(
    f'start cmd /k "cd /d {work_dir} && claude --dangerously-skip-permissions"',
    shell=True, cwd=work_dir
)
time.sleep(15)                       # 等待初始化

# 3) 找差集中的新窗口
for _ in range(60):
    new_found = []
    def _find_new(h, _):
        if win32gui.IsWindowVisible(h) and h not in snap_before:
            t = win32gui.GetWindowText(h)
            if t:
                new_found.append((h, t))
    win32gui.EnumWindows(_find_new, None)
    if new_found:
        hwnd = new_found[-1][0]
        break
    time.sleep(1)

# 4) AttachThreadInput + SetForegroundWindow 抢焦点
# 5) 写剪贴板 → ctrl+v → Enter (确认粘贴) → Enter (发送)
```

### 4.8 两阶段提示词（节选）

阶段 A — 强制 Claude 先写占位文件，再分析、最后覆盖写入最终 JSON：

```python
def _build_analysis_prompt(self, grp, task_id):
    analysis_path = str(TASK_DIR / f"analysis_{task_id}.json")
    base = self._build_group_prompt_text(grp)
    return (
        f"# ⚡ 强制首要任务（必须最先执行）\n"
        f"第一步必须调用 Write 工具写入：\n"
        f"  路径: {analysis_path}\n"
        f"  内容: {{\"task_id\": \"{task_id}\", \"summary\": \"分析中\", \"directions\": []}}\n"
        f"---\n"
        + base +
        f"请对上述 Alpha 组进行深度分析，要求：\n"
        f"1. 核心信号（一句话/条）\n"
        f"2. 同质化根源（2-3句）\n"
        f"3. 差异化缺口（2-3句）\n"
        f"4. 优化方向：列出 3-5 个具体方向，每个 10 字以内命名\n"
        f"分析完成后必须覆盖写入：\n"
        f"  {{\"task_id\": \"{task_id}\", \"summary\": \"...\", \"directions\": [...]}}\n"
    )
```

阶段 B — 把上一步 directions 注入回测提示词，让 Claude 调 `brain-mcp.create_simulation`：

```python
def _build_backtest_prompt(self, grp, task_id, analysis_data):
    result_path    = str(TASK_DIR / f"result_{task_id}.json")
    directions     = analysis_data.get('directions', [])
    summary        = analysis_data.get('summary', '')
    # ...
    return (
        f"上阶段分析摘要：{summary}\n"
        f"优化方向（共 {len(directions)} 个）：\n{dir_text}\n"
        f"执行步骤：\n"
        f"1. 针对每个优化方向设计 1 个差异化 Alpha 表达式\n"
        f"2. 对每个表达式调用 brain-mcp create_simulation 工具提交回测\n"
        f"3. 等待 status=UNSUBMITTED，记录指标\n"
        f"4. 写入结果文件 {result_path}\n"
    )
```

## 五、GUI 结构（Tkinter）

```
SmartAlphaApp（主窗口）
├── 顶部 LabelFrame "筛选参数"  ← 3 行参数（基础阈值 + 目标阈值）

    ├── 待优化列表      pending_tree  (双击 → AlphaAnalysisDialog)

每个表格列字段、双击行为、上下文菜单都在 `_build_*_tab` 函数里集中定义。

## 八、典型使用流程（5 步）

1. **登录**：填入 BRAIN 邮箱/密码（勾选"记住密码"会明文存到 `credentials.json`）。
2. **设参**：调整 Sharpe、Fitness、Margin、Turnover 阈值与目标阈值，设地区、Alpha 上限。
3. **筛选**：点[开始筛选] → 自动跑 4 步管线（拉 Alpha / 加载 OS PnL / 算自相关 / 分组）。
4. **AI 变异**：在[分组分析]选中一个/多个组，点[发送选中组(自动)] → 工具自动开 cmd + claude CLI 窗口，粘贴提示词，AI 完成分析+回测后写回结果文件。
5. **复盘**：成功列表里挑高 Sharpe 的 Alpha 手动到 BRAIN 平台 submit；待优化列表里的可双击进入"深度分析对话框"二次迭代，或加入"优化工作台"再跑一轮。

---

## 九、设计上值得借鉴的点

1. **AI 任务用文件协议解耦**：主程序不直接 API 调 Claude，而是通过 `task/analysis/result` JSON 文件做异步通信，天然支持多窗口并发、断线续跑、人工接管。
2. **强制占位文件**：提示词里要求 Claude **先写占位 JSON**，再做主任务，最后覆盖写入。这样即使 AI 中途崩溃，主程序也能从占位文件感知到任务已被接收。
3. **窗口差集法**：靠 `EnumWindows` 拿启动前/后两份快照求差集来定位新窗口，比按标题模糊搜索可靠得多。
4. **双层分组**：粗粒度（Region+Dataset）+ 细粒度（Jaccard），既稳又能在每个篮子里找出真正的同质化簇。
5. **目标阈值与筛选阈值分离**：筛选阈值用于"门槛过滤"，目标阈值用于"分类成功/待优化"，两套参数解耦。
6. **本地缓存策略**：OS Alpha PnL 体量大（几 MB pickle），每次都重新下载不现实，工具内置增量下载（`download_data` + `flag_increment=True`）。

---

## 十、常见问题速查

| 现象 | 排查方向 |
|------|----------|
| 登录失败 | 检查邮箱密码；确认本机能访问 `api.worldquantbrain.com` |
| 自动化粘贴失败 | 未装 `pyautogui` / `pywin32`；或 cmd 窗口被其他窗口遮挡 |
| Claude 不写 result 文件 | 检查 `brain-mcp` 是否注册；检查 `C:\Users\<user>\.claude\brain_tasks\` 目录是否可写 |
| 自相关恒为 0 | 该 region 的 OS 列表为空；先跑一次"筛选"以触发 `download_data` |
| 表达式相似度阈值难调 | 默认 0.5，复杂表达式可降到 0.3，简单 alpha 可升到 0.7 |

---

## 十一、扩展方向

- 把 `_paste_to_claude_window` 抽象到独立模块，做成 Linux/macOS 适配版（用 `xdotool` / `osascript`）。
- 把 OS PnL 缓存改成 SQLite / Parquet，缩短加载时间。
- 支持除 Jaccard 外的相似度（编辑距离、TF-IDF + 余弦）。
- 把 Claude CLI 调用替换为直接走 Anthropic API（省去窗口自动化），需配合 `brain-mcp` 的远程化。
- 增加单元测试覆盖 `_expr_similarity` / `group_alphas_by_region_dataset_expr` / `fetch_screened_alphas` 解析逻辑。

---

---

### 评论 #20 (作者: HB79930, 时间: 4个月前)

分享一个我最近在做 WorldQuant Brain / AI 辅助 Alpha 研发时沉淀出来的小型 Skill，主要解决的问题是：

> 如何让 AI / Codex / 本地脚本生成的 Alpha 候选，能够被稳定导入、回测、追踪、归档，并且保留每个 Alpha 的来源链路。

我发现，如果只是让 AI 直接生成公式，很容易出现几个问题：

1. Alpha ID 不统一，后续很难追踪；
2. Settings 格式不稳定，容易导入失败；
3. Rescue / Variant / Field Swap / Region Swap 之间没有父子关系；
4. 回测结果出来后，很难判断这个 Alpha 是独立母式，还是某个失败候选的变体；
5. 后续做 Prod Corr、Self Corr、Sub-universe、IS Ladder 分析时，缺少上下文。

所以我把它整理成了一个“BO 导入 Markdown Skill”。

核心思想是：
 **每个 Alpha 都必须是一个独立 Markdown 单元，并且在公式代码块里保留可解析的注释元数据。**

推荐格式如下：

```
#alphaID = EXAMPLE-R1-MEA-001
#relationType = ROOT
#rootAlphaID = EXAMPLE-R1-MEA-001
#lineageID = EXAMPLE-R1-MEA-001-LINE
#idea = 使用某类基本面/分析师/模型字段构造区域内排序信号
#hypothesis = 如果该字段能反映未来预期变化，则在行业内排序后可能产生稳定横截面收益
#dataRationale = 选择覆盖率较高、经济含义清晰、在目标 Region × Universe 可用的字段
#operatorRationale = 使用 ts_rank / ts_decay_linear / group_rank 平滑并控制行业暴露
#expectedBehavior = 希望 Sharpe、Fitness、Sub-universe 和 IS Ladder 同时保持稳定
#keyMetricsToWatch = Sharpe, Fitness, Turnover, Sub-universe Sharpe, IS Ladder, Self Corr, Prod Corr, Weight
#riskNotes = 注意字段覆盖率、Long/Short Count、Weight concentration 和 Region 字段可用性
#decisionRule = 若 Sharpe/Fitness 过线且 Prod Corr 可控，则进入 Submit Precheck；若仅相关性偏高，则进入 PC Decorr Rescue
#nextActionIfPass = 做正式复测、相关性检查和提交前归档
#nextActionIfFail = 根据失败原因进入 Field Swap / Region Swap / Parameter Decorr / Stop
rank(
    group_rank(ts_rank(ts_decay_linear(example_field, 13), 89), industry)
)
#Settings: Delay=1, Decay=12, Neutralization=INDUSTRY, Truncation=0.08, TestPeriod=1Y0M, Region=MEA, Universe=TOP400

```

如果某个 Alpha 是从另一个 Alpha rescue 出来的，可以这样写：

```
#alphaID = EXAMPLE-R1-MEA-001-PCD-002
#parentAlphaID = EXAMPLE-R1-MEA-001
#relationType = PC_DECORR_RESCUE_OF
#rootAlphaID = EXAMPLE-R1-MEA-001
#lineageID = EXAMPLE-R1-MEA-001-LINE
#rescueReason = Prod Corr 偏高，但 Sharpe / Fitness 仍有保留价值
#rescueMethod = 使用非标准窗口、不同平滑参数、弱化拥挤字段权重
#idea = 在不改变经济逻辑的前提下，对参数和算子结构进行低相关扰动
rank(
    group_rank(ts_rank(ts_decay_linear(example_field, 17), 75), industry)
)
#Settings: Delay=1, Decay=10, Neutralization=INDUSTRY, Truncation=0.08, TestPeriod=0Y0M, Region=MEA, Universe=TOP400

```

这个 Skill 的好处是：

1. **方便 AI 生成候选**
   可以直接要求 AI 按这个格式输出，不容易漏掉 Settings。
2. **方便 Codex / 本地脚本解析**
   所有元数据都是  `#key = value`  格式，后续可以很容易解析成表格。
3. **方便追踪 Alpha lineage**
   可以知道一个候选是 ROOT、RESCUE、FIELD_SWAP、REGION_SWAP，还是 FINAL_PUSH。
4. **方便复盘失败经验**
   不只是记录公式，还记录 idea、hypothesis、riskNotes、decisionRule。
5. **减少重复试错**
   后续看到某个 lineage 全部失败，就可以停止该方向，而不是无限微调。

我个人觉得，AI 时代做 Alpha，不应该只是“让 AI 随机生成更多公式”，而是要把研发流程工程化：

> 公式生成只是第一步，更重要的是候选管理、失败归因、链路追踪、字段可用性检查和结果归档。

一个很实用的扩展是：
在生成非 USA / 非常用 Region 的 Alpha 前，先检查目标  `Region × Universe × Field`  是否真的存在。如果字段不可用，就不要让 AI 硬生成公式，而是让它先寻找同 dataset / 同 category / 经济含义相近的替代字段。

简单原则：

```
生成 Alpha 前：
1. 检查 Region 是否支持该 Universe
2. 检查目标字段是否在该 Region × Universe 中存在
3. 检查字段覆盖率和类别
4. 如果字段不存在，先做 field substitute，而不是直接提交
5. 回测后根据失败原因进入 Rescue / Freeze / Submit Candidate

```

这个方法本质上不是某一个 Alpha 模板，而是一个“Alpha Factory 管理 Skill”。
它适合配合 ChatGPT / Claude / Codex / 本地 Python 脚本使用，也适合多人协作时做候选归档和复盘。

---

### 评论 #21 (作者: AK76468, 时间: 4个月前)

# 分享一个 Codex Skill：Brain Regular Alpha 转 Python Alpha

整理了一个自己用的 Codex skill： `brain-regular-to-python-alpha` 。

它的用途很直接：把 WorldQuant BRAIN 的 Regular Alpha / Fast Expression 转成 Python Alpha core code。适合在想把已有表达式迁移到 Python Alpha、检查复杂表达式语义、或者让 Codex 生成可审计 NumPy 实现时使用。

## 这个 skill 主要解决什么问题

Regular Alpha 到 Python Alpha 不是简单替换函数名。很多 operator 的语义依赖：

- 今日横截面还是历史窗口；
- NaN 和缺失 group 的处理；
- `filter` 、 `dense` 、 `k` 、 `constant` 、 `rettype`  等 named argument；
- `trade_when` 、 `hump`  这类需要跨日状态的 operator；
- computed intermediate 进入 time-series operator 时，需要  `store`  保存历史；
- Python Alpha 返回值必须是今日的一维  `np.float32`  数组。

所以这个 skill 的重点不是“机械翻译”，而是要求 Codex：

1. 先解析字段、变量、operator、窗口长度和依赖关系；
2. 对每个 operator 查询官方 BRAIN operator definition；
3. 区分 element-wise、cross-sectional、group、time-series、stateful operator；
4. 非平凡 operator 默认写成 helper function，方便人工审计；
5. 对无法从公开文档完全复刻的语义明确标注 approximation；
6. 默认只输出 Python Alpha core，不自动跑 simulation。

## 使用方式

把下面的目录放到你的 Codex skills 目录中，例如：

```
~/.agents/skills/brain-regular-to-python-alpha/
```

目录结构：

```
brain-regular-to-python-alpha/├── SKILL.md├── scripts/│   └── operator_lookup.py├── references/│   └── conversion-guide.md└── evals/    └── evals.json
```

然后在 Codex 里可以这样问：

```
Convert this Regular Alpha Fast Expression to Python Alpha core code without simulating:​ts_mean(group_neutralize(returns, sector), 20)
```

或者中文也可以：

```
把这个 Regular Alpha 转成 Python Alpha，不要跑回测：​trade_when(volume > ts_mean(volume, 20), rank(-returns), -1)
```

## 设计原则

- 官方 BRAIN API / Learn 文档优先；
- 本地 operator cache 只作为离线辅助和搜索提示；
- 不凭空编造平台内部 operator 的精确语义；
- Python Alpha 代码要自包含、可读、可审计；
- 缺失值不能随手变成 0；
- group operator 不能把缺失 group 当成有效分组；
- time-series over computed signal 要用 typed  `store` ；
- 最终输出必须  `.astype(np.float32)` 。

下面是完整源码。

## `SKILL.md`

```
---name: brain-regular-to-python-alphadescription: Convert a WorldQuant BRAIN Regular Alpha Fast Expression into a Python Alpha core implementation. Use this whenever the user provides a Regular Alpha or FASTEXPR expression and asks for a Python Alpha, Python version, conversion, rewrite, migration, or equivalent NumPy implementation. Query official BRAIN operator definitions first, preserve operator semantics, and return the @alpha-decorated core code without running simulations unless explicitly requested.---​# Convert Regular Alpha FastExpr to Python Alpha​Convert one Regular Alpha Fast Expression into Python Alpha code that follows the BRAIN Python Alpha execution model.​## Required reading​Read [references/conversion-guide.md](references/conversion-guide.md) before converting an expression.​For every operator used by the expression, use the official BRAIN API definitions as the authoritative source:​1. Authenticate with BRAIN MCP if needed.2. Call the BRAIN MCP `get_operators` tool.3. Extract the definitions and descriptions for the operators used by the expression.​Use the cached local operator lookup only as an auxiliary source for search hints, examples, or offline fallback:​```bashpython skills/brain-regular-to-python-alpha/scripts/operator_lookup.py operator_name
```

The local YAML knowledge files were AI-generated. Never treat them as authoritative when they conflict with the official BRAIN API or official Learn documentation. Forum posts are also supporting evidence only.

Do not invent exact semantics for platform-internal operators. Clearly label any approximation.

## Input

Expect a Regular Alpha Fast Expression. The user may also provide simulation settings or data-field metadata.

If settings are omitted, convert the code without simulating. Infer the minimum required Python  `lookback`  as the maximum raw historical window needed by the implementation and state it briefly after the code.

## Workflow

1. Parse assignments and the final expression. Preserve statement order and variable dependencies.
2. List data fields, group fields, vector fields, operators, named arguments, and maximum historical windows.
3. Classify each operator:
   - Element-wise arithmetic or logical operation.
   - Cross-sectional operation on today's  `[n_instruments]`  vector.
   - Group operation on today's signal and today's group vector.
   - Time-series operation over  `[time, n_instruments]` .
   - Stateful operation requiring  `store` .
   - Platform-internal operation that cannot be replicated exactly from published semantics.
4. Query official BRAIN MCP operator definitions for operators used by the expression. Use local files only as auxiliary references. If official documentation does not expose NaN behavior, parameter semantics, ranking convention, or state behavior, label the Python implementation as an approximation.
5. Write a self-contained core Python Alpha:
   - Include  `from brain.alphas import alpha` ,  `import numpy as np` , and  `import numpy.typing as npt` .
   - Include helper functions used by the Alpha.
   - Declare all required fields in  `@alpha(data=[...])` .
   - Declare typed store entries for cross-day state.
   - Return a one-dimensional  `np.float32`  array.
6. Review semantic edge cases against the checklist in the conversion guide.

## Output format

Return:

1. One Python code block containing the complete core Alpha definition.
2. One short  `Notes`  section listing:
   - Minimum required  `lookback` .
   - Any approximations or unresolved operator semantics.

Do not include BrainLabs setup, simulation calls, submission code, or backtest results unless the user asks for them.

## Quality rules

- Preserve semantics over brevity. Do not mechanically translate operator names.
- Implement each non-trivial FastExpr operator as a clearly named helper function by default, such as  `ts_zscore` ,  `ts_backfill` , or  `group_neutralize` . This keeps the mapping auditable. Inline only ordinary arithmetic such as unary minus, addition, subtraction, multiplication, and division.
- Use  `data.field[-1]`  only for operations that consume today's cross section. Keep a historical matrix for time-series operations.
- Prefer the provided raw  `data`  window for direct time-series operations. Use  `store`  when an intermediate computed signal must persist across days or when an operator is inherently stateful.
- Copy read-only data arrays before mutating them.
- Treat integer missing-value sentinels explicitly when integer fields participate in numerical calculations.
- Do not list  `universe`  in  `@alpha(data=[...])` ; use  `data.universe` .
- Cast the final output with  `.astype(np.float32)` .
- Do not simulate unless the user explicitly requests it.

```
​---​## `references/conversion-guide.md`​````markdown# FastExpr to Python Alpha Conversion Guide​## Execution model​BRAIN calls the decorated Python Alpha once per time step. Each declared `data.field` is a read-only array shaped `[available_history, n_instruments]`. The newest row is `data.field[-1]`. The function returns only today's signal as a one-dimensional `np.float32` array.​Use the raw history window where possible. For example, `ts_mean(returns, 20)` can use `np.nanmean(data.returns[-20:], axis=0)` when `returns` is a raw field. Use a typed `store` cache when a time-series operator wraps an intermediate value computed inside the Alpha, such as `ts_mean(group_rank(returns, sector), 20)`.​## Core template​```pythonfrom brain.alphas import alphaimport numpy as npimport numpy.typing as npt​​@alpha(    data=["returns"],    store=[],)def converted_alpha(data, store) -> npt.NDArray[np.float32]:    signal = -np.nanmean(data.returns[-20:], axis=0)    return signal.astype(np.float32)
```

## Operator implementation rules

Keep the generated code auditable: define one clearly named helper for each non-trivial FastExpr operator. Inline ordinary arithmetic such as unary minus, addition, subtraction, multiplication, and division. For example, prefer calling  `ts_zscore(filled, 66)`  over expanding the mean, standard deviation, and division directly inside the Alpha body.

### Element-wise operations

Translate ordinary arithmetic with NumPy, but preserve documented NaN behavior. Operators such as  `add(..., filter=true)`  and  `subtract(..., filter=true)`  require replacing NaNs before applying arithmetic. Do not silently use Python arithmetic when the FastExpr operator has extra arguments.

### Time-series operations

Time-series operators consume the trailing  `d`  rows along axis 0 and produce today's  `[n_instruments]`  vector. Preserve documented window parameters and named arguments.

Examples:

```
def ts_mean(x, d):    return np.nanmean(x[-d:], axis=0)​​def ts_delta(x, d):    if x.shape[0] <= d:        return np.full(x.shape[1], np.nan, dtype=np.float64)    return x[-1] - x[-(d + 1)]
```

Do not replace all time-series operators with simple NumPy reducers. Operators such as  `ts_rank` ,  `ts_quantile` ,  `ts_decay_linear` ,  `ts_regression` ,  `hump` , and  `trade_when`  need dedicated semantics.

### Computed intermediate history

When a time-series operator wraps a computed cross-sectional signal, cache the daily intermediate:

```
@alpha(    data=["returns", "sector"],    store=[{"name": "signal_history", "dims": "xi", "extend": np.float64(np.nan)}],)def converted_alpha(data, store) -> npt.NDArray[np.float32]:    today = data.returns[-1]    groups = data.sector[-1]    intermediate = group_neutralize(today, groups)​    if store.signal_history is None:        store.signal_history = intermediate[np.newaxis, :]    else:        store.signal_history = np.vstack(            [store.signal_history, intermediate[np.newaxis, :]]        )[-20:]​    return np.nanmean(store.signal_history, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
```

Use NumPy scalar constructors in typed store declarations, such as  `np.float64(np.nan)` . Do not use bare  `np.nan` ,  `0` , or  `0.0`  as  `extend`  values.

### Cross-sectional operations

Cross-sectional operators consume today's one-dimensional vector. Keep NaNs excluded from statistics and preserve them in the output unless the documented operator says otherwise.

```
def normalize(x, use_std=False, limit=0.0):    out = x.astype(np.float64, copy=True)    finite = np.isfinite(out)    if not finite.any():        return out    out[finite] -= np.mean(out[finite])    if use_std:        std = np.std(out[finite])        if std > 0:            out[finite] /= std    if limit > 0:        out[finite] = np.clip(out[finite], -limit, limit)    return out
```

Ranking conventions can affect exact equality. Query the operator definition and label the implementation as an approximation if tie handling or rate behavior is not published.

### Group operations

Use today's group vector, such as  `data.sector[-1]` . Missing group identifiers must remain excluded rather than becoming a real group. All-NaN groups remain NaN. Constant groups require operator-specific handling.

```
def group_neutralize(x, groups):    out = np.full(x.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)    valid_group = np.isfinite(groups)    for group in np.unique(groups[valid_group]):        mask = valid_group & (groups == group) & np.isfinite(x)        if mask.any():            out[mask] = x[mask] - np.mean(x[mask])    return out
```

Do not initialize group results with zeros. That incorrectly turns missing inputs and missing groups into tradable signals.

### Backfill semantics

The documented signature is  `ts_backfill(x, lookback=d, k=1)` . Respect both  `lookback`  and  `k` :  `k=1`  selects the nearest valid historical value, while larger values select the kth nearest valid historical value. A generic unlimited forward fill is not equivalent. Search raw historical values within the bounded prior window so that larger  `k`  values do not accidentally count recursively filled values as new observations.

When  `ts_backfill(x, backfill_days)`  is nested inside an outer time-series operator with  `outer_days` , retain  `backfill_days + outer_days`  rows of raw input. Because Python Alpha  `lookback`  counts extra rows beyond today, request at least  `backfill_days + outer_days - 1` .

### Stateful operators

Operators such as  `trade_when` ,  `hump` , and some turnover-targeting operators require previous outputs or platform-specific state. Use typed  `store`  entries where published semantics are sufficient. Otherwise describe the approximation explicitly.

### Integer fields

Integer arrays cannot store NaN. Convert integer missing-value sentinels before calculations:

```
def integer_field_to_float(x):    missing = np.iinfo(x.dtype).min    out = x.astype(np.float32)    out[x == missing] = np.nan    return out
```

### Per-share fields

Raw prices and per-share fundamentals may contain corporate-action discontinuities. When the expression uses these fields directly, inspect whether  `adjfactor`  handling is needed.  `returns`  is already split-adjusted.

## Review checklist

- Every FastExpr data field appears in  `@alpha(data=[...])` , except  `universe` .
- Every raw time-series operation uses enough history.
- Every time-series operation over a computed intermediate has a typed store cache.
- Named arguments such as  `filter` ,  `dense` ,  `constant` ,  `k` , and  `rettype`  are preserved.
- Missing values remain missing unless the documented operator changes them.
- Group fields use today's row and do not treat missing groups as valid categories.
- Any approximation is disclosed in  `Notes` .
- The final return value is one-dimensional and explicitly cast to  `np.float32` .

```
​---​## `scripts/operator_lookup.py`​```python#!/usr/bin/env python3"""Look up cached BRAIN operator definitions for offline conversion work."""​from __future__ import annotations​import jsonimport sysfrom pathlib import Path​​def main() -> int:    if len(sys.argv) != 2:        print("usage: operator_lookup.py OPERATOR_NAME", file=sys.stderr)        return 2​    operator_name = sys.argv[1]    repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]    knowledge_file = (        repo_root / "operator-analysis" / "knowledge" / "operators" / f"{operator_name}.yaml"    )    if knowledge_file.exists():        print(knowledge_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))        return 0​    operator_file = repo_root / "operator-analysis" / "brain_operators.json"    payload = json.loads(operator_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))​    for operator in payload.get("operators", []):        if operator.get("name") == operator_name:            print(json.dumps(operator, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))            return 0​    print(f"operator not found: {operator_name}", file=sys.stderr)    return 1​​if __name__ == "__main__":    raise SystemExit(main())```​---​## `evals/evals.json`​```json{  "skill_name": "brain-regular-to-python-alpha",  "evals": [    {      "id": 1,      "prompt": "Convert this Regular Alpha Fast Expression to Python Alpha core code without simulating: -ts_mean(returns, 20)",      "expected_output": "A self-contained @alpha core definition using the raw returns history window and returning float32.",      "files": []    },    {      "id": 2,      "prompt": "Convert this Regular Alpha Fast Expression to Python Alpha core code without simulating: ts_mean(group_neutralize(returns, sector), 20)",      "expected_output": "A self-contained @alpha core definition using today's returns and sector values, a typed xi store cache for computed intermediate history, correct missing-group handling, and a float32 return.",      "files": []    },    {      "id": 3,      "prompt": "Convert this Regular Alpha Fast Expression to Python Alpha core code without simulating: trade_when(volume > ts_mean(volume, 20), rank(-returns), -1)",      "expected_output": "A self-contained @alpha core definition with typed state for prior output, a documented handling choice for the exit condition, and a float32 return.",      "files": []    }  ]}```​---​## 补充说明​这个 skill 仍然是一个 Codex workflow 约束，不是一个能保证 100% 复刻平台内部实现的编译器。尤其是平台内部状态、ranking tie handling、部分 operator 的 NaN 细节，如果官方文档没有完全公开，就应该在输出里标注 approximation，然后人工复核。​我的实际用法是：先让它生成 Python Alpha core，再重点检查 helper function、`lookback`、`store`、缺失值处理和 group 处理。确认语义合理以后，再自己决定是否跑 simulation。​
```

---

### 评论 #22 (作者: ZH87224, 时间: 3个月前)

【Quant101】OpCount 拯救计划：因子化简

现在大家基本都用 AI 来挖因子了，AI 通常喜欢持续堆叠，哪里不对点哪里，有时候会导致因子复杂度急剧上升，通常表现在：

1. 过度堆叠字段，单因子 alpha0 拓展成组合因子：a0*alpha0 + a1*alpha1 + ...
2. 过度嵌套操作符，因子后面都没法看了 op(op(op(...)))
3. 防御性挖掘，AI通常喜欢加一些防御性的操作符，来保证计算的有效性，例如 abs() 保证为正，inverse(x + 0.01) 保证分母不为零，ts_backfill(winsorize(.)) 叠加保证因子有效等，但其实这些操作符是否有用，要看用到的数据字段本身的特点。

给大家分享一个可以在不改变因子表现的前提下，对因子进行化简的 skill，拯救六维中的 OpCount：

## SKILL.md

---

name: newt-simplify-alpha

description: >-

Simplifies WorldQuant BRAIN FastExpr alphas without changing their intended

economic meaning. Use when the user wants to reduce operator count, remove

redundant robustness wrappers, merge equivalent subexpressions, or compress

an already-working alpha while preserving submission quality.

---

# Alpha Simplification Workflow

This skill is for semantic-preserving simplification, not idea replacement.

Use it after a signal already makes economic sense and preferably already has a working baseline.

For detailed simplification heuristics and tested examples, see [reference.md](reference.md).

All proposed rewrites must remain valid FastExpr.

When writing or simplifying expressions, follow the syntax habits in `BRAIN_Consultant_Starter_Handbook.md` and verify operator usage against `squadb/knowledge/BRAIN Platform Operators ALL.md`.

## Core Principle

Keep the signal idea unchanged:

- Same economic story

- Same primary fields

- Same ranking / exposure intent

- Similar or better submission profile

If a change alters directionality, timing, coverage logic, or risk budget in a meaningful way, treat it as an alpha redesign, not simplification.

Also remember that operator count is a real computational budget:

- all operators count, including arithmetic, comparison, and boolean operators

- repeating the same operator expression multiple times increases count multiple times

- simplification is not only about deleting wrappers, but also about rewriting the same logic with fewer operator evaluations

When operator syntax or arity is unclear, check `squadb/knowledge/BRAIN Platform Operators ALL.md`.

If a simplification is mathematically neat but awkward, unsupported, or ambiguous in FastExpr syntax, reject it.

## Step 1: Lock the Baseline

Fetch baseline stats before simplifying:

- `get_alpha`

- `get_submit_check`

- `calc_self_corr`

- `get_prod_corr`

- `get_yearly_stats` when stability matters

Record:

- original expression

- operator count estimate

- Sharpe / Fitness / Turnover

- current fails or warnings

## Step 2: Generate Simplification Candidates

Prefer 4-8 tightly scoped variants rather than one big rewrite.

Each candidate should remove only one logical class of complexity at a time unless a pair is obviously linked.

Write candidates in valid FastExpr form, not pseudo-code.

Good candidate classes:

- remove sign-invariant wrappers

- merge linear subexpressions

- remove redundant robustness operators

- remove cosmetic sub-signal weights

- remove denominator safety constants when truly unnecessary

- test removing `ts_backfill` even though it does not count toward operator count

- extract repeated subexpressions into temporary variables

- replace nested binary aggregation with one multi-input operator when supported

## Step 3: Classify Changes by Safety

Use two buckets:

### A. Verified-safe simplifications

These are simplifications whose semantics are structurally preserved and also confirmed by simulation.

Examples:

- removing `reverse(x)` inside `abs(...)`

- removing `reverse(x)` when the next meaningful operator is sign-invariant, such as `ts_std_dev`

- merging obviously linear pieces when platform behavior remains unchanged in tests

### B. Empirical simplifications

These are changes that often preserve intent but may change NaN handling, cross-sectional shape, or tail behavior.

Examples:

- removing `winsorize`

- removing `abs`

- removing sub-signal weights

- removing denominator protection constants

- removing `ts_backfill`

Treat these as candidate variants, not identities.

## Step 4: Simulate in Small Batches

Use `simulate` to test candidates under the original production settings unless the simplification itself requires a special diagnostic run.

Compare against baseline on:

- Sharpe

- Fitness

- Turnover

- concentration / sub-universe behavior

- self correlation

- prod correlation

Do not accept a lower-operator version automatically.

Prefer the simplest expression that still preserves stable PnL and economic sense.

## Step 5: Validate Submission Quality

For the top 1-2 candidates:

- run `get_submit_check`

- run `calc_self_corr`

- run `get_prod_corr`

- check `get_yearly_stats` if the baseline was yearly-sensitive

If two variants are close, prefer the one with:

- fewer warnings

- cleaner unit behavior

- simpler explanation

- more stable yearly profile

## Step 6: Report the Outcome Clearly

Summarize simplification in three layers:

1. safest accepted simplification

2. more aggressive simplification that still worked empirically

3. simplifications tested but rejected

Always state whether a candidate is:

- mathematically/structurally safe

- only empirically acceptable

- rejected because it changed behavior

## Best Practices

- Keep the simplification cycle short: usually 1-2 rounds.

- Remove one complexity source at a time before combining removals.

- Do not sacrifice stable PnL just to save one operator.

- If a robustness wrapper is removed, verify that weight concentration and sub-universe behavior did not quietly worsen.

- Use `newtmcp` for all platform interaction.

- When in doubt, preserve economic clarity over minimal operator count.

- If `op(field)` appears repeatedly, compute it once, assign it to a variable, and reuse the variable.

- Prefer operators with unlimited inputs such as `add(f1, f2, f3)` over chained binary forms when the logic is the same.

- Keep every rewrite compatible with FastExpr punctuation, operator signatures, and statement style from `BRAIN_Consultant_Starter_Handbook.md`.

## reference.md

# Simplifying BRAIN Alphas Without Changing Meaning

This reference collects high-value simplification patterns for FastExpr alphas.

Use it after reading [SKILL.md](SKILL.md).

## 0. Syntax Constraint

Every simplification must still be expressible in valid FastExpr.

Before accepting a rewrite, confirm:

- the expression follows FastExpr syntax conventions from `BRAIN_Consultant_Starter_Handbook.md`

- the operator exists in `squadb/knowledge/BRAIN Platform Operators ALL.md`

- the operator arity matches FastExpr support

- any temporary-variable rewrite still ends with a valid final output expression

Do not return pseudo-code, mathematical shorthand, or unsupported rewrite patterns as final recommendations.

## 1. How Operator Counting Really Works

In this project, count operators aggressively:

- arithmetic operators count

- comparison operators count

- boolean operators count

- each extra operator means one more computation

- repeating the same subexpression repeats the operator cost

This means simplification is often about expression layout, not just removing wrappers.

Two high-value rules:

### 1.1 Extract repeated subexpressions

If the same transform appears multiple times, compute it once and reuse it through a temporary variable.

Example:

```fastexpr

x = op(field);

add(x, multiply(x, y))

```

is often better than recomputing `op(field)` in multiple places.

Use this whenever:

- `rank(field)` appears more than once

- `ts_delta(field, d)` appears more than once

- a shared preprocessed signal feeds multiple branches

### 1.2 Prefer multi-input operators when available

For operators that accept unlimited inputs, use the one-call form.

Example:

- `add(f1, f2, f3)` counts once

- `f1 + f2 + f3` effectively creates multiple operations

The same idea often applies to other multi-input operators such as `multiply`, `max`, and `min` when supported.

Always verify exact operator support in `squadb/knowledge/BRAIN Platform Operators ALL.md`.

## 2. `abs` Can Sometimes Be Removed

Ask why `abs` is there:

- Is the input already non-negative by construction?

- Is the downstream operator sign-invariant?

- Was `abs` added only as a generic robustness wrapper?

Good removal cases:

- `abs(x)` where `x` is already non-negative

- `reverse(abs(x))` when only magnitude matters downstream

- `abs(...)` before a sign-invariant dispersion operator, if sign information is already irrelevant

Risk:

- Removing `abs` can change long/short direction or make tails asymmetric.

- Treat most `abs` removals as empirical unless non-negativity is obvious.

## 3. `winsorize` May Be Redundant

`winsorize` is often used defensively, but it is not always needed.

Good times to test removal:

- downstream is `rank`, `group_rank`, `quantile`, or another operator that already compresses tails

- truncation / neutralization / ranking already keeps exposure well-behaved

- baseline has no weight concentration problem

What to re-check after removal:

- concentration warning

- sub-universe Sharpe

- yearly drawdown shape

Do not assume it is safe just because the final operator is a rank.

## 4. Denominator Safety Constants

Examples include:

- `divide(x, y + 0.1)`

- `inverse(max(y, 0.1))`

- `divide(x, max(abs(y), 0.01))`

These often exist only to avoid zero denominators.

You may test removing or shrinking them when:

- the denominator field is structurally positive

- the denominator is never near zero in practice

- the constant is large enough to distort the true signal

High caution:

- This can materially change scale and tail behavior.

- Prefer test variants such as remove / shrink / replace with milder guard.

## 5. Sub-Signal Weights Can Be Cosmetic

Weighted sums are common:

- `add(multiply(a, 0.6), multiply(b, 0.4))`

- `a * 2 + b`

If the result is later ranked, group-ranked, normalized, or neutralized, exact weights may not matter much.

Good tests:

- drop all weights to equal-weight form

- remove only clearly cosmetic multipliers

- preserve signs first, then test removing magnitude differences

Risk:

- Weights sometimes encode real preference, timing, or turnover control.

- If a weight controls a hedge leg or volatility leg, do not remove it casually.

## 6. `ts_backfill` Does Not Count, But Still Deserves Testing

Even though `ts_backfill` does not increase operator count, it can still hurt signal quality.

Always consider two variants:

- keep `ts_backfill`

- remove `ts_backfill`

Why removal can help:

- stale values may pollute fast changes

- backfilled data may create fake persistence

- missingness itself may contain information

Why removal can hurt:

- coverage drops

- long/short counts collapse

- sub-universe performance weakens

This is usually an empirical choice, not a semantic identity.

## 7. Linear and Sign-Invariant Simplifications

These are the first places to look for safe reductions.

Examples:

- `reverse(abs(x)) -> abs(x)`

- `ts_std_dev(reverse(x), d) -> ts_std_dev(x, d)`

- `subtract(ts_delta(a, 1), ts_delta(b, 1)) -> ts_delta(subtract(a, b), 1)` only after simulation confirms behavior

- if `op(field)` is reused across branches, assign it once before combining branches

- if a sum or product is just an aggregation, prefer one multi-input call over chained binary calls

Important:

- Some transforms are mathematically equivalent but not platform-equivalent in practice because of NaN handling, units, or operator implementation details.

- Treat any algebraic merge as verified-safe only after a simulation check.

## 8. Recommended Testing Order

Use this order because it gives the best chance of saving operators without accidentally changing the alpha:

1. remove obvious `reverse`

2. remove `abs` only when sign is provably irrelevant

3. merge linear terms

4. test `winsorize` removal

5. test `ts_backfill` removal

6. test denominator guard removal or shrinkage

7. test weight removal

## 9. Practical Acceptance Rules

Accept a simplification when:

- economic meaning is unchanged

- Sharpe / Fitness stay similar or improve

- no new concentration or sub-universe failures appear

- self/prod corr do not become materially worse

Reject or downgrade a simplification when:

- metrics fall noticeably

- yearly profile becomes less stable

- it introduces new warnings

- the expression becomes harder to explain despite fewer operators

## 10. Example From This Project

Original optimized form:

`group_rank(multiply(ts_std_dev(winsorize(ts_backfill(reverse(abs(subtract(ts_delta(f, 1), ts_delta(f, 1)))), 66), std=4), 22), less(rank(abs(ts_delta(f, 1))), 0.97)), subindustry)`

Verified-safe simplification:

`group_rank(multiply(ts_std_dev(winsorize(ts_backfill(abs(subtract(ts_delta(f, 1), ts_delta(f,f1))), 66), std=4), 22), less(rank(abs(ts_delta(f, 1))), 0.97)), subindustry)`

What changed:

- removed `reverse(...)` because it sat inside `abs(...)`

More aggressive empirical simplification that also worked in this case:

`group_rank(multiply(ts_std_dev(winsorize(ts_backfill(abs(ts_delta(subtract(f, f), 1)), 66), std=4), 22), less(rank(abs(ts_delta(f, 1))), 0.97)), subindustry)`

What changed:

- merged two `ts_delta` branches into one delta of a spread

Important project lesson:

- the same algebraic merge applied to the original unsimplified baseline changed metrics materially

- so even mathematically neat reductions must be verified on-platform

### 评论 #23 (作者: ZH87224, 时间: 3个月前)

【Quant101】FieldPer 拯救计划：Skill 围栏

最近参考 cnhkmcp 里的 improve-alpha-performance skill 开发了一个因子优化的 skill，用GEM+一二三阶挖出有信号的因子后，就丢给skill优化，优化目标是满足 RA 提交标准，但是用 AI 来挖因子有个问题，AI喜欢无限堆叠信号，往往一个 2Field 4Op 的因子最后虽然优化成功了，但是优化出来 8Field, 40Op （没错，我挖出好几个40+Op的因子，闹麻了）

经过一番研究，发现解决方案也很简单，就是给 skill 加上如下限制 prompt：

## Iteration and Best Practices

- **Cycle Limit**: 3-5 per alpha; pivot if stuck (e.g., new datafield).

- **Tracking**: Maintain a log (e.g., MD file with iterations, metrics deltas).

- **Efficiency**: Use parallel tools; focus 70% on ideas, 30% on tweaks.

- **Success Criteria**: Passing checks + stable yearly stats.

- **Keep Simple and Focus**: Final alpha's fields should less then 4, operators count should less than 8.

主要是最后一条：Keep Simple and  Focus。

这句话放在 Skill 里或者 Reference 里都可以，效果立竿见影

1
