这个的思路就是：先分析字段的经济学含义，然后分析策略的意思，找出存在的不足，针对这个进行科学的去分析

我用的模型是Claude 4.6
name: youhua
description: >-
  对指定 Alpha 进行多轮自动优化循环。自动完成：
  诊断分析 → 修复错误 → 生成候选表达式 → 批量回测 → 对比结果 → 检查提交条件。
  当因子达到可提交标准且生产相关性≤0.7时，自动提交并通知用户。
  每轮结束后展示对比表格，由用户决定是否继续下一轮。
  触发词：优化因子、优化Alpha、improve alpha、optimize alpha loop
---

# Alpha 优化循环（自动提交版）

输入 Alpha ID，执行多轮优化迭代，目标是修复错误并优化到可提交标准。
当因子符合提交条件且生产相关性≤0.7时，自动提交。

## 使用方式

```
/youhua <alpha_id>
```

例如：
```
/youhua GromWnvZ
```

---

## Step 0：读取输入

从 args 中解析：
- `alpha_id`：第一个词

优化方向固定为：**修复错误，优化到可提交标准**

---

## Step 1：诊断原始因子

并行执行以下查询：

```bash
python "C:\Users\33146\.claude\brain_cli.py" alpha --id <alpha_id> --output alpha_details.json
python "C:\Users\33146\.claude\brain_cli.py" yearly-stats --id <alpha_id> --output yearly_stats.json
python "C:\Users\33146\.claude\brain_cli.py" submission-check --id <alpha_id> --output submission_check.json
python "C:\Users\33146\.claude\brain_cli.py" check-correlation --id <alpha_id> --output correlation_check.json
```

读取结果，提取：
- `regular.code`：当前表达式
- `is.sharpe`、`is.fitness`、`is.turnover`、`is.margin`：核心指标
- `is.pnl`：Train/Test 分段
- 年度 Sharpe/Fitness 趋势
- 提交检查失败项
- **生产相关性**：检查是否≤0.7

输出简要诊断摘要：
```
## 原始因子诊断
- 表达式：...
- Sharpe: X.XX | Fitness: X.XX | Turnover: XX% | Margin: X.X‱
- 年度趋势：[上升/下降/震荡]
- 生产相关性：X.XX [通过/超标]
- 提交障碍：[列出失败项，若无则"通过"]
- 核心问题：一句话总结最大短板
```

---

## Step 2：生成候选表达式

基于诊断结果，生成 **5-10 个**候选表达式。

生成原则：
1. **优先修复提交检查中的错误项**
2. **针对性优化低指标**（低 Sharpe、高 turnover、弱 margin 等）
3. **保留原始信号结构**，只做局部改造（不要全部推倒重来）
4. **多样化策略**：参数微调、算子替换、结构组合各占一部分
5. **不要混信号**：单一信号原则，不堆叠不相关数据集

输出格式：
```
## 第 N 轮候选（共 K 个）

| # | 表达式 | 改动说明 | 预期方向 |
|---|--------|----------|----------|
| 1 | rank(ts_delta(close,5)) | 缩短回看窗口降低turnover | turnover↓ |
| 2 | ... | ... | ... |
```

---

## Step 3：批量回测

使用以下命令提交全部候选：

```bash
python "C:\Users\33146\.claude\brain_cli.py" multi-simulate \
  --codes "expr1,expr2,expr3" \
  --region <同原始因子> \
  --universe <同原始因子> \
  --delay <同原始因子> \
  --neutralization <同原始因子> \
  --decay <同原始因子> \
  --truncation <同原始因子> \
  --output round_N_results.json
```

等待回测完成，读取 `round_N_results.json`。

---

## Step 4：对比分析

读取回测结果，与原始因子对比，输出对比表：

```
## 第 N 轮回测结果

| # | Alpha ID | 表达式摘要 | Sharpe | Fitness | Turnover | Margin | 较原始 |
|---|----------|-----------|--------|---------|----------|--------|--------|
| 原始 | <id> | ... | 1.76 | 1.56 | 35% | 12‱ | — |
| v1 | <id> | ... | 1.89 | 1.72 | 28% | 14‱ | ✅+7% |
| v2 | <id> | ... | 1.65 | 1.48 | 40% | 10‱ | ❌-6% |
| ... |

### 本轮最优候选
- **Alpha ID**：xxx
- **表达式**：...
- **亮点**：Sharpe+0.13，Turnover-7%
- **缺陷**：Margin 略低

### 建议
- 值得继续优化的候选：v1, v3
- 下一轮方向建议：在 v1 基础上进一步降低 Turnover
```

---

## Step 5：检查提交条件并自动提交

对本轮最优候选执行提交条件检查：

```bash
python "C:\Users\33146\.claude\brain_cli.py" submission-check --id <best_alpha_id> --output check_<best_alpha_id>.json
python "C:\Users\33146\.claude\brain_cli.py" check-correlation --id <best_alpha_id> --output corr_<best_alpha_id>.json
```

判断逻辑：
1. **提交检查通过**（submission-check 无失败项）
2. **生产相关性≤0.7**

若同时满足以上两个条件，则自动提交：

```bash
python "C:\Users\33146\.claude\_submit_alpha.py" <best_alpha_id>
```

提交结果处理：
- **成功**：输出 `✅ 因子 ID <alpha_id> 已成功提交！`
- **失败**：输出错误信息，继续询问是否继续优化

若不满足提交条件，输出原因并继续下一步。

---

## Step 6：询问是否继续

```
是否继续优化？
- 输入 Y 或直接描述新方向 → 以本轮最优为基础，进入下一轮
- 输入 N 或"停止" → 结束，输出最终总结
- 输入具体 Alpha ID → 切换到该候选继续优化
```

若继续，回到 **Step 1**，以本轮最优候选为新的 `alpha_id`，重复循环。

---

## Step 7：最终总结（退出时输出）

```
## 优化总结

- 起始因子：<原始 alpha_id>，Sharpe=X.XX，Fitness=X.XX
- 优化轮数：N 轮
- 最终最优：<best_alpha_id>，Sharpe=X.XX（+X.XX），Fitness=X.XX（+X.XX）
- 提交状态：✅已自动提交 / ❌未提交（原因）

### 各轮最优对比
| 轮次 | Alpha ID | Sharpe | Fitness | Turnover | 提交状态 |
|------|----------|--------|---------|----------|----------|
| 0（原始）| ... | ... | ... | ... | — |
| 1 | ... | ... | ... | ... | — |
| N | ... | ... | ... | ... | ✅已提交 |
```

---

## Step 8：清理垃圾文件

任务完成后，自动清理运行过程中的临时文件，**保留优化思路文档**。

保留文件（优化思路记录）：
- `optimization_summary.md`：完整优化总结
- `round_N_input.json`：每轮输入的候选表达式
- 任何以 `_summary` 或 `_plan` 结尾的文件

删除文件（运行日志和临时数据）：
- `*_details.json`：Alpha 详情
- `*_results.json`：回测结果
- `check_*.json`：提交检查
- `corr_*.json`：相关性检查
- `yearly_stats.json`：年度统计
- `field_*.json`：字段查询
- `fields_*.json`：字段列表
- 其他临时 JSON 文件

执行清理：
```bash
cd "C:\Users\Administrator\.claude\skills\youhua"
# 删除详情文件
rm -f *_details.json
# 删除结果文件
rm -f *_results.json round_*_results.json
# 删除检查文件
rm -f check_*.json corr_*.json submission_check.json
# 删除统计文件
rm -f yearly_stats.json alpha_pnl.json
# 删除字段查询
rm -f field_*.json fields_*.json
```

清理完成后输出：
```
🧹 已清理临时文件，保留优化思路文档
```

---

## 重要规则

- **自动提交条件**：当因子满足提交检查且生产相关性≤0.7时，自动提交
- **提交通知**：提交成功后输出 `✅ 因子 ID <alpha_id> 已成功提交！`
- **回测设置继承**：所有回测参数与原始因子完全一致，不自行更改
- **中文输出**：全程使用简体中文
- **数据驱动**：所有结论基于实际回测数据，不猜测
- **每轮最多10个候选**：避免一次提交过多
- **优化目标**：修复错误并优化到可提交标准，不追求极致性能

---

### 评论 #25 (作者: JC25837, 时间: 3个月前)

分享一套BRAIN 数据字段智能分类方法论

一、为什么需要字段分类？

1.1 核心痛点

在 BRAIN 平台上，一个典型的 Region×Delay 组合可能包含：
- **200+ 数据集**（USA-D1 实测 235 个）
- **数万个字段**（仅 fundamental6 就有 886 个）
- **海量表达式空间**（每个字段可组合上百种算子）

**没有分类系统的后果**：
- ❌ 盲目尝试，浪费大量仿真额度
- ❌ 重复使用拥挤字段，self-corr 超阈值
- ❌ 错过高价值冷门字段
- ❌ 无法系统化点亮金字塔

1.2 分类的价值

✅ **提高效率**：快速定位目标字段，减少 80% 无效尝试 
✅ **降低拥挤**：识别冷门高价值区域
✅ **战略规划**：系统化覆盖金字塔维度 
✅ **知识沉淀**：建立可复用的字段库

---

二、五维分类体系

维度 1：技术可用性分类（Technical）

**目的**：判断字段是否"能用"

分类标准

| 类别 | 定义 | 判断指标 |
|------|------|----------|
| **A级-即用型** | 覆盖高、更新频繁、分布良好 | coverage≥0.7, 非零比例≥0.8, 日/周频更新 |
| **B级-条件型** | 需预处理或特定场景 | coverage 0.4~0.7, 季频更新, 需 winsorize |
| **C级-事件型** | 稀疏但有效 | coverage<0.4, 但非零时信号强 |
| **D级-不可用** | 缺失严重或无变化 | coverage<0.2, 或 ts_std_dev=0 |

快速检测表达式

```python
 覆盖率检测
datafield   看 Long+Short Count

非零比例
datafield != 0 ? 1 : 0

更新频率
ts_std_dev(datafield, 22) != 0 ? 1 : 0

数据范围
abs(datafield) > 1
abs(datafield) > 100

分布检查
0.4 < scale_down(datafield) && scale_down(datafield) < 0.6
```

---

维度 2：金融语义分类（Semantic）

**目的**：理解字段的经济含义

六大语义类别

| 类别 | 典型字段 | 经济直觉 | Alpha 模板 |
|------|----------|----------|------------|
| **估值类** | PE, PB, EV/EBITDA | 低估值→均值回归 | `-rank(field)` |
| **盈利质量类** | ROE, 利润率, 现金流 | 高质量→持续超额收益 | `rank(field)` |
| **成长类** | 收入增长率, EPS增长 | 高成长→动量延续 | `ts_delta(field, 22)` |
| **动量/反转类** | Returns, 技术指标 | 短期反转/中期动量 | `-ts_delta(close, 5)` |
| **流动性类** | Volume, Turnover | 异常流动性→价格发现 | `zscore(volume) - ts_mean(zscore(volume), 20)` |
| **情绪类** | Analyst rating, News sentiment | 预期修正→价格调整 | `ts_delta(sentiment, 5)` |

分类方法

1. **阅读字段 description**（MCP 接口返回）
2. **参考 researchPapers**（数据集元数据）
3. **使用 brain-explain-alphas skill** 验证理解
4. **一句话测试**："该字段越大，股票倾向于____，因为____"

---

维度 3：拥挤度分类（Crowding）

**目的**：避开红海，寻找蓝海

拥挤度等级表

| 等级 | userCount | alphaCount | alphaCount/fieldCount | 策略 |
|------|-----------|------------|----------------------|------|
| **极拥挤** | >10,000 | >100,000 | >500 | ⚠️ 必须深度变换 |
| **高拥挤** | 2,000~10,000 | 20,000~100,000 | 100~500 | ⚡ 需要创新组合 |
| **中等** | 500~2,000 | 5,000~20,000 | 50~100 | ✅ 性价比中等 |
| **冷门** | <500 | <5,000 | <50 | 🌟 优先开发 |
| **全新** | 0 | 0 | 0 | 💎 最大机会 |

实测案例（USA-D1-TOP3000）

```
fundamental6.assets: userCount=49,053, alphaCount=151,308 → 极拥挤
fundamental6.obscure_ratio: userCount=127, alphaCount=342 → 冷门
```

降拥挤策略矩阵

| 拥挤度 | 必须做 | 推荐做 |
|--------|--------|--------|
| 极拥挤 | 行业中性化 + 非线性变换 | 与冷门字段融合 |
| 高拥挤 | 时序变换 + 分桶激活 | Jaccard 去重验证 |
| 中等 | 基础变换（rank/zscore） | 子域分组处理 |
| 冷门/全新 | 朴素表达式即可 | 快速占领赛道 |

---

维度 4：金字塔战略分类（Pyramid）

**目的**：系统化点亮金字塔，提升 QualityFactor

金字塔三要素

```
Pyramid = Region × Delay × Category
```

按 Category 分类字段

| Category | 典型数据集 | 字段特征 | pyramidMultiplier 范围 |
|----------|------------|----------|------------------------|
| **Fundamental** | fundamental6, financial_health | 季频, coverage中等 | 1.0~1.5 |
| **Analyst** | analyst_ratings, consensus | 月频, 小盘缺失 | 1.3~1.8 |
| **PriceVolume** | close, volume, vwap | 日频, 高覆盖 | 1.0~1.2 |
| **News** | news_sentiment, event | 事件型, 稀疏 | 1.5~2.0 |
| **Alternative** | social_media, satellite | 创新型, 新 | 1.7~2.5 |

战略选择矩阵

**目标**：优先点亮高乘数 + 低拥挤的 Category

```python
 计算战略价值
strategic_value = pyramidMultiplier * (1 - crowding_score)

示例
Analyst (1.5 × 0.7) = 1.05  ← 优先
PriceVolume (1.1 × 0.2) = 0.88  ← 次优
```

---

维度 5：Alpha 表现分类（Performance）

**目的**：从历史表现预测未来潜力

五档表现分类

| 档位 | 朴素 Sharpe | Fitness | 鲁棒性 | 建议 |
|------|-------------|---------|--------|------|
| **S级-金矿** | ≥2.0 | ≥1.5 | 各档设置浮动<20% | 立即深度开发 |
| **A级-优质** | 1.5~2.0 | 1.2~1.5 | 浮动<30% | 重点投入 |
| **B级-合格** | 1.25~1.5 | 1.0~1.2 | 浮动<50% | 常规开发 |
| **C级-边缘** | 1.0~1.25 | 0.8~1.0 | 不稳定 | 需复杂变换 |
| **D级-无效** | <1.0 | <0.8 | N/A | 放弃 |

冒烟测试套件（必跑5式）

```python
 Test 1: 反转/动量
-ts_delta(field, 5)
-ts_delta(field, 22)

Test 2: 横截面 rank
rank(field)
-rank(field)

Test 3: 时序 zscore
ts_zscore(field, 20)

Test 4: 行业中性
group_neutralize(rank(field), subindustry)

Test 5: 分桶激活
if_else(rank(field) > 0.8, 1, if_else(rank(field) < 0.2, -1, 0))
```

**判断规则**：任一表达式 Sharpe≥1.25 → 字段有信号

鲁棒性检测矩阵

| 测试维度 | 变化范围 | 合格标准 |
|----------|----------|----------|
| Neutralization | None/INDUSTRY/SUBINDUSTRY/MARKET | Sharpe 浮动<30% |
| Decay | 0/4/10/22 | Sharpe 浮动<20% |
| Truncation | 0/0.05/0.10 | Sharpe 浮动<25% |
| Universe | TOP3000/TOP1000/TOP500 | Sharpe 浮动<40% |
| Sub-universe | 大盘/中盘/小盘 | Sharpe 差异<50% |

---

三、自动化分类工作流

3.1 工作流架构图

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Phase 0: 目标定义                                       │
│  输入：Region × Delay × Universe × Category              │
│  输出：明确的金字塔坐标（如 USA-D1-TOP3000-Analyst）      │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                 │
                 ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Phase 1: 数据采集                                       │
│  - MCP/CLI 拉取数据集列表（datasets API）                │
│  - 过滤目标 Region/Delay/Universe                        │
│  - 拉取每个数据集的字段列表（datafields API）            │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                 │
                 ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Phase 2: 多维度分类                                     │
│  - 技术可用性：6步法表达式批量跑                         │
│  - 金融语义：description + researchPapers 解析          │
│  - 拥挤度：userCount/alphaCount 计算                    │
│  - 金字塔：themes + pyramidMultiplier 映射              │
│  - Alpha表现：冒烟测试5式批量仿真                       │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                 │
                 ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Phase 3: 综合评分与排序                                 │
│  Score = f(Technical, Semantic, Crowding, Pyramid, Perf) │
│  输出：字段优先级列表 Top 20                             │
└────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────┘
                 │
                 ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Phase 4: 归档与知识沉淀                                 │
│  - 生成字段分类报告（markdown）                          │
│  - 更新字段库（JSON/CSV）                               │
│  - 记录经验教训（operations-log）                       │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

3.2 核心代码模板（Python）

```python
import json
import subprocess
from typing import List, Dict

========== Step 1: 数据采集 ==========
def fetch_datasets(region: str, delay: int, universe: str) -> List[Dict]:
    """拉取数据集列表"""
    cmd = [
        "python", "brain_cli.py", "datasets",
        "--region", region,
        "--delay", str(delay),
        "--universe", universe,
        "--limit", "50",
        "--output", "datasets.json"
    ]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)

    with open("datasets.json") as f:
        return json.load(f)["results"]

def fetch_datafields(dataset_id: str, region: str, delay: int) -> List[Dict]:
    """拉取字段列表"""
    cmd = [
        "python", "brain_cli.py", "datafields",
        "--region", region,
        "--delay", str(delay),
        "--dataset", dataset_id,
        "--limit", "50",
        "--output", f"fields_{dataset_id}.json"
    ]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)

    with open(f"fields_{dataset_id}.json") as f:
        return json.load(f)["results"]

========== Step 2: 技术分类 ==========
def classify_technical(field: Dict) -> str:
    """技术可用性分类"""
    coverage = field.get("coverage", 0)

    if coverage >= 0.7:
        return "A级-即用型"
    elif coverage >= 0.4:
        return "B级-条件型"
    elif coverage >= 0.2:
        return "C级-事件型"
    else:
        return "D级-不可用"

========== Step 3: 拥挤度分类 ==========
def classify_crowding(field: Dict) -> str:
    """拥挤度分类"""
    user_count = field.get("userCount", 0)
    alpha_count = field.get("alphaCount", 0)

    if user_count > 10000 or alpha_count > 100000:
        return "极拥挤"
    elif user_count > 2000 or alpha_count > 20000:
        return "高拥挤"
    elif user_count > 500 or alpha_count > 5000:
        return "中等"
    elif user_count > 0:
        return "冷门"
    else:
        return "全新"

========== Step 4: 综合评分 ==========
def calculate_score(field: Dict, dataset: Dict) -> float:
    """综合评分函数"""
     技术得分
    coverage = field.get("coverage", 0)
    tech_score = coverage * 2

     拥挤度得分（反向）
    alpha_count = field.get("alphaCount", 0)
    field_count = dataset.get("fieldCount", 1)
    crowding = alpha_count / max(field_count, 1)
    crowd_score = max(0, 3 - crowding / 1000)

     金字塔得分
    pyramid_mult = dataset.get("pyramidMultiplier", 1.0)
    pyramid_score = pyramid_mult * 3

     综合
    total = tech_score + crowd_score + pyramid_score
    return round(total, 2)

========== Step 5: 主流程 ==========
def main_workflow(region: str, delay: int, universe: str):
    """完整分类工作流"""

     1. 采集数据集
    print(f"[Phase 1] 采集 {region}-D{delay}-{universe} 数据集...")
    datasets = fetch_datasets(region, delay, universe)
    print(f"找到 {len(datasets)} 个数据集")

     2. 遍历数据集，分类字段
    all_fields = []
    for ds in datasets[:10]:   限制前10个数据集以节省时间
        ds_id = ds["id"]
        print(f"[Phase 2] 分析数据集 {ds_id}...")

        fields = fetch_datafields(ds_id, region, delay)

        for field in fields:
            classified = {
                "dataset": ds_id,
                "field_id": field["id"],
                "field_name": field.get("name", "N/A"),
                "technical": classify_technical(field),
                "crowding": classify_crowding(field),
                "pyramid_mult": ds.get("pyramidMultiplier", 1.0),
                "score": calculate_score(field, ds),
                "coverage": field.get("coverage", 0),
                "user_count": field.get("userCount", 0),
                "alpha_count": field.get("alphaCount", 0),
            }
            all_fields.append(classified)

     3. 排序输出 Top 20
    all_fields.sort(key=lambda x: x["score"], reverse=True)
    top20 = all_fields[:20]

    print("\n[Phase 3] Top 20 优先字段：")
    print("=" * 100)
    for i, field in enumerate(top20, 1):
        print(f"{i:2d}. {field['dataset']}.{field['field_name']} | "
              f"技术:{field['technical']} | 拥挤:{field['crowding']} | "
              f"得分:{field['score']}")

     4. 保存结果
    with open("field_classification_report.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump(all_fields, f, indent=2)

    print("\n[Phase 4] 分类报告已保存到 field_classification_report.json")

运行
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main_workflow("USA", 1, "TOP3000")
```

---

四、AI 提示词模板

4.1 通用分类提示词

```
你是 WorldQuant BRAIN 平台的数据字段分类专家。请对以下字段进行五维分类：

**字段信息**：
- Dataset ID: {dataset_id}
- Field ID: {field_id}
- Description: {description}
- Coverage: {coverage}
- UserCount: {user_count}
- AlphaCount: {alpha_count}
- PyramidMultiplier: {pyramid_multiplier}

**分类要求**：
1. **技术可用性**：根据 coverage、更新频率判断为 A/B/C/D 级
2. **金融语义**：归类为估值/盈利/成长/动量/流动性/情绪，并给出一句话经济直觉
3. **拥挤度**：根据 userCount 和 alphaCount 判断为极拥挤/高拥挤/中等/冷门/全新
4. **金字塔战略**：评估 pyramidMultiplier 和稀缺性，给出战略优先级（高/中/低）
5. **推荐表达式**：基于以上分析，给出3个朴素表达式用于冒烟测试

**输出格式**（JSON）：
{
  "technical_class": "A级-即用型",
  "semantic_class": "估值类",
  "economic_intuition": "PE越低，股票被低估，未来有均值回归空间",
  "crowding_class": "高拥挤",
  "strategic_priority": "中",
  "recommended_expressions": [
    "-rank(field)",
    "group_neutralize(rank(field), industry)",
    "ts_zscore(field, 20)"
  ],
  "risk_notes": "该字段极度拥挤，必须配合行业中性化和非线性变换"
}
```

4.2 批量分类提示词

```
你是 BRAIN 字段批量分类助手。我提供一个数据集的所有字段元数据（JSON），你需要：

**任务**：
1. 对每个字段执行五维分类（技术/语义/拥挤/金字塔/表现）
2. 计算综合评分：Score = coverage*2 + (1-crowding)*3 + pyramid_mult*3
3. 输出 Top 20 优先级字段列表

**输入数据**：
{fields_json}

**输出格式**（Markdown 表格）：
| 排名 | 字段名 | 技术 | 语义 | 拥挤度 | 评分 | 推荐表达式 |
|------|--------|------|------|--------|------|-----------|
| 1 | fundamental6.roa | A级 | 盈利质量 | 冷门 | 8.5 | rank(field) |
| ... | ... | ... | ... | ... | ... | ... |

**额外要求**：
- 标注出"全新"字段（alphaCount=0）作为最高优先级
- 标注出"极拥挤"字段并给出降拥挤策略
- 给出数据集整体评估（优势类别、拥挤程度、开发建议）
```

4.3 深度分析提示词（SOUL）

```
你是 BRAIN Alpha 研究的"灵魂"（SOUL），具备以下能力：

**核心职责**：
从一个字段的原始元数据中，挖掘出其"隐藏价值"和"Alpha 潜力"。

**分析框架**：
1. **技术深度扫描**
   - 用6步法表达式推断数据分布、缺失模式、更新频率
   - 预测可能的数据质量陷阱（look-ahead bias、系统性缺失）

2. **金融逻辑推演**
   - 从字段定义反推背后的经济机制
   - 生成3种可能的经济假说（看涨/看跌/中性）
   - 评估假说的可检验性和稳健性

3. **竞争格局分析**
   - 根据 userCount/alphaCount 推断竞争强度
   - 识别"拥挤陷阱"：看似冷门实际已被深度开发
   - 发现"蓝海机会"：被忽视但潜力大的字段

4. **Alpha 构建路径**
   - 设计"三阶段Alpha开发路线"（朴素→变换→融合）
   - 预测可能的 Sharpe 范围和风险点
   - 给出避坑建议和优化方向

**输入**：
- 字段完整元数据（含 description、coverage、userCount 等）
- 该字段所属数据集的全局信息
- 目标金字塔坐标（Region-Delay-Universe-Category）

**输出**（深度报告）：
1. **字段画像**：一句话总结 + 五维分类
2. **Alpha 潜力评分**（0-10分）+ 详细理由
3. **开发路线图**：朴素表达式 → 初级变换 → 高级融合
4. **风险清单**：数据风险、拥挤风险、金融逻辑风险
5. **预期收益**：预测 IS Sharpe 范围、提交成功概率

**示例输入**：
{
  "dataset": "fundamental6",
  "field": "cash_flow_from_operations",
  "description": "Cash flow from operating activities",
  "coverage": 0.52,
  "userCount": 3214,
  "alphaCount": 12456,
  "pyramidMultiplier": 1.3
}

**示例输出**：
 字段深度分析报告：fundamental6.cash_flow_from_operations

一、字段画像
**核心定义**：经营活动现金流，反映公司主营业务的"真实造血能力"  
**五维分类**：技术B级 | 盈利质量类 | 中等拥挤 | 金字塔优先级中 | 预期表现A级

二、Alpha 潜力评分：7.5/10
**理由**：
- ✅ 金融逻辑强：现金流 > 利润，预测未来盈利质量
- ✅ 覆盖率适中：0.52 对 Fundamental 类合理
- ⚠️ 拥挤度中等：需要变换避开主流表达式
- ✅ 金字塔加成：1.3x 乘数提升战略价值

三、开发路线图

阶段1：朴素基线（预期 Sharpe 1.2~1.5）
```python
 现金流质量信号
rank(cash_flow_from_operations / total_assets)

现金流改善信号
ts_delta(cash_flow_from_operations, 66)   季度环比
```

阶段2：降拥挤变换（预期 Sharpe 1.5~1.8）
```python
 行业相对现金流
group_neutralize(
    rank(cash_flow_from_operations), 
    subindustry
)

现金流惊喜（vs 分析师预期）
cash_flow_from_operations - analyst_forecast_cfo
```

阶段3：融合增强（预期 Sharpe 1.8~2.2）
```python
 现金流 × 估值错配
rank(cash_flow_from_operations) * (-rank(pe_ratio))

现金流改善 × 低拥挤字段
ts_delta(cfo, 66) * sigmoid(group_zscore(scarce_field, industry))
```

四、风险清单
1. **数据风险**：金融行业 CFO 定义不同，需剔除或单独处理
2. **拥挤风险**：直接 rank(cfo) 自相关可能超 0.5，必须变换
3. **逻辑风险**：仅 CFO 高不够，需结合负债率/资本支出综合判断

五、预期收益
- **IS Sharpe 范围**：1.5 ~ 2.0（中性化后）
- **提交成功概率**：70%（需通过 self-corr 和 sub-universe 检查）
- **开发成本**：10~15 次仿真（朴素5次 + 变换10次）

六、行动建议
1. **立即行动**：跑阶段1的2个朴素表达式冒烟
2. **谨慎行动**：若朴素 Sharpe < 1.0，放弃该字段
3. **深度开发**：若朴素 Sharpe ≥ 1.3，进入阶段2变换
4. **横向拓展**：同时测试 free_cash_flow、capex 等相关字段
```

---

七、总结与展望

核心价值

本方法论通过**五维分类体系**（技术/语义/拥挤/金字塔/表现），将 BRAIN 平台上的海量字段从"黑盒"变为"透明"，实现：

✅ **效率提升**：字段筛选时间从数小时降至数分钟  
✅ **成功率提高**：避开80%的无效尝试和拥挤陷阱  
✅ **知识沉淀**：建立可复用、可迭代的字段分类库  
✅ **战略清晰**：系统化点亮金字塔，而非盲目试错

### 评论 #26 (作者: JC25837, 时间: 3个月前)

Gem Workflow Enhanced 完整工作流文件，这个是在skills：Gem Workflow基础上融合 [JR57542](/hc/zh-cn/profiles/34782588698007-JR57542) 帖子“【经验分享】用AI 跑了几百次回测还找不到优质信号，到底是池塘没鱼还是鱼饵不对？燃烧token！给 AI 装上导航！”进行改进的skills文件执行出来的工作流文件

## 基础量化 Alpha 挖掘流程（基于GLB/MINVOL1M/fundamental72 实战提炼）

> 本文件从一次完整挖掘（13批104表达式，Sharpe 0.63→1.49，+137%）中提炼可复用的流程、经验与优化点。所有结论均有回测数据支撑，可直接引用到后续 fundamental 类数据集的 Alpha 挖掘。

---

## 第一部分：完整工作流阶段

### Phase 0 — 认证与平台设置确认
**动作**:
1. `authenticate` 认证
2. `get_platform_setting_options` 确认 region/universe/delay/neutralization 有效组合
3. `get_datasets` + `get_datafields`（按 search 关键词分主题拉取）拉取字段
4. 创建输出目录 `./gem_enhanced_output/{region}_{delay}_{dataset_id}/`

**经验 #1 — 字段命名规律识别**:
fundamental72 字段命名遵循 `fnd72_{s?}_pit_or_{bs|is|cf}_{a|q}_*` 规律：
- `_s_` 前缀 = MATRIX 标准化每日填充值（**优先用，适合 ts_ 运算**）
- 无 `_s_` = VECTOR 原始报告日触发值
- `bs`=资产负债表 / `is`=利润表 / `cf`=现金流
- `_a_`=年度 / `_q_`=季度

**经验 #2 — 蓝海字段识别**:
优先选 `alphaCount=0` 且 `coverage>0.7` 的字段。本数据集 `fnd72_s_pit_or_*_a_*` 系列是 2026-03 新建字段，绝大多数未开发，命中 `GLB/D1 Dataset Power Pool June'26` 主题池（乘数 1.5）。**蓝海字段 = 低相关性潜力**。

**经验 #3 — 平台设置确认要点**:
GLB/Delay=1 的可用 Universe 是 `["TOP3000","MINVOL1M","MINVOL10M","TOPDIV3000"]`，且**特有 `COUNTRY` 中性化**（这是 GLB 区域的关键杠杆，见后）。

---

### Phase 0.5 — 论文预搜索
**动作**: 用 web_search 搜索 3-4 个核心因子主题（盈利能力/应计/资产增长/杠杆），优先近5年顶刊。

**经验 #4 — 论文驱动避免幻想**:
搜索后建立"理论→字段聚类→Alpha构造逻辑"映射表。本次确认：
- Novy-Marx(2013) 盈利能力溢价 → EBITDA/Assets 或 EBITDA/Revenue
- Sloan(1996) 应计异常（20国验证）→ (NI-CFO)/Assets
- Cooper-Gulen-Schill(2008) 资产增长 → ts_delta(Assets)/Assets
- NBER w22116 杠杆异常 → Debt/Assets

**可引用**: 论文国际证据（如 Pincus 20国应计验证）是判断因子在 GLB（全球）区域适用性的依据。

---

### Phase 1 — 字段语义聚类
**动作**: 按会计科目将字段聚类（盈利/估值规模/杠杆/现金流质量/投资融资），每类标注覆盖率、alphaCount、蓝海标记。

**经验 #5 — 聚类维度设计**:
fundamental 数据集聚类为 5 类最实用：C1盈利能力 / C2估值规模 / C3杠杆 / C4现金流质量 / C5投资融资。每类选 3-7 个高覆盖字段。

---

### Phase 2 — Idea 生成
**动作**: 每个聚类生成 1-2 个有论文依据的 Idea，包含经济学假说和表达式骨架。

**经验 #6 — Idea 必须含三要素**:
1. 论文引用（经济学基础）
2. 方向假设（正向/反向）
3. 表达式骨架（≤8 算子）

---

### Phase 3 — 螺旋迭代回测（核心）
**动作**: 每批 8 条 `create_multi_simulation`，分析结果 → 信号灯 → 修正 idea → 下一批。

#### 第一轮（探索）
**经验 #7 — 第一批要覆盖广**:
第一批 8 条覆盖全部 5 聚类 + 4 算子族（cross_section/group/arithmetic/time_series），快速定位有鱼的方向。**不要在第一批就深挖单点**。

**经验 #8 — 信号灯判断**:
- 🟢 GREEN: 显著信号，加码深挖
- 🟡 YELLOW: 有潜力但证据不足，换 1-2 算子族继续
- 🔴 RED: 信号弱，结构性改动
- ⚫ DEAD: 高置信死路（但尝试<4族不判 DEAD）

**经验 #9 — 算子多样性区分"池塘没鱼"vs"鱼饵不对"**:
用了 4+ 算子族仍无信号 → 数据质量问题（换数据集）；只用 1-2 族不行 → 探索不充分（换算子族）。本次用 5 族有信号 → 非 DEAD，继续挖。

#### 第二轮（收敛 + Fine-Tune）
**经验 #10 — Fine-Tune 闸门**:
仅当 Sharpe>1.40 且 Fitness>0.90 才允许纯参数微调，否则必须走结构/字段驱动。本次第 11 才突破闸门（Sharpe 1.44, Fitness 1.23）。

---

### Phase 4 — 精细调优与提交检查
**动作**: Fine-Tune 闸门后微调 std/decay/窗口 → `check_correlation`(PROD<0.7) → `get_submission_check`。

**经验 #11 — 提交检查顺序**:
先 `check_correlation`(PROD) 确认 <0.7，再 `get_submission_check`。`get_submission_check` 偶尔报相关性格式错误，用 `check_correlation` 兜底。

---

## 第二部分：可引用的优化杠杆（按贡献排序）

### 杠杆 1 — country 中性化（GLB 核心杠杆，+63%）
**发现**: `group_zscore(x, country)` 在 GLB 区域是质变杠杆。EBITDA/Assets 从 Sharpe 0.63（rank）→ 1.03（country 中性）。

**根因**: GLB 是跨国数据，不同国家宏观环境（通胀、利率、会计准则）差异大。country 中性化消除国家层面公共因子，让信号回归"同国家内相对强弱"。

**可引用规则**:
> GLB/跨国区域优先用 `group_zscore(信号, country)` 或平台 `neutralization=COUNTRY`。subindustry/industry 中性在本案例中不如 country（subindustry 0.70 vs country 1.49）。

### 杠杆 2 — 分母选择（Revenue > Assets，跨国可比性）
**发现**: EBITDA/Revenue（利润率）Sharpe 1.07 > EBITDA/Assets（资产回报）1.03。且 APAC 子区从 0.57→0.93（大幅提升）。

**根因**: 利润率是比率，跨国可比性强；资产账面价值受会计准则（US GAAP vs IFRS）和重资产/轻资产行业差异影响大。

**可引用规则**:
> 跨国基本面因子优先用"利润流/收入"（margin）而非"利润流/资产"。OperInc/Revenue、NI/Revenue、EBITDA/Revenue 都有效，EBITDA/Revenue 最强。

### 杠杆 3 — winsorize 去极值（std 降维单调提升，+28%）
**发现**: `winsorize(x, std=N)` 的 N 越小（去极值越激进），Fitness 越高，单调直到拐点：

| std | Sharpe | Fitness |
|-----|--------|---------|
| 4 | 1.21 | 0.95 |
| 3 | 1.22 | 0.96 |
| 2 | 1.27 | 1.02 ✓ |
| 1.5 | 1.30 | 1.05 |
| 1 | 1.37 | 1.14 |
| 0.8 | 1.39 | 1.17 |
| **0.5** | **1.44** | **1.23** |
| **0.3** | **1.48** | **1.28** |
| 0.25 | 1.48 | 1.28 |
| 0.2 | 1.48 | 1.28 |
| 0.1 | 1.44 | 1.24（反转，过度 winsorize 损失信息）|

**可引用规则**:
> winsorize std 存在拐点（本次 std=0.3）。从 std=4 起递减测试，找到 Sharpe 停止增长的拐点。**拐点之前是免费提升，必做**。过度 winsorize（std 过小）会损失信号信息导致反转。

### 杠杆 4 — ts_decay 平滑（提升 Fitness/2Y-Sharpe）
**发现**: `ts_decay_linear(信号, D)` 大幅提升 Fitness 和 2Y-Sharpe：
- 无 decay: Fitness 0.92, 2Y 1.81
- decay=10: Fitness 0.94, 2Y 1.79
- decay=40: Fitness 0.95, 2Y 1.75
- **decay=50: Fitness 1.29, 2Y 2.01**（本次最优）

**可引用规则**:
> 基本面年度因子天然低换手，加 `ts_decay_linear(信号, 40-50)` 平滑可显著提升 Fitness 和 2Y-Sharpe 稳定性。窗口 40-50 最优（30 偏短，60 偏长略降）。

### 杠杆 5 — 单因子优于多因子组合（反直觉）
**发现**: 单因子 winsorize margin（1.49）> 任何两因子/三因子组合（1.18-1.47）。

**可引用规则**:
> 当单因子已很强（Sharpe>1.3）时，加第二因子往往稀释信号而非增强。多因子组合仅在单因子 <1.2 时有效。**不要盲目堆因子**。

### 杠杆 6 — trade_when 条件过滤（特定场景）
**发现**: `trade_when(greater(NI,0), CFO/NI, 0)`（只对盈利公司）将 2Y-Sharpe 从 1.03→1.71。但 `trade_when(EBITDA>0)` 失败（过滤无效）。

**可引用规则**:
> trade_when 适用于"分母可能为负导致比率失真"的场景（如 CFO/NI）。条件要选能真正过滤噪声的（NI>0 有效，EBITDA>0 无效因为几乎所有公司 EBITDA>0）。第三参数（exit）用 0 表示不强制退出。

---

## 第三部分：反模式库（anti_pattern，避免重蹈覆辙）

| # | 模式 | 结果 | 原因 | 教训 |
|---|------|------|------|------|
| 1 | `trade_when(EBITDA>0)` | -0.17 | 几乎所有公司 EBITDA>0，过滤无效引入偏差 | 条件须能真正筛选 |
| 2 | `signed_power(x, 1.5)` 放大尾部 | EMEA 崩 -0.08 | 放大极端值破坏稳健性 | 避免非线性放大 |
| 3 | 三因子组合（margin+assets+CFO） | 1.11 < 两因子 1.19 | 信号稀释 | 单因子强时勿堆因子 |
| 4 | `ts_delta(EBITDA,250)` 盈利变化 | 0.00 | 年度变化率噪声大 | 用水平值不用变化率 |
| 5 | EBITDA/Assets + winsorize（Assets分母） | 0.79 | Assets 分母跨国不可比 | 跨国用 Revenue 分母 |
| 6 | `group_zscore(x, subindustry)` | 0.70 | GLB 用 country 最优 | GLB 优先 country 中性 |
| 7 | `hump(x, 0.01)` 降换手 | 换手 0.0062<1% FAIL | 基本面因子已低换手 | 勿再加 hump |
| 8 | std=0.1 过度 winsorize | 1.44 < 1.48 | 损失信号信息 | 找拐点勿过头 |
| 9 | `rank(ts_rank(x,250))` 双重排名 | 0.02 | 时序排名对年度基本面无效 | 基本面用截面/组内排名 |
| 10 | `group_rank` 替代 `group_zscore` | 0.40 < 1.07 | rank 分布不如 zscore 连续 | 优先 group_zscore |
| 11 | 平台 neutralization 换 SUBINDUSTRY/SLOW_AND_FAST | 未提升 | INDUSTRY 已最优 | 表达式内 country 中性 + 平台 INDUSTRY 是黄金组合 |
| 12 | 平台 decay=0 或 decay=10 | 略降 | decay=4 最优 | 平台 decay 4 + 表达式 ts_decay(50) 分工 |

---

## 第四部分：关键决策规则

### 规则 1 — DoD 完成判定
```
Sharpe >= 1.58; Fitness >= 1.0; Turnover 1%-30%; Margin >= 0.0010; 单票权重 <10%
Robust Universe Sharpe >= 1.0; 2Y Sharpe >= 1.58; PROD correlation < 0.7
零 FAIL 后才能 get_submission_check
```

### 规则 2 — Fine-Tune 闸门
```
IF Sharpe > 1.40 AND Fitness > 0.90:
    允许纯参数微调（std/decay/窗口）
ELSE:
    必须走结构/字段驱动（换分母/换中性/换字段/组合）
```

### 规则 3 — 反向指标识别
```
IF Sharpe <= -1.20 AND Fitness <= -0.50:
    标记 CAND_NEG / CAND_SHORTFLIP
    下一批至少生成 2 个反向版本：-(expr) 或 multiply(-1, expr)
```
**注**: 本次杠杆反向 Sharpe -0.56（未达 -1.20 但方向明确），验证正向杠杆在 AMER 有效（0.87）——**反向线索即使未达强阈值也值得验证**。

### 规则 4 — 每批严格 8 条
multi_simulation 2-8 条，**严格 8 条**。失败/无效候选必须在回测前重写补齐。关键表达式用 `create_simulation` 单独跑（但注意 hook 可能拦截，需用户确认）。

### 规则 5 — 核心字段锁定
以基准/首个有效方向的核心字段为准，**禁止混数据集**。本次锁定 fundamental72 的 EBITDA + Revenue + Assets + CFO + NI + Borrow 字段族，只在它们上做变换。

### 规则 6 — 算子约束
- operatorCount ≤ 8（平台最终裁决）
- 具名参数必须 `name=value`（如 `winsorize(x, std=4)`，`ts_returns(x, d, mode=1)`）
- 不同函数名各计 1；`+ - * /` 各计 1（同符号多次仍 1）；字段名/常数/括号不计
- `add/subtract` 形式只计函数不再计 `+/-`

---

## 第五部分：算子族分类与多样性评分

```python
OPERATOR_FAMILIES = {
    'cross_section': ['rank','zscore','winsorize','normalize','quantile','scale','vector_neut'],
    'group': ['group_zscore','group_rank','group_neutralize','group_mean','group_backfill'],
    'arithmetic': ['divide','subtract','add','multiply','reverse','signed_power','log','abs'],
    'time_series': ['ts_decay_linear','ts_zscore','ts_rank','ts_delta','ts_mean','ts_std_dev','ts_corr'],
    'conditional': ['if_else','trade_when','greater','less','and','or','is_nan']
}
```

**评分规则**: 一轮回测覆盖族数 <4 且无信号 → 判 DEAD 前 Must 再试 2 族。覆盖 ≥4 族仍弱 → 数据问题，换数据集。

---

## 第六部分：本次回测的完整表达式谱（可复用模板）

### 最优模板（直接复用）
```
ts_decay_linear(group_zscore(winsorize(divide({利润流}, {收入}), std=0.3), country), 50)
```
- 利润流: EBITDA > OperInc > NI
- 区域: GLB（country 中性）；若其他区域，country 换 sector/industry
- std: 0.3 起测，找拐点
- decay: 50

### 备选模板
1. **现金盈利质量**: `trade_when(greater(NI,0), rank(divide(CFO,NI)), 0)` — 2Y-Sharpe 强（1.71）
2. **多因子组合（单因子<1.2时）**: `add(group_zscore(divide(EBITDA,Assets),country), group_zscore(divide(CFO,Assets),country))`
3. **杠杆正向（AMER）**: `rank(divide(add(LTBorrow,STBorrow),Assets))` — AMER 0.87

---

## 第七部分：性能基准与期望管理

| 指标 | 本次实测 | 参考 |
|------|---------|------|
| 起步 Sharpe | 0.63 | 第一批探索 |
| country 中性后 | 1.03 | GLB 杠杆 |
| winsorize 后 | 1.27-1.49 | 去极值 |
| 最终 Sharpe | 1.49 | 单因子天花板 |
| Fitness | 1.29 | PASS |
| 2Y-Sharpe | 2.01 | PASS |
| PROD corr | <0.7 | PASS |
| 总表达式数 | 104 | 13批×8 |
| 突破 Fine-Tune 闸门批次 | 第 11 批 | ~85% 进度 |
| 成功率 | 1 个高质量候选 / 104 回测 | 接近 1/500 RA 参考值（本次效率更高）|

**期望管理**: fundamental 单因子单数据集在 GLB/MINVOL1M 的 Sharpe 天花板约 1.49-1.58。突破 1.58 需：① 换更优数据集 ② Super Alpha 组合 ③ 区域聚焦（如纯 EMEA 用 industry 中性）。

---

## 第八部分：可引用经验速查（Top 10）

1. **GLB 跨国必用 country 中性**（`group_zscore(x, country)`），+63% Sharpe
2. **跨国用 Revenue 分母**（margin）不用 Assets，跨国可比性强
3. **winsorize std 降维找拐点**（本次 0.3），免费 +28% Sharpe
4. **ts_decay(40-50) 平滑**提升 Fitness/2Y，基本面因子标配
5. **单因子强时（>1.3）勿堆因子**，组合会稀释
6. **trade_when 修比率失真**（CFO/NI 加 NI>0 条件），2Y 大幅提升
7. **蓝海字段（alphaCount=0, cov>0.7）优先**，低相关+主题池加成
8. **Fine-Tune 闸门（1.40/0.90）前只走结构**，不纯调参
9. **反向线索即使未达 -1.20 也验证**（杠杆反向→正向 AMER 0.87）
10. **算子族覆盖 ≥4 才能判 DEAD**，防过早放弃

---

### 评论 #27 (作者: ZH87224, 时间: 2个月前)

一个skill生成因子分析报告并更新到平台

---

name: newt-alpha-report

description: Write a comprehensive Chinese-and-English BRAIN alpha analysis report from an alpha ID, save to squadb/alphas/{region}/{alpha_id}.md, and update the alpha description on the BRAIN platform via NewtMCP. Use when the user wants a report for a specific alpha, asks to analyze or document a BRAIN alpha, or needs submission-ready English description text for an alpha.

# Alpha Report Writer

Generates a structured, bilingual alpha analysis report for any WorldQuant BRAIN alpha. For the detailed report format specification, see [reference.md](reference.md).

## Workflow

### Step 1: Gather Alpha Information

Use NewtMCP tools to collect everything needed for the report:

- `get_alpha(alpha_id)`: Retrieve the alpha's expression, region, universe, delay, neutralization, and basic metrics (Sharpe, Fitness, Turnover, Margin, Returns, Drawdown). Note the `settings` dict for neutralization and other configuration.

- Search field definitions: For each datafield in the expression, use `search_fields` on the NewtMCP server to get field descriptions. Cross-reference with `squadb/datasets/` if a local dataset directory exists.

- Identify datasets: From each field's dataset membership, look up the dataset ID and name. Check `squadb/datasets/<dataset_id>/dataset_info.json` or `squadb/datasets/<dataset_id>/compressed_*.json` when available locally.

- Verify operators: Look up every operator used in the expression against `squadb/knowledge/BRAIN Platform Operators ALL.md` for accurate semantics.

- Get yearly stats: Call `get_yearly_stats(alpha_id)` for long-horizon performance context.

- Get self-correlation: Call `calc_self_corr(alpha_id)` for stability assessment.

### Step 2: Generate the Report

Follow the report template in [reference.md](reference.md) exactly. Key requirements:

- **Metadata block**: YAML frontmatter with alpha_id, region, universe, delay, neutralization, fields (names only), datasets (ID + name), operators (names only).

- **Title**: `# {alpha_id} 因子分析报告`

- **Chinese section**: Five subsections (0-4) covering factor description, strategy intuition, data rationale, operator rationale, and a brief summary.

- **English section**: Exactly three lines with `Idea:`, `Rationale for data used:`, and `Rationale for operators used:` as headers, each followed by a single continuous paragraph with no internal line breaks.

See [reference.md](reference.md) for the full format specification, writing guidelines, and self-check checklist.

### Step 3: Save the Report

Write the report to `squadb/alphas/{region}/{alpha_id}.md`, where `{region}` is the uppercase region code returned by `get_alpha` (e.g., `USA`, `GLB`, `EUR`).

### Step 4: Classify and Update BRAIN Platform

First, determine the category that best fits the alpha's core economic story and data sources:

- PRICE_REVERSION — mean-reversion, overbought/oversold, contrarian price patterns

- PRICE_MOMENTUM — trend-following, continuation, breakout, price persistence

- VOLUME — trading-volume-based, liquidity-driven, turnover signals

- FUNDAMENTAL — financial-statement-derived, valuation, cash-flow, balance-sheet

- ANALYST — analyst estimates, revisions, earnings forecasts, recommendations

- PRICE_VOLUME — price × volume interaction (VWAP-style, volume-weighted)

- RELATION — inter-stock relationships, pairs, relative value, cross-sectional

- SENTIMENT — news, social media, alternative data sentiment

If no single category clearly fits, leave it as None.

Then call `update_alpha` on the NewtMCP server:

- `alpha_id`: The alpha being reported on

- `name`: Set to `None` (do not change)

- `description`: Full three-line English submission-ready text

- `category`: The best-fit category from the analysis, or `None` if ambiguous

- All other parameters (`settings`, `tags`, etc.): Set to `None` (keep existing values)

## Notes

- Do not guess field semantics or dataset names. Use `search_fields` and local dataset docs.

- If a field definition is unavailable from MCP or local docs, note the uncertainty explicitly in the Chinese report; for the English text, write the most defensible concise description based on the field name and expression context.

- Verify the report passes the self-check checklist in [reference.md](reference.md) before finalizing.

## Reference

## Report Format Specification

### Metadata Frontmatter

```yaml

---

alpha_id: {alpha_id}

region: {region_code}

universe: {universe}

delay: {delay}

neutralization: {neutralization}

fields:

- {field_name}

datasets:

- {dataset_id}: {dataset_name}

operators:

- {operator_name}

---

```

- `fields`: List only field names (no descriptions).

- `datasets`: List as `dataset_id: dataset_name` pairs.

- `operators`: List only operator names (no usage syntax).

### Title

```markdown

# {alpha_id} 因子分析报告

```

### Part 1: Chinese Analysis Report (简体中文)

Five subsections. Write in continuous narrative, not bullet lists. Integrate field descriptions and operator semantics rather than copy-pasting. Use LaTeX for formulas with `...` blocks.

#### 0. 因子描述

Basic information: region, universe, delay, dataset types, key performance metrics (Sharpe, Fitness, Turnover), and factor type classification (fundamental / technical / sentiment / mixed).

#### 1. 策略直觉（Idea）

What market phenomenon or hypothesis the alpha captures. Explain the economic intuition behind the signal. If the exact long/short direction cannot be determined from the expression alone, note that explicitly.

#### 2. 数据 rationale

For each field: its role in the expression, economic/statistical meaning, how fields interact. If field descriptions are unavailable, make reasonable inferences based on field names and expression context, and clearly label those as inferences.

Include subsections:

- Individual field analysis

- How fields work together

- Any data-layer implications (frequency, coverage, sign conventions — flag unknowns)

#### 3. 算子 rationale

Walk through the expression from inner to outer, explaining each operator's transformation and what it adds to the signal. Cover:

- Vector vs. scalar handling

- Cross-sectional vs. time-series operations

- Grouping operations

- Final scaling/smoothing

#### 4. 小结

One or two sentences summarizing the risk profile and applicable context. What conditions does this factor depend on? Where might it break?

### Part 2: English Submission-Ready Text

Exactly three lines, each starting with the specified header followed by a space and a single continuous paragraph (no line breaks within the paragraph):

```

Idea: {single continuous paragraph, no internal newlines}

Rationale for data used: {single continuous paragraph, no internal newlines}

Rationale for operators used: {single continuous paragraph, no internal newlines}

```

**Rules:**

- Three headers and only three: `Idea:`, `Rationale for data used:`, `Rationale for operators used:`

- Each header followed by exactly one space, then the paragraph text

- No internal line breaks within any paragraph

- Concise and submission-ready: core ideas only, no boilerplate

- No additional section headers

- Do not fabricate facts not derivable from the expression, field names, or provided metadata

### Part 3: Category Classification

Based on the alpha's core economic story and data sources, classify it into one of these categories:

- PRICE_REVERSION — mean-reversion, overbought/oversold, contrarian price patterns

- PRICE_MOMENTUM — trend-following, continuation, breakout, price persistence

- VOLUME — trading-volume-based, liquidity-driven, turnover signals

- FUNDAMENTAL — financial-statement-derived, valuation, cash-flow, balance-sheet

- ANALYST — analyst estimates, revisions, earnings forecasts, recommendations

- PRICE_VOLUME — price × volume interaction (VWAP-style, volume-weighted)

- RELATION — inter-stock relationships, pairs, relative value, cross-sectional

- SENTIMENT — news, social media, alternative data sentiment

If no single category clearly fits the alpha's core logic, leave it unset (None).

### Self-Check Checklist

Before finalizing the report, verify:

- [ ] Metadata frontmatter uses valid YAML and includes all required fields

- [ ] Chinese report covers all five subsections (0-4)

- [ ] English section has exactly three headers with correct spelling

- [ ] Each English paragraph is a single continuous line (no internal newlines)

- [ ] LaTeX formulas are wrapped in `...` blocks

- [ ] File saved to correct path: `squadb/alphas/{region}/{alpha_id}.md`

- [ ] Field and operator descriptions are integrated, not copy-pasted

- [ ] Uncertainties are explicitly flagged in the Chinese report

- [ ] Category is correctly classified and passed to update_alpha

### Example

See `squadb/alphas/USA/1Y7M1G0K.md` for a complete example of the expected output format and writing style.

# YH: Single-Alpha Optimizer

Optimize one Alpha through evidence-backed controlled batches. Never submit automatically.

## Hard Rules

- Require an Alpha ID. Fetch the incumbent expression, description, settings, aggregate/latest-three-complete-year metrics, checks, PC/SC, and metadata for signal fields once.
- Build the mechanism card in this file before mutation. Do not use simulations to discover what the signal means.
- Keep signal fields in one dataset. A second same-dataset field needs a causal scale, condition, residual, denominator, or confirmation role. PV fallback requires explicit authorization and prior same-dataset attempts.
- Keep platform operator count `<=7`; hold settings fixed except the declared experiment; change one mechanism per candidate.
- Missing/zero PC or SC is `PENDING`, never pass. One failed candidate, high PC, 429, timeout, or batch boundary never ends optimization.
- Never call submit or property-setting paths.

## Context Budget

- Save raw platform responses to disk. Do not print or read a raw file larger than 32 KB into model context; extract named fields or a targeted fragment.
- Cache immutable incumbent facts and the mechanism card. Later rounds read only changed candidate evidence.
- After each batch read `workflow_status.json` and `decision_summary.json` first. Read `policy_results.json` only when a listed failed gate needs detail; read checkpoint/raw files only by pointer.
- Do not repeatedly poll in chat. Run background workers, then request one compact status per meaningful state change.
- Run the unchanged control only in the first reproducibility batch or after settings change. Otherwise reference its cached Alpha ID and metrics.
- Keep narrative logs short: update pivot, tried interventions, untried families, and next action; do not restate the full history.

## Reference Routing

This file is self-contained. Use only the section needed for the current action:

- Candidate generation or a new causal family: `Candidate Design`.
- Gate, units, recent-trend, Investability, or exhaustion interpretation: `Optimization Policy`.
- Simulation, retry, resume, or background-process issue: `Runtime and Recovery`.
- Job, mechanism-card, evaluation, or stop-evidence shape: `Schemas`.

## Single-File Bootstrap

Treat this `SKILL.md` as the only required distributed artifact.

On first use:

1. Inspect the skill directory for `scripts/alpha_policy.py`, `scripts/ace_batch_runner.py`, `scripts/background_control.py`, and `scripts/pc_query_runner.py`.
2. If any file is missing, create the missing files from the exact embedded source blocks in `Embedded Runtime Files`.
3. Preserve filenames exactly. Create `scripts/` if needed.
4. Do not overwrite an existing local script unless the user explicitly asks or the file is clearly the stale placeholder you created from this document.
5. After materializing missing files, run representative self-tests:

```powershell
python scripts/alpha_policy.py self-test
python scripts/ace_batch_runner.py --self-test
python scripts/pc_query_runner.py --self-test
```

6. If the skill directory is not writable, materialize the same files into a writable sibling runtime directory such as `.yh_runtime/scripts/`, then invoke commands from that location instead.

## Main Workflow

1. Create an external run root. Persist `current_pivot_id`, `tried_interventions`, `untried_repair_families`, and `next_required_action`.
2. Freeze and diagnose the incumbent. Explain information source, sign, update/holding horizon, cross-section, exposure, trading mechanism, failure regime, and falsification condition.
3. Select one diagnosed family. Use 4-6 candidates only when each has a named hypothesis; otherwise use fewer. Prefer representation, same-dataset relation, horizon, cross-section, tail/concentration, exposure, then cadence.
4. Preflight syntax, dataset identity, signal role, duplicates, operator count, mechanism card, and settings.
5. Simulate with one outer slot. A transient batch failure is retried/bisected; permanent expression failure rejects only that candidate.
6. Query PC and SC with `scripts/pc_query_runner.py`. It normalizes Margin to bps, latest three yearly rows, and Investability evidence.
7. Follow `workflow_status.next_required_action`; do not invent an aggregate score.

Example commands:

```powershell
python scripts/alpha_policy.py count --expression "<expr>"
python scripts/ace_batch_runner.py --job job.json --run-dir batch --dry-run
python scripts/background_control.py start --job job.json --run-dir batch
python scripts/background_control.py status --run-dir batch
python scripts/background_control.py start --mode pc --job job.json --run-dir batch
python scripts/background_control.py status --mode pc --run-dir batch
```

## Routing

- `PC_REPAIR_CONTINUE` / `SC_REPAIR_CONTINUE`: open the next distinct structural family.
- `QUALITY_REPAIR_FROM_LOW_PC_PIVOT`: freeze the PC `<0.65` pivot and repair one failed quality/cost/recent-year gate, then recheck PC/SC.
- `INVESTABILITY_REPAIR_FROM_LOW_PC_PIVOT`: diagnose constrained ratios, concentration, liquidity, counts, and curve before compression.
- `CORRELATION_PENDING`: continue non-correlation work and retry later.
- `CONTINUE_REQUIRED`: pending or incomplete exhaustion evidence forbids stopping.
- `REPLAN_REQUIRED`: re-read the mechanism and design a new causal family; research exhaustion is not completion.
- `FINALIST_REVALIDATE`: fetch fresh checks and raw curves. Only verified `final_revalidation.status=PASS` with evidence can set `stop_allowed=true`.

Final research targets: PC `<0.65` (prefer `<0.60`), SC `<0.60`, Sharpe/Fitness `>2`, Turnover `[0.05,0.20)`, Margin `[10,40]` bps, healthy Investability and latest-three-year behavior, one dataset, operator count `<=7`, and no required final `FAIL/ERROR/PENDING`.

# Candidate Design

Do not use simulations to discover what the strategy means. Complete the evidence-backed mechanism card in `Schemas` first; each intervention must follow from one diagnosed failure and include a falsification condition.

## Decompose the Incumbent

Describe the Alpha in six layers:

| Layer | Questions |
|---|---|
| Field | Which field/dataset drives the ordering? Is it crowded or sparse? |
| Meaning | Level, change, surprise, residual, persistence, reversal, risk, or event? |
| Time | Which window/update frequency determines the horizon? |
| Cross-section | Global rank/z-score, group-relative, residual, or raw magnitude? |
| Exposure/tail | Industry, size, liquidity, volatility, extreme weights? |
| Trading | Decay, hump, trigger, turnover, and holding cadence? |

Choose one diagnosed PC source before generating expressions.

## Single-Dataset Boundary

Allowed by default:

- one field transformed through level/change/revision/acceleration;
- two fields from the same dataset forming a causal ratio, spread, residual, scale, quality check, or update condition;
- platform group metadata such as industry/subindustry when permitted;
- multiple transformations of the same economic quantity.

Rejected by default:

- adding an analyst Alpha leg to a model Alpha;
- adding sentiment/news/option/PV return legs only to reduce correlation;
- multiplying unrelated same-dataset fields without one economic mechanism;
- noise fields, IDs, dates, or classification codes used as signals.

PV fallback requires all of:

1. `allow_pv_fallback=true`;
2. at least two same-dataset structural families tried and recorded;
3. a named liquidity, volatility, execution, or market-state role;
4. no independent PV prediction leg;
5. explicit reporting that the result is no longer strict Single Dataset if the platform classifies it that way.

## PC Repair Families

Use this order unless diagnosis supports another:

1. **Information representation:** level -> revision/surprise/relative change/acceleration.
2. **Same-dataset relation:** meaningful spread/ratio/residual between related fields.
3. **Horizon:** short-long contrast matched to update frequency.
4. **Cross-section:** global -> group-relative or residualized.
5. **Tail/concentration:** winsorize/rank/z-score/truncation; fractional `signed_power` only for diagnosed tail compression, exponent >1 only for justified tail amplification.
6. **Exposure:** one neutralization A/B.
7. **Cadence:** one decay or event-trigger A/B.

Parameter tuning is last and limited. Do not assume correlations multiply under fusion.

## Quality Repair Families

Start from a saved PC `<0.65` pivot:

- Recent decay: remove stale levels, align window with field updates, or use revision/residual form.
- Low Fitness: improve signal consistency first; do not force Turnover below the 5% research floor.
- Margin outside 10-40 bps: adjust one trading cadence parameter, then recheck PC.
- Weak Investability: diagnose liquidity/size/weight concentration before using distribution compression.
- Bad middle year: test a stable economic regime representation, not a condition fitted to that year.

## Candidate Batch Contract

Each candidate must record:

```yaml
candidate_id: R1-C1
parent_id: incumbent-or-pivot-id
mechanism_card_id: YH-base-20260719
dataset_ids: [model123]
field_ids: [model123_score]
changed_mechanism: level_to_revision
hypothesis: "A revision signal changes crowded level exposure."
falsified_if: "PC does not improve by tolerance or quality breaches the loss envelope."
signal_role: same_mechanism_transformation
independent_signal_blend: false
expression: "..."
settings_delta: {}
operator_count_local: 0
```

Include one unchanged control in each batch. A batch is an experiment, not a population search.

## Forbidden Patterns

- random field/window/neutralization mutation;
- more candidates merely to fill 10 batch positions;
- unrelated signal addition or multiplication;
- `reverse` solely to pass correlation;
- simultaneous dataset, window, Universe, neutralization, and decay changes;
- stopping the run because PC remains high after one family;
- promoting a candidate with missing PC/SC;
- automatic submission.

# Optimization Policy

## Target Layers

Keep platform limits and research targets separate.

| Metric | User research target | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| PC | `<0.65`, prefer `<0.60` | Candidate research gate |
| SC | `<0.60` | Candidate research gate |
| Sharpe | `>2.0` | Preferred final quality |
| Fitness | `>2.0` | Preferred final quality |
| Turnover | `>=0.05` and `<0.20` | Fraction, not percent text |
| Margin | `>=10` and `<=40` bps | Normalize explicitly |
| Operator count | `<=7` | Implements "below 8" |

The current platform check response is authoritative for submission limits. Do not rewrite a live platform `limit` with this table.

Use PC bands:

- `PC < 0.60`: preferred band; stop rewarding lower PC and optimize preservation.
- `0.60 <= PC < 0.65`: acceptable band; repair quality from this low-PC pivot.
- `PC >= 0.65`: repair state; continue to another diagnosed family.
- PC missing: `PENDING`; retry after bounded backoff while continuing other useful work.
- Elapsed time alone never converts missing PC into rejection or a completed conclusion.

## Units

- Store `turnover` as a fraction: 5% is `0.05`.
- Store `margin_bps` in basis points/permyriad units: `10` means 10 bps (`0.001` as a decimal return).
- Keep the raw platform value and its display unit alongside normalized values.
- Reject comparisons when the unit cannot be determined.

## Recent-Three-Year Trend

Use the last three `complete=true` calendar years located by the actual `year` value. Never use fixed array positions and do not include a partial current year.

Default `robust_upward` rule for each of Sharpe, Fitness, and Margin:

1. all three values are finite;
2. latest value exceeds the oldest by more than the metric tolerance;
3. the middle year is not below `min(oldest, latest) - tolerance`;
4. no year breaches the declared collapse floor.

Exact `y1 < y2 < y3` is a preferred bonus. It is not required by default because fitting three independent annual points encourages overfitting. Use `trend_mode=strict` only when the user explicitly insists after this tradeoff is stated.

Default tolerances are research policy, not platform rules:

```yaml
trend_tolerance:
  sharpe: 0.15
  fitness: 0.15
  margin_bps: 2.0
collapse_floor:
  sharpe: 0.0
  fitness: 0.0
  margin_bps: 0.0
```

## Investability Policy

`Investability constrained Aggregate Data` is not one scalar quality score. Inspect:

- constrained Sharpe, Fitness, Turnover, Margin, Returns, Drawdown;
- constrained/unconstrained Sharpe and Fitness ratios;
- constrained yearly/PnL curve, recent drawdown, and flat/negative segments;
- Long/Short count, maximum weight, liquidity and size buckets when available.

Default research warnings:

```yaml
min_investability_sharpe_ratio: 0.70
min_investability_fitness_ratio: 0.60
require_positive_investability_sharpe: true
require_positive_investability_fitness: true
```

Ratios are configurable research floors. If constrained data is absent, return `PENDING`, never PASS.

## Eligibility and Routing Priority

Apply gates in this order:

1. permanent syntax, dataset, operator-budget, or mixed-signal violation;
2. transient/auth/platform state;
3. PC and SC state;
4. Investability state;
5. Sharpe/Fitness, Turnover/Margin, and recent trend;
6. fresh platform checks and curve inspection.

Do not collapse these gates into one weighted score. Among fully eligible candidates, use tolerance-aware Pareto comparison.

## Overfitting Controls

- Use 4-6 candidates per default batch; batches have no fixed count limit.
- One declared mechanism per candidate.
- At most 2 parameter values for one mechanism before deciding whether the family has signal.
- Never choose a candidate only because it has the best single backtest number.
- Preserve the incumbent and low-PC pivot snapshots.
- Reject unexplained signal additions and post-hoc annual curve fitting.
- When only cosmetic window/decay changes remain, re-diagnose the economic mechanism and open a new causal family; do not declare successful completion.

A low-PC candidate with any failed quality, Robust Universe/Investability, cost, or three-year gate is a pivot, not a finalist. Route it into the next distinct causal repair family or materially distinct intervention. A single unsuccessful cadence/decay value exhausts only that intervention, not the quality-repair phase.

An exhaustion claim requires executable evidence. Any pending PC/SC/evidence item, untried family, missing family, or family with fewer than two materially distinct conclusive interventions invalidates the claim. Even a valid claim routes to `REPLAN_REQUIRED`, never to optimization success.

# Runtime and Recovery

## Execution Paths

### MCP Path

Use configured BRAIN MCP tools for live facts and simulations. Typical capabilities include `get_alpha_details`, `get_alpha_yearly_stats`, `get_submission_check`, `check_correlation`, `get_datafields`, `get_operators`, `get_platform_setting_options`, and `create_multi_simulation`.

The local MCP implementation inspected on 2026-07-19 accepts 2-8 expressions in one multi-sim. Treat this as a capability, not a permanent platform limit.

### Python/ACE Path

The supplied ACE library accepts one outer multi-sim slot with 2-10 children and falls back to single simulation for a one-item batch. Use `scripts/ace_batch_runner.py` with an explicit `ace_lib_dir`.

Use the already configured authenticated ACE session. Keep authentication material outside every job, checkpoint, log, and skill file.

## Background Behavior

Running Python in the background:

- frees the AI interaction from one long blocking tool call;
- persists logs/checkpoints if the UI disconnects;
- supports status polling and resume;
- does not reduce BRAIN server compute time or bypass quotas.

Launch:

```powershell
python scripts/background_control.py start --job C:\path\job.json --run-dir C:\path\run
python scripts/background_control.py status --run-dir C:\path\run
python scripts/background_control.py start --mode pc --job C:\path\job.json --run-dir C:\path\run
python scripts/background_control.py status --mode pc --run-dir C:\path\run
```

If the `scripts/` directory is absent because only `SKILL.md` was shared, materialize the embedded runtime files first, then run the same commands.

## One-Slot Batching

- Default: one outer slot, 4-6 children.
- MCP: cap at discovered tool limit, currently 8.
- Python/ACE: cap at 10.
- Never interpret `1x10` as ten simultaneous independent sessions.
- Do not share one mutable session across outer worker threads.

## Durable States

Use per-candidate states:

```text
PENDING -> RUNNING -> COMPLETE
                  -> RETRY_TRANSIENT
                  -> RETRY_AUTH
                  -> RETRY_CORRELATION
                  -> CORRELATION_PENDING
                  -> PERMANENT_EXPRESSION_FAIL
                  -> DATASET_VIOLATION
                  -> OPERATOR_BUDGET_FAIL
                  -> RESUMABLE_ERROR_STOP
                  -> RESUMABLE_BUDGET_STOP
```

Write checkpoints atomically after submission, batch completion, retry scheduling, and result normalization.

## Error Classification

| Error | Action | Research evidence? |
|---|---|---:|
| 429 / Retry-After / concurrent limit | bounded backoff with jitter, reduce pressure | No |
| timeout / connection / 5xx | retry; keep checkpoint | No |
| 401 | refresh session and retry | No |
| batch FAIL with unclear child | bisect batch | No |
| syntax / unknown operator / bad field / unit | reject that candidate | Yes, as evidence |
| PC >=0.65 | route to next PC family | Yes, as evidence |
| missing PC | checkpoint as pending, retry after bounded backoff, continue other work | No |

Use `Retry-After` when present. Otherwise use capped exponential backoff with jitter. Never retry forever.

## Retry and Stop Policy

Defaults:

```yaml
request_timeout_seconds: configurable
max_transient_attempts: 4
backoff_base_seconds: 10
backoff_cap_seconds: 90
max_auth_refresh: 2
outer_slots: 1
batch_size: 6
```

When a multi-sim batch fails without a clear permanent cause, split it into halves. Continue until each bad expression is isolated or the user stops the run.

At budget stop, preserve `checkpoint.json`, `results.json`, `runner.log`, and the next due retry time. Resume only pending/retry states; never re-run completed candidates.

Batch runner completion is reported as `BATCH_COMPLETE`; optimization completion is represented separately by `workflow_status.json`. It contains `status`, `optimization_complete`, `stop_allowed`, `routes`, and `next_required_action`; the default is `stop_allowed=false`.

Set `final_revalidation: {status: PASS, evidence: ["checks.json", "raw_yearly.json"]}` only after fresh platform checks and curve review. This is the sole normal path that changes `stop_allowed` to true.

Do not apply a cumulative PC-stage cap or wait-loop. Use one immediate query plus at most one quick transient retry per invocation, preserve unresolved candidates as `CORRELATION_PENDING`, continue other work, and revisit them after new simulations or platform recovery. Only numeric PC may pass correlation gates.

Use `scripts/pc_query_runner.py` for this stage. It writes `pc_checkpoint.json`, `pc_results.json`, and per-attempt payloads under `pc_raw/`. It performs direct bounded HTTP requests because the supplied ACE `get_prod_corr()` helper loops on `Retry-After` without a wall-clock cap.

PC and SC are separate facts. A numeric PC with missing SC is `CORRELATION_PENDING`, not a correlation pass. Policy inputs contain normalized decision facts plus raw-file pointers; they must not embed `raw_simulation_result`.

# Schemas

## Mandatory Mechanism Card

Create this artifact from live Alpha facts before generating candidates. Every evidence pointer must identify a fetched object or saved raw response, not a guess.

```json
{
  "card_id": "YH-base-20260719",
  "alpha_id": "base-alpha-id",
  "expression": "rank(model123_score)",
  "strategy_summary": "Ranks firms by a model score expected to forecast relative returns.",
  "main_dataset_id": "model123",
  "information_source": "The model score aggregates a documented forecast signal.",
  "expected_direction": "Higher score should lead to higher subsequent cross-sectional return.",
  "horizon": "20-60 trading days",
  "update_frequency": "daily",
  "cross_section": "Compare securities within the configured equity universe.",
  "risk_exposures": ["industry", "size", "liquidity"],
  "trading_mechanism": "Rebalance when the score ordering changes; decay controls implementation speed.",
  "likely_failure_regime": "Model revisions become stale or cluster by industry.",
  "falsification_condition": "The signal has no stable forward ordering after matching its update horizon and neutralizing the diagnosed exposure.",
  "field_metadata": [
    {"field": "model123_score", "dataset_id": "model123", "description": "...", "type": "MATRIX", "unit": "...", "coverage": 0.91, "update_frequency": "daily"}
  ],
  "settings_evidence": {"region": "USA", "universe": "TOP3000", "delay": 1, "neutralization": "INDUSTRY"},
  "evidence_pointers": ["alpha_details.json", "field_metadata.json", "yearly_stats.json", "checks.json"],
  "analysis_completed": true
}
```

The runner hard-stops when required fields, field metadata, settings evidence, or at least four evidence pointers are missing.
