`outer_slots` must remain 1 for this skill. `batch_size` must be 1-10; values over 8 require the Python/ACE path.

## Stop Evidence

```json
{
  "reason": "RESEARCH_EXHAUSTION",
  "pending_items": [],
  "untried_repair_families": [],
  "families": {
    "information_representation": {
      "status": "EXHAUSTED",
      "interventions": [
        {"mechanism_id": "revision", "candidate_ids": ["R1-C1"], "result_status": "CONCLUSIVE_FAIL"},
        {"mechanism_id": "acceleration", "candidate_ids": ["R2-C1"], "result_status": "CONCLUSIVE_NO_IMPROVEMENT"}
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

The `families` object must contain all seven families defined by `alpha_policy.REPAIR_FAMILIES`, each with at least two distinct conclusive interventions. Run:

```powershell
python scripts/alpha_policy.py validate-stop --input stop_evidence.json
```

Research exhaustion can only produce `REPLAN_REQUIRED`, never `stop_allowed=true`.

# Stop Contract

Do not issue a final unsuccessful handoff. Research exhaustion must be written to `stop_evidence.json` and checked with:

```powershell
python scripts/alpha_policy.py validate-stop --input stop_evidence.json
```

Invalid exhaustion returns `CONTINUE_REQUIRED`; valid exhaustion returns `REPLAN_REQUIRED` with `stop_allowed=false`. Non-finalist stop is limited to explicit user stop or a documented platform/auth block after bounded recovery.

## Finalist Revalidation

Only fresh evidence may promote a finalist:

```json
{
  "final_revalidation": {
    "status": "PASS",
    "evidence": ["checks.json", "raw_yearly.json"]
  }
}
```

Review current platform checks and raw yearly curves before setting `stop_allowed=true`. A candidate with missing PC/SC, stale checks, incomplete yearly data, or unresolved Investability evidence is not a finalist.

# Distribution Layout

This skill is distributable as a single file:

```text
yh/
  SKILL.md
```

Optional local materialization after first use:

```text
yh/
  SKILL.md
  scripts/
    ace_batch_runner.py
    alpha_policy.py
    background_control.py
    pc_query_runner.py
```

Keep authentication material, raw platform responses, checkpoints, and run outputs outside the shared skill package.

# Embedded Runtime Files

Use the following blocks as the source of truth when `scripts/` is missing.

## File: scripts/alpha_policy.py

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Deterministic preflight and routing policy for one BRAIN Alpha candidate."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import math
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

DEFAULT_POLICY = {
    "pc_target": 0.65,
    "pc_preferred": 0.60,
    "sc_target": 0.60,
    "sharpe_target": 2.0,
    "fitness_target": 2.0,
    "turnover_min": 0.05,
    "turnover_max": 0.20,
    "margin_bps_min": 10.0,
    "margin_bps_max": 40.0,
    "operator_max": 7,
    "trend_mode": "robust_upward",
    "trend_tolerance": {"sharpe": 0.15, "fitness": 0.15, "margin_bps": 2.0},
    "collapse_floor": {"sharpe": 0.0, "fitness": 0.0, "margin_bps": 0.0},
    "min_investability_sharpe_ratio": 0.70,
    "min_investability_fitness_ratio": 0.60,
}

TRANSIENT_TERMS = (
    "429",
    "retry-after",
    "rate limit",
    "rate_limit",
    "timeout",
    "timed out",
    "connection",
    "temporarily unavailable",
    "concurrent_simulation_limit_exceeded",
    "http 500",
    "http 502",
    "http 503",
    "http 504",
)
AUTH_TERMS = ("401", "authentication", "unauthorized", "session expired", "token expired")
PERMANENT_TERMS = (
    "syntax",
    "parse error",
    "unknown operator",
    "unknown variable",
    "invalid field",
    "data field",
    "unit mismatch",
    "unsupported syntax",
)
REPAIR_FAMILIES = (
    "information_representation",
    "same_dataset_relation",
    "horizon",
    "cross_section",
    "tail_concentration",
    "exposure",
    "cadence",
)

def finite(value: Any) -> float | None:
    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return result if math.isfinite(result) else None

def merged_policy(override: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    policy = dict(DEFAULT_POLICY)
    for key, value in (override or {}).items():
        if key in {"trend_tolerance", "collapse_floor"}:
            nested = dict(policy[key])
            nested.update(value or {})
            policy[key] = nested
        else:
            policy[key] = value
    return policy

def count_operators(expression: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    stripped = re.sub(r"(['\"])(?:\.|(?!\1).)*\1", "", expression or "")
    functions = re.findall(r"\b([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*\(", stripped)
    symbols = re.findall(r"==|!=|<=|>=|&&|\|\||[+\-*/<>]", stripped)
    return {
        "function_call_count": len(functions),
        "function_calls": functions,
        "symbolic_operator_count": len(symbols),
        "symbolic_operators": symbols,
        "conservative_total": len(functions) + len(symbols),
        "note": "Platform operatorCount is authoritative; this local total is conservative.",
    }

def classify_error(error: Any) -> str:
    text = str(error or "").strip().lower()
    if not text:
        return "BATCH_UNKNOWN"
    if any(term in text for term in AUTH_TERMS):
        return "AUTH"
    if any(term in text for term in TRANSIENT_TERMS):
        return "TRANSIENT"
    if any(term in text for term in PERMANENT_TERMS):
        return "PERMANENT_EXPRESSION"
    return "BATCH_UNKNOWN"

def trend_result(yearly: list[dict[str, Any]], metric: str, policy: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    records = []
    for item in yearly or []:
        value = finite(item.get(metric))
        year = item.get("year")
        if item.get("complete") is True and value is not None and year is not None:
            records.append((int(year), value))
    records = sorted(records)[-3:]
    if len(records) < 3:
        return {"status": "PENDING", "years": records, "reason": "Need three complete years"}

values = [item[1] for item in records]
    tolerance = float(policy["trend_tolerance"].get(metric, 0.0))
    floor = float(policy["collapse_floor"].get(metric, -math.inf))
    exact = values[0] < values[1] < values[2]
    robust = (
        values[2] > values[0] + tolerance
        and values[1] >= min(values[0], values[2]) - tolerance
        and min(values) >= floor
    )
    passed = exact if policy.get("trend_mode") == "strict" else robust
    return {
        "status": "PASS" if passed else "FAIL",
        "years": [year for year, _ in records],
        "values": values,
        "exact_monotonic": exact,
        "robust_upward": robust,
        "tolerance": tolerance,
    }

def dataset_gate(candidate: dict[str, Any], baseline: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    dataset_ids = {str(x) for x in candidate.get("dataset_ids", []) if str(x).strip()}
    main = str(
        candidate.get("main_dataset_id")
        or baseline.get("main_dataset_id")
        or next(iter(baseline.get("dataset_ids") or []), "")
    ).strip()
    if not dataset_ids or not main:
        return {"status": "PENDING", "reason": "Dataset metadata is incomplete"}
    if candidate.get("independent_signal_blend") is True:
        return {"status": "FAIL", "reason": "Independent signal blending is forbidden"}
    if dataset_ids == {main}:
        return {"status": "PASS", "mode": "SINGLE_DATASET", "dataset_ids": sorted(dataset_ids)}

pv_ids = {str(x) for x in candidate.get("pv_dataset_ids", []) if str(x).strip()}
    extra = dataset_ids - {main}
    allowed_roles = {"liquidity", "execution", "volatility", "market_state"}
    fallback_ok = (
        candidate.get("allow_pv_fallback") is True
        and bool(extra)
        and extra.issubset(pv_ids)
        and str(candidate.get("pv_role", "")).lower() in allowed_roles
        and int(candidate.get("same_dataset_families_exhausted", 0) or 0) >= 2
    )
    if fallback_ok:
        return {"status": "PASS", "mode": "PV_FALLBACK", "dataset_ids": sorted(dataset_ids)}
    return {"status": "FAIL", "reason": f"Dataset boundary violated: {sorted(dataset_ids)}"}

def operator_gate(candidate: dict[str, Any], policy: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    official = candidate.get("official_operator_count")
    local = count_operators(str(candidate.get("expression", "")))
    count = finite(official)
    source = "platform"
    if count is None:
        count = float(local["conservative_total"])
        source = "local_conservative"
    passed = count <= float(policy["operator_max"])
    return {"status": "PASS" if passed else "FAIL", "count": int(count), "source": source, "local": local}

def scientific_gate(candidate: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    required = ("mechanism_card_id", "changed_mechanism", "hypothesis", "falsified_if", "signal_role")
    missing = [key for key in required if not str(candidate.get(key, "")).strip()]
    if candidate.get("mechanism_card_verified") is not True:
        missing.append("mechanism_card_verified")
    if candidate.get("independent_signal_blend") is not False:
        return {"status": "FAIL", "reason": "Independent signal blending is forbidden"}
    if missing:
        return {"status": "FAIL", "reason": f"Scientific metadata incomplete: {sorted(set(missing))}"}
    return {"status": "PASS"}

def investability_gate(candidate: dict[str, Any], policy: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    main = candidate.get("metrics") or {}
    constrained = candidate.get("investability")
    if not isinstance(constrained, dict):
        return {"status": "PENDING", "reason": "Investability-constrained data is missing"}
    main_sharpe = finite(main.get("sharpe"))
    main_fitness = finite(main.get("fitness"))
    inv_sharpe = finite(constrained.get("sharpe"))
    inv_fitness = finite(constrained.get("fitness"))
    if None in (main_sharpe, main_fitness, inv_sharpe, inv_fitness):
        return {"status": "PENDING", "reason": "Investability metrics are incomplete"}
    sharpe_ratio = inv_sharpe / main_sharpe if main_sharpe else None
    fitness_ratio = inv_fitness / main_fitness if main_fitness else None
    curve_status = str(constrained.get("curve_status", "PENDING")).upper()
    if curve_status in {"PENDING", "UNAVAILABLE", "UNKNOWN"}:
        return {
            "status": "PENDING",
            "sharpe_ratio": sharpe_ratio,
            "fitness_ratio": fitness_ratio,
            "curve_status": curve_status,
            "reason": "Investability curve evidence is missing",
        }
    ratios_ok = (
        sharpe_ratio is not None
        and fitness_ratio is not None
        and sharpe_ratio >= float(policy["min_investability_sharpe_ratio"])
        and fitness_ratio >= float(policy["min_investability_fitness_ratio"])
    )
    curve_ok = curve_status in {"HEALTHY", "PASS"}
    passed = inv_sharpe > 0 and inv_fitness > 0 and ratios_ok and curve_ok
    return {
        "status": "PASS" if passed else "FAIL",
        "sharpe_ratio": sharpe_ratio,
        "fitness_ratio": fitness_ratio,
        "curve_status": curve_status,
    }

def evaluate(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    policy = merged_policy(payload.get("policy"))
    baseline = payload.get("baseline") or {}
    candidate = payload.get("candidate") or {}
    metrics = candidate.get("metrics") or {}

dataset = dataset_gate(candidate, baseline)
    operators = operator_gate(candidate, policy)
    scientific = scientific_gate(candidate)
    trends = {
        metric: trend_result(candidate.get("yearly") or [], metric, policy)
        for metric in ("sharpe", "fitness", "margin_bps")
    }
    investability = investability_gate(candidate, policy)

pc = finite(metrics.get("pc"))
    sc = finite(metrics.get("sc"))
    pc_query_elapsed = finite(
        candidate.get("pc_query_stage_elapsed_minutes", candidate.get("pc_query_elapsed_minutes"))
    ) or 0.0
    sharpe = finite(metrics.get("sharpe"))
    fitness = finite(metrics.get("fitness"))
    turnover = finite(metrics.get("turnover"))
    margin = finite(metrics.get("margin_bps"))

gates = {
        "dataset": dataset,
        "operators": operators,
        "scientific": scientific,
        "pc": {"status": "PENDING" if pc is None else ("PASS" if pc < policy["pc_target"] else "FAIL"), "value": pc},
        "sc": {"status": "PENDING" if sc is None else ("PASS" if sc < policy["sc_target"] else "FAIL"), "value": sc},
        "sharpe": {"status": "PENDING" if sharpe is None else ("PASS" if sharpe > policy["sharpe_target"] else "FAIL"), "value": sharpe},
        "fitness": {"status": "PENDING" if fitness is None else ("PASS" if fitness > policy["fitness_target"] else "FAIL"), "value": fitness},
        "turnover": {"status": "PENDING" if turnover is None else ("PASS" if policy["turnover_min"] <= turnover < policy["turnover_max"] else "FAIL"), "value": turnover},
        "margin_bps": {"status": "PENDING" if margin is None else ("PASS" if policy["margin_bps_min"] <= margin <= policy["margin_bps_max"] else "FAIL"), "value": margin},
        "investability": investability,
        "trends": trends,
    }

simulation_status = str(candidate.get("simulation_status", "COMPLETE")).upper()
    error_class = classify_error(candidate.get("error")) if candidate.get("error") else None
    route = "FINALIST_REVALIDATE"
    if dataset["status"] == "PENDING":
        route = "RETRY_METADATA"
    elif dataset["status"] == "FAIL" or operators["status"] == "FAIL" or scientific["status"] == "FAIL" or candidate.get("syntax_valid") is False:
        route = "REJECT_STATIC"
    elif simulation_status in {"PENDING", "RUNNING", "SUBMITTED"}:
        route = "WAIT_SIMULATION"
    elif simulation_status not in {"COMPLETE", "WARNING"} or error_class:
        route = {
            "TRANSIENT": "RETRY_TRANSIENT",
            "AUTH": "REFRESH_AUTH",
            "PERMANENT_EXPRESSION": "REJECT_STATIC",
            "BATCH_UNKNOWN": "ISOLATE_BATCH_ERROR",
            None: "ISOLATE_BATCH_ERROR",
        }[error_class]
    elif pc is None or sc is None:
        route = "RETRY_CORRELATION"
    elif pc >= policy["pc_target"]:
        route = "PC_REPAIR_CONTINUE"
    elif sc >= policy["sc_target"]:
        route = "SC_REPAIR_CONTINUE"
    elif investability["status"] == "PENDING":
        route = "RETRY_INVESTABILITY_DATA"
    elif investability["status"] == "FAIL":
        route = "INVESTABILITY_REPAIR_FROM_LOW_PC_PIVOT"
    elif any(gates[name]["status"] != "PASS" for name in ("sharpe", "fitness", "turnover", "margin_bps")):
        route = "QUALITY_REPAIR_FROM_LOW_PC_PIVOT"
    elif any(item["status"] != "PASS" for item in trends.values()):
        route = "QUALITY_REPAIR_FROM_LOW_PC_PIVOT"
    elif candidate.get("submission_fails"):
        route = "FIX_PLATFORM_CHECKS_FROM_LOW_PC_PIVOT"

if pc is None:
        pc_band = "PENDING"
    elif pc < policy["pc_preferred"]:
        pc_band = "PREFERRED"
    elif pc < policy["pc_target"]:
        pc_band = "ACCEPTABLE"
    else:
        pc_band = "REPAIR"

return {
        "candidate_id": candidate.get("candidate_id"),
        "route": route,
        "eligible_final": route == "FINALIST_REVALIDATE",
        "pc_band": pc_band,
        "pc_query": {
            "elapsed_minutes": pc_query_elapsed,
            "attempts": candidate.get("pc_query_attempts"),
            "last_response": candidate.get("pc_last_response"),
            "terminal": False,
        },
        "error_class": error_class,
        "gates": gates,
        "policy": policy,
    }

def validate_stop_evidence(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    reason = str(payload.get("reason", "RESEARCH_EXHAUSTION")).upper()
    if reason == "USER_STOP" and payload.get("user_stop") is True:
        return {"valid": True, "stop_allowed": True, "route": "USER_STOP", "errors": []}
    if reason == "PLATFORM_BLOCKED":
        attempts = int(payload.get("recovery_attempts", 0) or 0)
        evidence = payload.get("evidence") or []
        valid = attempts >= 3 and isinstance(evidence, list) and bool(evidence)
        errors = [] if valid else ["Platform stop requires at least three recovery attempts and non-empty evidence"]
        return {"valid": valid, "stop_allowed": valid, "route": "RESUMABLE_PLATFORM_STOP" if valid else "CONTINUE_REQUIRED", "errors": errors}

errors: list[str] = []
    pending = payload.get("pending_items") or []
    if pending:
        errors.append("Pending PC/SC or evidence items prevent an exhaustion claim")
    untried = payload.get("untried_repair_families") or []
    if untried:
        errors.append(f"Untried repair families remain: {sorted(str(item) for item in untried)}")
    families = payload.get("families") or {}
    if not isinstance(families, dict):
        families = {}
        errors.append("families must be an object")
    missing = [family for family in REPAIR_FAMILIES if family not in families]
    if missing:
        errors.append(f"Missing repair-family evidence: {missing}")
    for family in REPAIR_FAMILIES:
        record = families.get(family) or {}
        interventions = record.get("interventions") or []
        if str(record.get("status", "")).upper() != "EXHAUSTED":
            errors.append(f"{family}: status must be EXHAUSTED")
        if not isinstance(interventions, list) or len(interventions) < 2:
            errors.append(f"{family}: at least two materially distinct interventions are required")
            continue
        mechanisms: set[str] = set()
        for index, intervention in enumerate(interventions):
            if not isinstance(intervention, dict):
                errors.append(f"{family}[{index}]: intervention must be an object")
                continue
            mechanism = str(intervention.get("mechanism_id") or intervention.get("intervention") or "").strip()
            candidate_ids = intervention.get("candidate_ids") or []
            result_status = str(intervention.get("result_status", "")).upper()
            if not mechanism:
                errors.append(f"{family}[{index}]: mechanism_id is required")
            else:
                mechanisms.add(mechanism)
            if not isinstance(candidate_ids, list) or not candidate_ids:
                errors.append(f"{family}[{index}]: candidate_ids are required")
            if result_status not in {"CONCLUSIVE_FAIL", "CONCLUSIVE_NO_IMPROVEMENT"}:
                errors.append(f"{family}[{index}]: result_status is not conclusive")
        if len(mechanisms) < 2:
            errors.append(f"{family}: interventions are not materially distinct")

valid = not errors
    return {
        "valid": valid,
        "stop_allowed": False,
        "route": "REPLAN_REQUIRED" if valid else "CONTINUE_REQUIRED",
        "errors": errors,
        "next_required_action": (
            "Re-diagnose the mechanism and design a new controlled family without relaxing constraints blindly."
            if valid else "Complete pending evidence and untried/distinct intervention records before claiming exhaustion."
        ),
    }

def write_json(value: Any, output: str | None) -> None:
    rendered = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    if output:
        Path(output).write_text(rendered + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        print(rendered)

def self_test() -> dict[str, Any]:
    sample = {
        "baseline": {"dataset_ids": ["model123"]},
        "candidate": {
            "candidate_id": "test",
            "mechanism_card_id": "card",
            "mechanism_card_verified": True,
            "changed_mechanism": "control",
            "hypothesis": "unchanged control",
            "falsified_if": "result differs under identical settings",
            "signal_role": "control",
            "independent_signal_blend": False,
            "expression": "rank(ts_delta(model123_score, 20))",
            "dataset_ids": ["model123"],
            "main_dataset_id": "model123",
            "official_operator_count": 2,
            "simulation_status": "COMPLETE",
            "metrics": {"pc": 0.59, "sc": 0.5, "sharpe": 2.2, "fitness": 2.3, "turnover": 0.12, "margin_bps": 22},
            "investability": {"sharpe": 1.8, "fitness": 1.7, "curve_status": "HEALTHY"},
            "yearly": [
                {"year": 2023, "complete": True, "sharpe": 1.3, "fitness": 1.2, "margin_bps": 15},
                {"year": 2024, "complete": True, "sharpe": 1.5, "fitness": 1.4, "margin_bps": 17},
                {"year": 2025, "complete": True, "sharpe": 1.8, "fitness": 1.7, "margin_bps": 20},
            ],
            "submission_fails": [],
        },
    }
    result = evaluate(sample)
    assert result["route"] == "FINALIST_REVALIDATE", result
    sample["candidate"]["metrics"]["pc"] = 0.70
    assert evaluate(sample)["route"] == "PC_REPAIR_CONTINUE"
    assert classify_error("HTTP 429 Retry-After") == "TRANSIENT"
    sample["candidate"]["metrics"]["pc"] = None
    assert evaluate(sample)["route"] == "RETRY_CORRELATION"
    sample["candidate"]["pc_query_stage_elapsed_minutes"] = 30
    assert evaluate(sample)["route"] == "RETRY_CORRELATION"
    sample["candidate"]["pc_query_stage_elapsed_minutes"] = 0
    sample["candidate"]["metrics"]["pc"] = 0.59
    sample["candidate"]["dataset_ids"] = ["model123", "analyst9"]
    assert evaluate(sample)["route"] == "REJECT_STATIC"
    sample["candidate"]["dataset_ids"] = ["model123"]
    sample["candidate"]["yearly"][1]["fitness"] = 0.5
    assert evaluate(sample)["route"] == "QUALITY_REPAIR_FROM_LOW_PC_PIVOT"
    sample["candidate"]["mechanism_card_verified"] = False
    assert evaluate(sample)["route"] == "REJECT_STATIC"
    sample["candidate"]["mechanism_card_verified"] = True
    sample["candidate"]["investability"]["curve_status"] = "PENDING"
    assert evaluate(sample)["route"] == "RETRY_INVESTABILITY_DATA"
    incomplete_stop = {"reason": "RESEARCH_EXHAUSTION", "pending_items": ["R4.pc"]}
    assert validate_stop_evidence(incomplete_stop)["route"] == "CONTINUE_REQUIRED"
    family_record = {"status": "EXHAUSTED", "interventions": [
        {"mechanism_id": "a", "candidate_ids": ["a1"], "result_status": "CONCLUSIVE_FAIL"},
        {"mechanism_id": "b", "candidate_ids": ["b1"], "result_status": "CONCLUSIVE_NO_IMPROVEMENT"},
    ]}
    complete_stop = {"reason": "RESEARCH_EXHAUSTION", "families": {family: family_record for family in REPAIR_FAMILIES}}
    exhaustion = validate_stop_evidence(complete_stop)
    assert exhaustion["valid"] is True and exhaustion["route"] == "REPLAN_REQUIRED" and exhaustion["stop_allowed"] is False
    return {"ok": True, "tests": 11}

def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    count_parser = sub.add_parser("count")
    count_parser.add_argument("--expression", required=True)
    eval_parser = sub.add_parser("evaluate")
    eval_parser.add_argument("--input", required=True)
    eval_parser.add_argument("--output")
    error_parser = sub.add_parser("classify-error")
    error_parser.add_argument("--message", required=True)
    stop_parser = sub.add_parser("validate-stop")
    stop_parser.add_argument("--input", required=True)
    stop_parser.add_argument("--output")
    sub.add_parser("self-test")
    args = parser.parse_args()

if args.command == "count":
        write_json(count_operators(args.expression), None)
    elif args.command == "evaluate":
        payload = json.loads(Path(args.input).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        write_json(evaluate(payload), args.output)
    elif args.command == "classify-error":
        write_json({"class": classify_error(args.message)}, None)
    elif args.command == "validate-stop":
        payload = json.loads(Path(args.input).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        write_json(validate_stop_evidence(payload), args.output)
    else:
        write_json(self_test(), None)
    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validate and run bounded, resumable one-slot Regular Alpha batches."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import importlib
import json
import random
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from alpha_policy import classify_error, dataset_gate, merged_policy, operator_gate

CARD_FIELDS = (
    "card_id", "alpha_id", "expression", "strategy_summary", "main_dataset_id",
    "information_source", "expected_direction", "horizon", "update_frequency",
    "cross_section", "trading_mechanism", "likely_failure_regime",
    "falsification_condition",
)
CANDIDATE_FIELDS = (
    "candidate_id", "parent_id", "mechanism_card_id", "changed_mechanism",
    "hypothesis", "falsified_if", "dataset_ids", "signal_role", "expression",
)
SIGNAL_ROLES = {
    "control", "same_mechanism_transformation", "causal_scale", "causal_condition",
    "residual_benchmark", "confirmation", "pv_execution_control",
}
TERMINAL = {"COMPLETE", "PERMANENT_EXPRESSION_FAIL"}

def now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)

def iso(value: datetime | None = None) -> str:
    return (value or now()).isoformat()

def parse_time(value: str) -> datetime:
    parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value)
    return parsed if parsed.tzinfo else parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)

def atomic_json(path: Path, value: Any) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    temp.replace(path)

def json_safe(value: Any) -> Any:
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {str(key): json_safe(item) for key, item in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
        return [json_safe(item) for item in value]
    if hasattr(value, "to_dict"):
        try:
            return json_safe(value.to_dict(orient="records"))
        except TypeError:
            return json_safe(value.to_dict())
    return str(value)

def require_text(record: dict[str, Any], fields: tuple[str, ...], label: str) -> None:
    missing = [field for field in fields if not str(record.get(field, "")).strip()]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(f"{label} missing required fields: {', '.join(missing)}")

def validate_mechanism_card(job: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    card = job.get("mechanism_card")
    if not isinstance(card, dict):
        raise ValueError("mechanism_card is required before candidate generation")
    require_text(card, CARD_FIELDS, "mechanism_card")
    if card.get("analysis_completed") is not True:
        raise ValueError("mechanism_card.analysis_completed must be true")
    if not isinstance(card.get("risk_exposures"), list) or not card["risk_exposures"]:
        raise ValueError("mechanism_card.risk_exposures must be a non-empty list")
    fields = card.get("field_metadata")
    if not isinstance(fields, list) or not fields:
        raise ValueError("mechanism_card.field_metadata must contain every signal field")
    for index, field in enumerate(fields):
        if not isinstance(field, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"field_metadata[{index}] must be an object")
        require_text(field, ("field", "dataset_id", "description"), f"field_metadata[{index}]")
    if not isinstance(card.get("settings_evidence"), dict) or not card["settings_evidence"]:
        raise ValueError("mechanism_card.settings_evidence is required")
    evidence = card.get("evidence_pointers")
    if not isinstance(evidence, list) or len({str(x).strip() for x in evidence if str(x).strip()}) < 4:
        raise ValueError("mechanism_card needs at least four distinct evidence_pointers")
    baseline = job.get("baseline") or {}
    baseline_id = str(baseline.get("alpha_id") or "").strip()
    if baseline_id and baseline_id != str(card["alpha_id"]).strip():
        raise ValueError("mechanism_card.alpha_id must match baseline.alpha_id")
    return card

def validate_job(job: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    runtime = job.get("runtime") or {}
    if bool(runtime.get("check_submission", False)) or bool(runtime.get("auto_submit", False)):
        raise ValueError("Submission paths are forbidden in yh")
    if int(runtime.get("outer_slots", 1)) != 1:
        raise ValueError("This skill requires outer_slots=1")
    batch_size = int(runtime.get("batch_size", 6))
    if not 1 <= batch_size <= 10:
        raise ValueError("batch_size must be within 1..10")
    if float(runtime.get("request_timeout_seconds", 600)) <= 0:
        raise ValueError("request_timeout_seconds must be positive")

card = validate_mechanism_card(job)
    baseline = job.get("baseline") or {}
    if not baseline.get("main_dataset_id"):
        baseline["main_dataset_id"] = card["main_dataset_id"]
    candidates = job.get("candidates") or []
    if not candidates:
        raise ValueError("At least one candidate is required")
    seen_ids: set[str] = set()
    seen_expr: set[str] = set()
    policy = merged_policy(job.get("policy"))
    for candidate in candidates:
        if not isinstance(candidate, dict):
            raise ValueError("Every candidate must be an object")
        require_text(candidate, CANDIDATE_FIELDS, "candidate")
        candidate_id = str(candidate["candidate_id"]).strip()
        if candidate_id in seen_ids:
            raise ValueError(f"Duplicate candidate_id: {candidate_id}")
        compact = "".join(str(candidate["expression"]).split())
        if compact in seen_expr:
            raise ValueError(f"Duplicate expression: {candidate_id}")
        if str(candidate["mechanism_card_id"]).strip() != str(card["card_id"]).strip():
            raise ValueError(f"{candidate_id}: mechanism_card_id does not match")
        if candidate.get("independent_signal_blend") is not False:
            raise ValueError(f"{candidate_id}: independent_signal_blend must be false")
        if str(candidate["signal_role"]).strip().lower() not in SIGNAL_ROLES:
            raise ValueError(f"{candidate_id}: unsupported signal_role")
        dataset = dataset_gate(candidate, baseline)
        if dataset["status"] != "PASS":
            raise ValueError(f"{candidate_id}: {dataset.get('reason', 'dataset metadata incomplete')}")
        operators = operator_gate(candidate, policy)
        if operators["status"] != "PASS":
            raise ValueError(f"{candidate_id}: operator budget exceeded ({operators['count']})")
        if candidate.get("syntax_valid") is False:
            raise ValueError(f"{candidate_id}: syntax_valid is false")
        seen_ids.add(candidate_id)
        seen_expr.add(compact)

def load_or_initialize(job_path: Path, job: dict[str, Any], checkpoint_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    request_timeout = float((job.get("runtime") or {}).get("request_timeout_seconds", 600))
    if checkpoint_path.exists():
        state = json.loads(checkpoint_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        state.pop("deadline_at", None)
        state["request_timeout_seconds"] = request_timeout
        for item in state.get("candidates", {}).values():
            if item.get("state") in {"RUNNING", "RESUMABLE_BUDGET_STOP", "RETRY_TRANSIENT", "RETRY_AUTH"}:
                item["state"] = "PENDING"
        state["status"] = "RUNNING"
        state["resumed_at"] = iso()
        return state
    started = now()
    return {
        "version": 2,
        "job_path": str(job_path.resolve()),
        "started_at": iso(started),
        "request_timeout_seconds": request_timeout,
        "status": "RUNNING",
        "candidates": {
            str(item["candidate_id"]): {
                **json_safe(item),
                "mechanism_card_verified": True,
                "state": "PENDING", "attempts": 0, "last_error": None,
                "alpha_id": None, "result": None,
            }
            for item in job["candidates"]
        },
    }

def remaining_seconds(state: dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    return max(1.0, float(state.get("request_timeout_seconds", 600)))

def import_ace(job: dict[str, Any]):
    ace_dir = Path(str(job.get("ace_lib_dir", ""))).expanduser().resolve()
    if not (ace_dir / "ace_lib.py").exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"ace_lib.py not found in {ace_dir}")
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ace_dir))
    return importlib.import_module("ace_lib")

def build_alpha(ace, expression: str, settings: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    allowed = {"region", "universe", "delay", "decay", "neutralization", "truncation",
               "pasteurization", "test_period", "unit_handling", "nan_handling",
               "max_trade", "visualization"}
    kwargs = {key: value for key, value in settings.items() if key in allowed}
    return ace.generate_alpha(regular=expression, alpha_type="REGULAR", **kwargs)

def result_expression(result: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    value = (result.get("simulate_data") or {}).get("regular", "")
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        value = value.get("code") or value.get("expression") or ""
    return "".join(str(value).split())

def run_batch(ace, session, batch: list[dict[str, Any]], job: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    runtime = job.get("runtime") or {}
    alpha_list = [build_alpha(ace, item["expression"], job.get("settings") or {}) for item in batch]
    config = {
        "get_pnl": bool(runtime.get("get_pnl", False)),
        "get_stats": bool(runtime.get("get_yearly_stats", True)),
        "save_pnl_file": False, "save_stats_file": False, "save_result_file": False,
        "check_submission": False, "check_self_corr": False, "check_prod_corr": False,
    }
    return ace.simulate_alpha_list_multi(
        session, alpha_list, limit_of_concurrent_simulations=1,
        limit_of_multi_simulations=max(2, len(alpha_list)), simulation_config=config,
    )

def child_batch(job_path: Path, batch_path: Path, output_path: Path) -> int:
    job = json.loads(job_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    batch = json.loads(batch_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    ace = import_ace(job)
    results = run_batch(ace, ace.start_session(), batch, job)
    atomic_json(output_path, json_safe(results or []))
    return 0

def bounded_batch(job_path: Path, batch: list[dict[str, Any]], run_dir: Path,
                  attempt_tag: str, timeout: float) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    batch_path = run_dir / "batch_raw" / f"{attempt_tag}_input.json"
    output_path = run_dir / "batch_raw" / f"{attempt_tag}_output.json"
    atomic_json(batch_path, batch)
    command = [sys.executable, str(Path(__file__).resolve()), "--child-batch",
               "--job", str(job_path), "--batch-file", str(batch_path),
               "--output-file", str(output_path)]
    try:
        completed = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=max(1.0, timeout))
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
        raise TimeoutError("ACE batch exceeded its per-request timeout") from exc
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        message = (completed.stderr or completed.stdout or "child batch failed")[-4000:]
        raise RuntimeError(message)
    return json.loads(output_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

def save_results(state: dict[str, Any], run_dir: Path) -> None:
    complete = [
        {
            "candidate_id": item.get("candidate_id"),
            "state": item.get("state"),
            "attempts": item.get("attempts"),
            "alpha_id": item.get("alpha_id"),
            "last_error": item.get("last_error"),
            "raw_result_path": f"{run_dir / 'checkpoint.json'}#/candidates/{item.get('candidate_id')}/result",
        }
        for item in state["candidates"].values()
        if item.get("state") == "COMPLETE"
    ]
    atomic_json(run_dir / "results.json", {"status": state["status"], "completed": complete})

def budget_stop(state: dict[str, Any], queue: list[list[str]], checkpoint: Path, run_dir: Path) -> int:
    state["status"] = "RESUMABLE_BUDGET_STOP"
    queued = {item for batch in queue for item in batch}
    for candidate_id, item in state["candidates"].items():
        if item.get("state") not in TERMINAL and (candidate_id in queued or item.get("state") == "RUNNING"):
            item["state"] = "RESUMABLE_BUDGET_STOP"
    atomic_json(checkpoint, state)
    save_results(state, run_dir)
    return 2

def run(job_path: Path, run_dir: Path, dry_run: bool = False) -> int:
    job = json.loads(job_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    validate_job(job)
    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    checkpoint = run_dir / "checkpoint.json"
    state = load_or_initialize(job_path, job, checkpoint)
    atomic_json(checkpoint, state)
    runtime = job.get("runtime") or {}
    batch_size = int(runtime.get("batch_size", 6))
    max_attempts = int(runtime.get("max_transient_attempts", 4))
    base_backoff = float(runtime.get("backoff_base_seconds", 10))
    cap_backoff = float(runtime.get("backoff_cap_seconds", 90))
    pending = [key for key, item in state["candidates"].items() if item.get("state") not in TERMINAL]
    queue = [pending[index:index + batch_size] for index in range(0, len(pending), batch_size)]
    if dry_run:
        state["status"] = "DRY_RUN_COMPLETE"
        state["planned_batches"] = queue
        atomic_json(checkpoint, state)
        save_results(state, run_dir)
        return 0

while queue:
        candidate_ids = queue.pop(0)
        batch = [state["candidates"][candidate_id] for candidate_id in candidate_ids]
        for item in batch:
            item["state"] = "RUNNING"
            item["attempts"] = int(item.get("attempts", 0)) + 1
        atomic_json(checkpoint, state)
        attempt_tag = "_".join(candidate_ids) + f"_a{max(item['attempts'] for item in batch)}"
        try:
            raw_results = bounded_batch(job_path, batch, run_dir, attempt_tag, remaining_seconds(state))
            normalized = [json_safe(item) for item in raw_results]
            by_expression = {result_expression(item): item for item in normalized if result_expression(item)}
            missing: list[str] = []
            for item in batch:
                result = by_expression.get("".join(item["expression"].split()))
                alpha_id = str((result or {}).get("alpha_id") or "").strip()
                if result and alpha_id:
                    item.update(state="COMPLETE", alpha_id=alpha_id, result=result, last_error=None)
                else:
                    item.update(state="PENDING", last_error="No alpha_id/result returned")
                    missing.append(item["candidate_id"])
            if len(missing) > 1:
                midpoint = len(missing) // 2
                queue[0:0] = [missing[:midpoint], missing[midpoint:]]
            elif missing:
                item = state["candidates"][missing[0]]
                if item["attempts"] < max_attempts:
                    queue.append(missing)
                else:
                    item["state"] = "RESUMABLE_ERROR_STOP"
        except TimeoutError as exc:
            for item in batch:
                item["last_error"] = str(exc)
                item["state"] = "RETRY_TRANSIENT" if item["attempts"] < max_attempts else "RESUMABLE_ERROR_STOP"
            if len(candidate_ids) > 1:
                midpoint = len(candidate_ids) // 2
                retryable = [item["candidate_id"] for item in batch if item["state"] == "RETRY_TRANSIENT"]
                if retryable:
                    midpoint = max(1, len(retryable) // 2)
                    queue[0:0] = [part for part in (retryable[:midpoint], retryable[midpoint:]) if part]
            elif batch[0]["state"] == "RETRY_TRANSIENT":
                queue.append(candidate_ids)
        except Exception as exc:
            error_class = classify_error(exc)
            for item in batch:
                item["last_error"] = str(exc)[:4000]
            if error_class in {"TRANSIENT", "AUTH"} and max(item["attempts"] for item in batch) < max_attempts:
                for item in batch:
                    item["state"] = "RETRY_AUTH" if error_class == "AUTH" else "RETRY_TRANSIENT"
                delay = min(cap_backoff, base_backoff * 2 ** (max(item["attempts"] for item in batch) - 1))
                delay *= random.uniform(0.85, 1.15)
                atomic_json(checkpoint, state)
                if delay >= remaining_seconds(state):
                    queue.insert(0, candidate_ids)
                    for item in batch:
                        item["state"] = "RESUMABLE_ERROR_STOP" if item["attempts"] >= max_attempts else "RETRY_TRANSIENT"
                    if all(item["state"] != "RETRY_TRANSIENT" for item in batch):
                        queue.pop(0)
                    continue
                time.sleep(delay)
                queue.append(candidate_ids)
            elif len(candidate_ids) > 1:
                midpoint = len(candidate_ids) // 2
                for item in batch:
                    item["state"] = "PENDING"
                queue[0:0] = [candidate_ids[:midpoint], candidate_ids[midpoint:]]
            else:
                batch[0]["state"] = "PERMANENT_EXPRESSION_FAIL" if error_class == "PERMANENT_EXPRESSION" else "RESUMABLE_ERROR_STOP"
        atomic_json(checkpoint, state)
        save_results(state, run_dir)

terminal = {item.get("state") for item in state["candidates"].values()}
    state["status"] = "BATCH_COMPLETE" if terminal.issubset(TERMINAL) else "PARTIAL_RESUMABLE"
    state["finished_at"] = iso()
    atomic_json(checkpoint, state)
    save_results(state, run_dir)
    return 0 if state["status"] == "BATCH_COMPLETE" else 3

def sample_job() -> dict[str, Any]:
    card = {
        "card_id": "card", "alpha_id": "base", "expression": "rank(model_score)",
        "strategy_summary": "rank a documented model forecast", "main_dataset_id": "model",
        "information_source": "model forecast", "expected_direction": "higher is better",
        "horizon": "20 days", "update_frequency": "daily", "cross_section": "USA equities",
        "risk_exposures": ["industry"], "trading_mechanism": "rebalance on rank changes",
        "likely_failure_regime": "stale forecasts", "falsification_condition": "no forward ordering",
        "field_metadata": [{"field": "model_score", "dataset_id": "model", "description": "forecast"}],
        "settings_evidence": {"region": "USA", "delay": 1},
        "evidence_pointers": ["details", "fields", "stats", "checks"], "analysis_completed": True,
    }
    candidate = {
        "candidate_id": "C0", "parent_id": "base", "mechanism_card_id": "card",
        "changed_mechanism": "control", "hypothesis": "unchanged control",
        "falsified_if": "result differs under identical settings", "dataset_ids": ["model"],
        "signal_role": "control", "independent_signal_blend": False,
        "official_operator_count": 1, "expression": "rank(model_score)",
    }
    return {"mechanism_card": card, "baseline": {"alpha_id": "base", "main_dataset_id": "model"},
            "runtime": {"outer_slots": 1, "request_timeout_seconds": 600}, "candidates": [candidate]}

def self_test() -> dict[str, Any]:
    job = sample_job()
    validate_job(job)
    broken = json.loads(json.dumps(job))
    broken.pop("mechanism_card")
    try:

    except ValueError:

    try:

        assert loaded["request_timeout_seconds"] == 600
    finally:

def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--job")
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--self-test", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--child-batch", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--batch-file")
    parser.add_argument("--output-file")

    if args.self_test:

        return child_batch(Path(args.job).resolve(), Path(args.batch_file).resolve(), Path(args.output_file).resolve())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Start and inspect the ACE batch runner as a detached background process."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import ctypes
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

def atomic_json(path: Path, value: Any) -> None:
    temp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    temp.replace(path)

def pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:
    if pid <= 0:
        return False
    if os.name == "nt":
        synchronize = 0x00100000
        query_limited_information = 0x00001000
        still_active = 259
        handle = ctypes.windll.kernel32.OpenProcess(
            synchronize | query_limited_information,
            False,
            pid,
        )
        if not handle:
            return False
        try:
            exit_code = ctypes.c_ulong()
            if not ctypes.windll.kernel32.GetExitCodeProcess(handle, ctypes.byref(exit_code)):
                return False
            return exit_code.value == still_active
        finally:
            ctypes.windll.kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
    except (OSError, ProcessLookupError, PermissionError, SystemError):
        return False
    return True

def start(job: Path, run_dir: Path, python: str, dry_run: bool, mode: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    process_path = run_dir / ("pc_process.json" if mode == "pc" else "process.json")
    if process_path.exists():
        previous = json.loads(process_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if pid_alive(int(previous.get("pid", 0) or 0)):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Runner is already active with pid {previous['pid']}")

runner = Path(__file__).with_name("pc_query_runner.py" if mode == "pc" else "ace_batch_runner.py")
    command = [python, str(runner), "--job", str(job.resolve()), "--run-dir", str(run_dir.resolve())]
    if dry_run:
        command.append("--dry-run")

creationflags = 0
    if os.name == "nt":
        creationflags = subprocess.CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP | subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW
    log_path = run_dir / "runner.log"
    with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as log_handle:
        process = subprocess.Popen(
            command,
            cwd=str(run_dir),
            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
            stdout=log_handle,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            creationflags=creationflags,
            close_fds=True,
        )
    metadata = {
        "pid": process.pid,
        "started_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "job": str(job.resolve()),
        "run_dir": str(run_dir.resolve()),
        "mode": mode,
        "command": command,
    }
    atomic_json(process_path, metadata)
    return metadata

def status(run_dir: Path, mode: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    process_path = run_dir / ("pc_process.json" if mode == "pc" else "process.json")
    checkpoint_path = run_dir / ("pc_checkpoint.json" if mode == "pc" else "checkpoint.json")
    result: dict[str, Any] = {"run_dir": str(run_dir.resolve())}
    if process_path.exists():
        process = json.loads(process_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        process["alive"] = pid_alive(int(process.get("pid", 0) or 0))
        result["process"] = process
    else:
        result["process"] = None
    checkpoint = json.loads(checkpoint_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if checkpoint_path.exists() else None
    if checkpoint is None:
        result["checkpoint"] = None
    else:
        candidates = checkpoint.get("candidates") or {}
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        compact = []
        for candidate_id, item in candidates.items():
            if mode == "pc" and isinstance(item.get("correlations"), dict):
                state = "/".join(f"{kind}:{fact.get('status', 'UNKNOWN')}" for kind, fact in item["correlations"].items())
            else:
                state = str(item.get("state") or item.get("status") or "UNKNOWN")
            counts[state] = counts.get(state, 0) + 1
            compact.append({
                "candidate_id": candidate_id,
                "state": state,
                "attempts": item.get("attempts"),
                "alpha_id": item.get("alpha_id"),
                "last_error": item.get("last_error"),
            })
        result["checkpoint"] = {"status": checkpoint.get("status"), "counts": counts, "candidates": compact}
        process = result.get("process") or {}
        workflow_path = run_dir / "workflow_status.json"
        if workflow_path.exists():
            result["workflow_status"] = json.loads(workflow_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        if process and process.get("alive") is False and checkpoint.get("status") in {"RUNNING", "RESUMABLE_BUDGET_STOP"}:
            result["effective_status"] = "PROCESS_EXITED_UNCLEANLY"
    return result

def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    start_parser = sub.add_parser("start")
    start_parser.add_argument("--job", required=True)
    start_parser.add_argument("--run-dir", required=True)
    start_parser.add_argument("--python", default=sys.executable)
    start_parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    start_parser.add_argument("--pretty", action="store_true")
    start_parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=("backtest", "pc"), default="backtest")
    status_parser = sub.add_parser("status")
    status_parser.add_argument("--run-dir", required=True)
    status_parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=("backtest", "pc"), default="backtest")
    status_parser.add_argument("--pretty", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()

if args.command == "start":
        value = start(
            Path(args.job).expanduser(),
            Path(args.run_dir).expanduser(),
            args.python,
            args.dry_run,
            args.mode,
        )
    else:
        value = status(Path(args.run_dir).expanduser(), args.mode)
    print(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2 if args.pretty else None,
                     separators=None if args.pretty else (",", ":")))
    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Query BRAIN PC/SC, normalize evidence, and emit compact workflow routing."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import importlib
import json
import random
import sys
import tempfile
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from alpha_policy import evaluate, validate_stop_evidence

CORRELATIONS = {"pc": "prod", "sc": "self"}
RETRY_STATES = {"PENDING", "RETRY_TRANSIENT", "RETRY_AUTH"}
DECISION_KEYS = (
    "candidate_id", "parent_id", "mechanism_card_id", "changed_mechanism",
    "hypothesis", "falsified_if", "dataset_ids", "main_dataset_id",
    "allow_pv_fallback", "pv_dataset_ids", "pv_role",
    "same_dataset_families_exhausted", "signal_role",
    "independent_signal_blend", "expression", "official_operator_count",
    "operator_count_local", "syntax_valid", "submission_fails", "error",
)

def utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)

def iso(value: datetime | None = None) -> str:
    return (value or utc_now()).isoformat()

def parse_time(value: str) -> datetime:
    result = datetime.fromisoformat(value)
    return result if result.tzinfo else result.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)

def atomic_json(path: Path, value: Any) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temp = path.with_suffix(path.suffix + ".tmp")
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, default=str) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    temp.replace(path)

def finite(value: Any) -> float | None:
    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return result if -1.0 <= result <= 1.0 and result != 0 else None

def positive_float(value: Any) -> float | None:
    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return result if result > 0 else None

def extract_correlation(payload: Any) -> float | None:
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        return None
    direct = finite(payload.get("max"))
    if direct is not None:
        return direct
    schema = payload.get("schema") or {}
    if isinstance(schema, dict):
        direct = finite(schema.get("max"))
        if direct is not None:
            return direct
    values: list[float] = []
    for row in payload.get("records") or []:
        if not isinstance(row, dict):
            continue
        for key in ("correlation", "prodCorrelation", "productionCorrelation", "selfCorrelation", "max"):
            value = finite(row.get(key))
            if value is not None:
                values.append(value)
    return max(values) if values else None

def import_ace(job: dict[str, Any]):
    ace_dir = Path(str(job.get("ace_lib_dir", ""))).expanduser().resolve()
    if not (ace_dir / "ace_lib.py").exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"ace_lib.py not found in {ace_dir}")
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ace_dir))
    return importlib.import_module("ace_lib")

def candidate_alpha_ids(job: dict[str, Any], run_dir: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    result = {
        str(item.get("candidate_id")): str(item.get("alpha_id"))
        for item in job.get("candidates") or []
        if item.get("candidate_id") and item.get("alpha_id")
    }
    checkpoint_path = run_dir / "checkpoint.json"
    if checkpoint_path.exists():
        checkpoint = json.loads(checkpoint_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        for candidate_id, item in (checkpoint.get("candidates") or {}).items():
            alpha_id = str(item.get("alpha_id") or "").strip()
            if alpha_id:
                result[str(candidate_id)] = alpha_id
    if not result:
        raise ValueError("No candidate alpha_id found in job or backtest checkpoint")
    return result

def new_fact() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "status": "PENDING", "value": None, "attempts_total": 0,
        "attempts_this_run": 0, "last_http_status": None,
        "last_response": None, "last_raw_path": None,
    }

def migrate_candidate(item: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(item.get("correlations"), dict):
        for kind in CORRELATIONS:
            item["correlations"].setdefault(kind, new_fact())
        return item
    pc = new_fact()
    old_status = str(item.get("status", "PC_PENDING"))
    pc["status"] = {
        "PC_AVAILABLE": "AVAILABLE", "PC_QUERY_ERROR": "QUERY_ERROR",
        "CORRELATION_PENDING": "CORRELATION_PENDING",
        "PC_RETRY_TRANSIENT": "RETRY_TRANSIENT", "PC_RETRY_AUTH": "RETRY_AUTH",
    }.get(old_status, "PENDING")
    pc["value"] = item.get("pc")
    pc["attempts_total"] = int(item.get("attempts", 0) or 0)
    pc["last_http_status"] = item.get("last_http_status")
    pc["last_response"] = item.get("last_response")
    pc["last_raw_path"] = item.get("last_raw_path")
    return {"candidate_id": item.get("candidate_id"), "alpha_id": item.get("alpha_id"),
            "correlations": {"pc": pc, "sc": new_fact()}}

def initialize(job: dict[str, Any], run_dir: Path, state_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if state_path.exists():
        state = json.loads(state_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        state["version"] = 2
        state["status"] = "RUNNING"
        for candidate_id, original in list((state.get("candidates") or {}).items()):
            item = migrate_candidate(original)
            state["candidates"][candidate_id] = item
            for fact in item["correlations"].values():
                fact["attempts_this_run"] = 0
                if fact.get("status") in {"CORRELATION_PENDING", "RETRY_TRANSIENT", "RETRY_AUTH"}:
                    fact["status"] = "PENDING"
        state["resumed_at"] = iso()
        return state
    return {
        "version": 2, "status": "RUNNING", "stage_started_at": iso(),
        "max_attempts_per_run": min(2, max(1, int((job.get("runtime") or {}).get("pc_max_attempts_per_run", 2)))),
        "candidates": {
            candidate_id: {"candidate_id": candidate_id, "alpha_id": alpha_id,
                           "correlations": {"pc": new_fact(), "sc": new_fact()}}
            for candidate_id, alpha_id in candidate_alpha_ids(job, run_dir).items()
        },
    }

def unresolved(state: dict[str, Any]) -> list[tuple[dict[str, Any], str, dict[str, Any]]]:
    maximum = int(state.get("max_attempts_per_run", 2))
    result = []
    for item in state["candidates"].values():
        for kind, fact in item["correlations"].items():
            if fact.get("status") in RETRY_STATES and int(fact.get("attempts_this_run", 0)) < maximum:
                result.append((item, kind, fact))
    return result

def stats_object(result: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    stats = result.get("is_stats") or {}
    if isinstance(stats, list):
        return stats[0] if stats and isinstance(stats[0], dict) else {}
    return stats if isinstance(stats, dict) else {}

def margin_bps(stats: dict[str, Any]) -> float | None:
    value = stats.get("margin_bps")
    if value is not None:
        try:
            return float(value)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return None
    try:
        return float(stats["margin"]) * 10000 if stats.get("margin") is not None else None
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None

def normalized_metrics(result: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    stats = stats_object(result)
    metrics = {key: stats.get(key) for key in ("sharpe", "fitness", "turnover", "returns", "drawdown") if stats.get(key) is not None}
    normalized_margin = margin_bps(stats)
    if normalized_margin is not None:
        metrics["margin_bps"] = normalized_margin
    return metrics

def normalized_yearly(result: dict[str, Any], supplied: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    rows = supplied if isinstance(supplied, list) and supplied else result.get("stats") or result.get("yearly") or []
    normalized = []
    for row in rows if isinstance(rows, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(row, dict) or row.get("year") is None:
            continue
        try:
            year = int(row["year"])
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            continue
        item = {key: row.get(key) for key in ("sharpe", "fitness", "turnover", "returns", "drawdown") if row.get(key) is not None}
        item.update(year=year, complete=bool(row.get("complete", year < utc_now().year)))
        value = margin_bps(row)
        if value is not None:
            item["margin_bps"] = value
        normalized.append(item)
    return sorted(normalized, key=lambda item: item["year"])[-3:]

def normalized_investability(result: dict[str, Any], supplied: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    existing = dict(supplied) if isinstance(supplied, dict) else {}
    constrained = stats_object(result).get("investabilityConstrained") or {}
    if not isinstance(constrained, dict) and not existing:
        return None
    merged = dict(existing)
    for key in ("sharpe", "fitness", "turnover", "returns", "drawdown", "longCount", "shortCount"):
        if merged.get(key) is None and isinstance(constrained, dict) and constrained.get(key) is not None:
            merged[key] = constrained[key]
    value = margin_bps(constrained if isinstance(constrained, dict) else {})
    if merged.get("margin_bps") is None and value is not None:
        merged["margin_bps"] = value
    merged.setdefault("curve_status", "PENDING")
    return merged or None

def compact_candidate(source: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {key: source[key] for key in DECISION_KEYS if key in source}

def workflow_summary(decisions: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    routes = [str(item.get("route")) for item in decisions]
    finalists = [item.get("candidate_id") for item in decisions if item.get("eligible_final")]
    repair = [route for route in routes if "REPAIR" in route or route.startswith("FIX_")]
    correlation_pending = any(route == "RETRY_CORRELATION" for route in routes)
    if finalists:
        status, action = "FINALIST_REVALIDATE", "Run fresh checks and raw-curve review for the listed candidates."
    elif repair:
        status, action = "NEEDS_NEXT_BATCH", "Create the next controlled batch from the returned repair routes."
    elif correlation_pending:
        status, action = "CORRELATION_PENDING", "Continue non-correlation analysis and retry unresolved PC/SC later."
    elif any(route.startswith("RETRY_") or route in {"REFRESH_AUTH", "WAIT_SIMULATION", "ISOLATE_BATCH_ERROR"} for route in routes):
        status, action = "NEEDS_RECOVERY", "Resume the pending evidence or platform recovery action."
    else:
        status, action = "NEEDS_RESEARCH_DECISION", "Inspect rejected candidates and untried causal families before stopping."
    return {
        "status": status, "optimization_complete": False, "stop_allowed": False,
        "finalist_candidate_ids": finalists, "routes": routes,
        "next_required_action": action,
    }

def failed_or_pending_gates(decision: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    failures: list[str] = []
    for name, gate in (decision.get("gates") or {}).items():
        if name == "trends" and isinstance(gate, dict):
            for metric, trend in gate.items():
                status = str((trend or {}).get("status", "PENDING")).upper()
                if status != "PASS":
                    failures.append(f"trend.{metric}:{status}")
        elif isinstance(gate, dict):
            status = str(gate.get("status", "PENDING")).upper()
            if status != "PASS":
                failures.append(f"{name}:{status}")
        elif gate is not True:
            failures.append(f"{name}:{'PENDING' if gate is None else 'FAIL'}")
    return failures

def write_policy_artifacts(job: dict[str, Any], run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    backtest_path = run_dir / "checkpoint.json"
    backtest = json.loads(backtest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if backtest_path.exists() else {}
    job_candidates = {str(item.get("candidate_id")): item for item in job.get("candidates") or []}
    elapsed = max(0.0, (utc_now() - parse_time(state["stage_started_at"])).total_seconds() / 60)
    inputs, decisions = [], []
    for candidate_id, corr_item in state.get("candidates", {}).items():
        source = dict(job_candidates.get(candidate_id) or {})
        backtest_item = (backtest.get("candidates") or {}).get(candidate_id) or {}
        result = backtest_item.get("result") or {}
        candidate = compact_candidate(source)
        metrics = dict(source.get("metrics") or {})
        metrics.update(normalized_metrics(result if isinstance(result, dict) else {}))
        for kind in CORRELATIONS:
            fact = corr_item["correlations"][kind]
            metrics[kind] = fact.get("value") if fact.get("status") == "AVAILABLE" else None
        candidate.update({
            "alpha_id": corr_item.get("alpha_id") or backtest_item.get("alpha_id"),
            "mechanism_card_verified": bool(backtest_item.get("mechanism_card_verified", source.get("mechanism_card_verified", False))),
            "simulation_status": "COMPLETE" if backtest_item.get("state") == "COMPLETE" else backtest_item.get("state", "PENDING"),
            "correlation_status": {kind: corr_item["correlations"][kind]["status"] for kind in CORRELATIONS},
            "pc_query_stage_elapsed_minutes": elapsed,
            "metrics": metrics,
            "yearly": normalized_yearly(result if isinstance(result, dict) else {}, source.get("yearly")),
            "investability": normalized_investability(result if isinstance(result, dict) else {}, source.get("investability")),
            "raw_simulation_result_path": f"{backtest_path}#/candidates/{candidate_id}/result",
        })
        payload = {"policy": job.get("policy") or {}, "baseline": job.get("baseline") or {}, "candidate": candidate}
        inputs.append(payload)
        decisions.append(evaluate(payload))
    summary = workflow_summary(decisions)
    if isinstance(job.get("stop_evidence"), dict) and not summary["finalist_candidate_ids"]:
        stop_decision = validate_stop_evidence(job["stop_evidence"])
        summary["stop_evidence"] = stop_decision
        summary.update(
            status=stop_decision["route"],
            optimization_complete=False,
            stop_allowed=bool(stop_decision["stop_allowed"]),
            next_required_action=stop_decision.get("next_required_action", summary["next_required_action"]),
        )
    final_revalidation = job.get("final_revalidation") or {}
    evidence = final_revalidation.get("evidence")
    if summary["status"] == "FINALIST_REVALIDATE" and str(final_revalidation.get("status", "")).upper() == "PASS" and isinstance(evidence, list) and evidence:
        summary.update(status="FINALIST_READY", optimization_complete=True, stop_allowed=True,
                       next_required_action="Stop is permitted; report the finalist and remaining risks.")
    summary.update(correlation_stage_status=state.get("status"), updated_at=iso())
    summary["context_contract"] = {
        "read_first": ["workflow_status.json", "decision_summary.json"],
        "read_on_demand": ["policy_results.json"],
        "raw_read_limit_bytes": 32768,
    }
    compact_items = []
    for payload, decision in zip(inputs, decisions):
        metrics = payload["candidate"].get("metrics") or {}
        compact_items.append({
            "candidate_id": decision.get("candidate_id"),
            "route": decision.get("route"),
            "pc_band": decision.get("pc_band"),
            "metrics": {key: metrics.get(key) for key in ("pc", "sc", "sharpe", "fitness", "turnover", "margin_bps")},
            "failed_or_pending_gates": failed_or_pending_gates(decision),
        })
    atomic_json(run_dir / "policy_inputs.json", {"status": state.get("status"), "items": inputs})
    atomic_json(run_dir / "policy_results.json", {"status": state.get("status"), "items": decisions})
    atomic_json(run_dir / "decision_summary.json", {"status": summary["status"], "next_required_action": summary["next_required_action"], "items": compact_items})
    atomic_json(run_dir / "workflow_status.json", summary)

def finalize(job: dict[str, Any], run_dir: Path, state: dict[str, Any], state_path: Path, results_path: Path) -> int:
    for item in state["candidates"].values():
        for fact in item["correlations"].values():
            if fact.get("status") in RETRY_STATES:
                fact["status"] = "CORRELATION_PENDING"
                fact["last_response"] = fact.get("last_response") or "NO_NUMERIC_RESULT_AFTER_BOUNDED_RETRIES"
    facts = [fact for item in state["candidates"].values() for fact in item["correlations"].values()]
    available = [fact for fact in facts if fact.get("status") == "AVAILABLE"]
    pending = [fact for fact in facts if fact.get("status") == "CORRELATION_PENDING"]
    errors = [fact for fact in facts if fact.get("status") == "QUERY_ERROR"]
    state["status"] = "COMPLETE" if len(available) == len(facts) else ("COMPLETE_WITH_PENDING" if available else ("COMPLETE_WITH_ERRORS" if errors and not pending else "CORRELATION_PENDING"))
    state["finished_at"] = iso()
    atomic_json(state_path, state)
    atomic_json(results_path, {"status": state["status"], "candidates": list(state["candidates"].values())})
    write_policy_artifacts(job, run_dir, state)
    return 0 if available else 4

def run(job_path: Path, run_dir: Path, dry_run: bool = False) -> int:
    job = json.loads(job_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    state_path = run_dir / "pc_checkpoint.json"
    results_path = run_dir / "pc_results.json"
    state = initialize(job, run_dir, state_path)
    atomic_json(state_path, state)
    if dry_run:
        state["status"] = "DRY_RUN_COMPLETE"
        atomic_json(state_path, state)
        return 0

ace = import_ace(job)
    session = ace.start_session()
    runtime = job.get("runtime") or {}
    interval = max(1.0, float(runtime.get("pc_poll_interval_seconds", 30)))
    request_timeout = max(10.0, min(float(runtime.get("pc_request_timeout_seconds", 30)), 60.0))
    maximum = int(state.get("max_attempts_per_run", 2))

while unresolved(state):
        for item, kind, fact in list(unresolved(state)):
            fact["attempts_this_run"] = int(fact.get("attempts_this_run", 0)) + 1
            fact["attempts_total"] = int(fact.get("attempts_total", 0)) + 1
            raw_path = run_dir / "pc_raw" / f"{item['candidate_id']}_{kind}_attempt_{fact['attempts_total']}.json"
            try:
                response = session.get(
                    f"{ace.brain_api_url}/alphas/{item['alpha_id']}/correlations/{CORRELATIONS[kind]}",
                    timeout=(request_timeout, request_timeout),
                )
                fact["last_http_status"] = response.status_code
                try:
                    payload = response.json()
                except Exception:
                    payload = {"raw_text": str(getattr(response, "text", ""))[:2000]}
                atomic_json(raw_path, payload)
                fact["last_raw_path"] = str(raw_path)
                if response.status_code == 401:
                    session = ace.check_session_and_relogin(session)
                    fact.update(status="RETRY_AUTH", last_response="HTTP_401")
                elif response.status_code == 429 or 500 <= response.status_code <= 599:
                    fact.update(status="RETRY_TRANSIENT", last_response=f"HTTP_{response.status_code}")
                elif response.status_code != 200:
                    fact.update(status="QUERY_ERROR", last_response=f"HTTP_{response.status_code}")
                else:
                    value = extract_correlation(payload)
                    if value is None:
                        fact.update(status="PENDING", last_response="EMPTY_ZERO_OR_NON_NUMERIC")
                    else:
                        fact.update(status="AVAILABLE", value=value, last_response="NUMERIC", completed_at=iso())
            except Exception as exc:
                fact.update(status="RETRY_TRANSIENT", last_response=f"EXCEPTION: {str(exc)[:500]}")
            atomic_json(state_path, state)
        if unresolved(state):
            time.sleep(interval * random.uniform(0.9, 1.1))
        for item in state["candidates"].values():
            for fact in item["correlations"].values():
                if fact.get("status") in RETRY_STATES and int(fact.get("attempts_this_run", 0)) >= maximum:
                    fact["status"] = "CORRELATION_PENDING"
    return finalize(job, run_dir, state, state_path, results_path)

def self_test() -> dict[str, Any]:
    assert extract_correlation({"max": 0.61}) == 0.61
    assert extract_correlation({"records": [{"selfCorrelation": 0.58}, {"selfCorrelation": 0.63}]}) == 0.63
    assert extract_correlation({"records": []}) is None
    result = {
        "is_stats": {"sharpe": 2.2, "fitness": 2.1, "turnover": 0.12, "margin": 0.002,
                     "investabilityConstrained": {"sharpe": 1.8, "fitness": 1.6, "margin": 0.0015}},
        "stats": [{"year": "2024", "sharpe": 1.2, "fitness": 1.1, "margin": 0.001}],
    }
    assert normalized_metrics(result)["margin_bps"] == 20.0
    assert normalized_investability(result, None)["margin_bps"] == 15.0
    assert normalized_yearly(result, None)[0]["margin_bps"] == 10.0
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
        run_dir = Path(directory)
        candidate = {
            "candidate_id": "C0", "mechanism_card_id": "card", "changed_mechanism": "control",
            "hypothesis": "control", "falsified_if": "result differs", "signal_role": "control",
            "independent_signal_blend": False, "dataset_ids": ["model"], "main_dataset_id": "model",
            "official_operator_count": 1, "expression": "rank(model_score)",
        }
        atomic_json(run_dir / "checkpoint.json", {"candidates": {"C0": {**candidate, "mechanism_card_verified": True, "state": "COMPLETE", "alpha_id": "a", "result": result}}})
        state = {"status": "RUNNING", "stage_started_at": iso(), "candidates": {"C0": {
            "candidate_id": "C0", "alpha_id": "a", "correlations": {"pc": new_fact(), "sc": new_fact()}}}}
        job = {"baseline": {"main_dataset_id": "model"}, "candidates": [candidate],
               "stop_evidence": {"reason": "RESEARCH_EXHAUSTION", "pending_items": ["C0.sc"]}}
        code = finalize(job, run_dir, state, run_dir / "pc_checkpoint.json", run_dir / "pc_results.json")
        assert code == 4 and state["status"] == "CORRELATION_PENDING"
        policy_input = json.loads((run_dir / "policy_inputs.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["items"][0]["candidate"]
        assert "raw_simulation_result" not in policy_input and policy_input["metrics"]["margin_bps"] == 20.0
        workflow = json.loads((run_dir / "workflow_status.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert workflow["stop_allowed"] is False and workflow["status"] == "CONTINUE_REQUIRED"
        decision_summary = json.loads((run_dir / "decision_summary.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        assert "raw_simulation_result" not in json.dumps(decision_summary) and decision_summary["items"][0]["candidate_id"] == "C0"
    return {"ok": True, "tests": 12}

def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--job")
    parser.add_argument("--run-dir")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--self-test", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.self_test:
        print(json.dumps(self_test(), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        return 0
    if not args.job or not args.run_dir:
        parser.error("--job and --run-dir are required unless --self-test is used")
    return run(Path(args.job).expanduser().resolve(), Path(args.run_dir).expanduser().resolve(), args.dry_run)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

This file is not required for runtime. Generate it only if the local Codex UI needs skill list metadata.
